if not game:IsLoaded() then
	game.Loaded:Wait()
end

do
	local v = print
	local v2 = warn
	local tbl = {}
	local n = 250
	local tbl2 = {}
	local n2 = 0
	local now = os.clock()

	local function fn(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
		local str = "[rymogs:" .. tostring(arg2) .. "] " .. tostring(arg3)
		tbl[#tbl + 1] = string.format("%8.2f %s %s", os.clock() - now, arg, str)

		if #tbl > n then
			table.remove(tbl, 1)
		end

		arg4 = arg4 or str
		local now2 = os.clock()
		local v3 = tbl2[arg4]
		local flag

		if v3 then
			flag = now2 - v3 < (arg5 or 1)
		else
			flag = v3
		end

		if flag then
			return
		end

		if not v3 then
			n2 += 1

			if n2 > 600 then
				tbl2 = {}
				n2 = 0
			end
		end

		tbl2[arg4] = now2

		if arg == "W" then
			if _G.RyQuiet ~= true then
				pcall(v2, str)
			end
		elseif _G.RyDebug ~= false then
			pcall(v, str)
		end
	end

	_G.RyLog = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
		fn("I", arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
	end

	_G.RyWarn = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
		fn("W", arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
	end

	local ryLogFileName = "rymogs_tp_" .. os.date("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S") .. ".txt"
	local tbl3 = {}
	local now2 = os.clock()

	local function ryLogFlush()
		if #tbl3 == 0 then
			return
		end
		local str = table.concat(tbl3, "\n") .. "\n"
		tbl3 = {}
		now2 = os.clock()

		pcall(function()
			if appendfile then
				appendfile(ryLogFileName, str)
			elseif writefile then
				writefile(ryLogFileName, (isfile and readfile and isfile(ryLogFileName) and readfile(ryLogFileName) or "") .. str)
			end
		end)
	end

	_G.RyFileLog = function(arg, arg2)
		if _G.RyLogFile == false then
			return
		end
		local n3 = #tbl3 + 1
		local v3 = tostring
		tbl3[n3] = string.format("%8.2f [%s] %s", os.clock() - now, tostring(arg), v3(arg2))

		if #tbl3 >= 25 or os.clock() - now2 > 2 then
			ryLogFlush()
		end
	end

	_G.RyLogFileName = ryLogFileName
	_G.RyLogFlush = ryLogFlush

	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			task.wait(3)
			pcall(ryLogFlush)
		end
	end)

	_G.RyDump = function()
		local str = table.concat(tbl, "\n")
		pcall(v, "===== RYMOGS LOG (" .. #tbl .. " lines) =====\n" .. str)
		local v3 = setclipboard or toclipboard

		if type(v3) == "function" then
			pcall(v3, str)
		end

		return str
	end
end

do
	local ryHubJobId = tostring(game.JobId)
	local ryHubJobId2 = _G.RyHubJobId

	if ryHubJobId2 ~= nil then
		pcall(function()
			if _G.RyInvisActive == true and type(_G.RyInvisStop) == "function" then
				_G.RyInvisStop("session")
			end
		end)

		pcall(function()
			if _G.RyAltInvisActive == true and type(_G.RyAltInvisStop) == "function" then
				_G.RyAltInvisStop()
			end
		end)
	end

	_G.RyHubSession = (tonumber(_G.RyHubSession) or 0) + 1
	_G.RyHubJobId = ryHubJobId
	_G.RyHubLoadedAt = os.clock()

	for _, v in ipairs({
		"StealTargetUID",
		"StealTarget",
		"TPSyncActive",
		"SelectedPet",
		"ActiveStealUid",
		"MyPlot",
		"ApproachFails",
		"NoApproachFor",
		"LastApproach",
		"RyBestTarget",
		"StealTgt",
		"GrappleShotToken",
		"_TPSyncGen",
		"LastFire",
		"StealFails",
		"__Plat",
		"RagdollUntil",
		"RagdollPhysLastT",
		"StealHoldDuration",
	}) do
		_G[v] = nil
	end

	for _, v in ipairs({
		"TPStop",
		"StealHold",
		"RyTPActive",
		"RyStealHold",
		"isCloning",
		"__ResetBusy",
		"RyInvisActive",
		"RyAltInvisActive",
		"InvisActive",
		"invisibleStealEnabled",
		"RecoveryInProgress",
		"ChannelsReady",
	}) do
		_G[v] = false
	end

	_G.RyLog("init", string.format("session %d, server %s%s", _G.RyHubSession, ryHubJobId:sub(1, 8), ryHubJobId2 == ryHubJobId and " (re-executed in the same server)" or ""))
end

		return _G
	end

local HttpService
HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local TweenService
TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

local Workspace
Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

do
	local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
	Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
	game:GetService("PathfindingService")
	_G.RyFpsBoost = false
	local MaterialService = game:GetService("MaterialService")
	local tbl = nil
	local tbl2 = nil
	local connection = nil
	local tbl3 = nil

	local function fn()
		if tbl then
			return
		end

		tbl = {
			GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
			FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd,
			FogStart = Lighting.FogStart,
			EnvironmentDiffuseScale = Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale,
			EnvironmentSpecularScale = Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale,
		}

		pcall(function()
			tbl.Quality = settings().Rendering.QualityLevel
			tbl.Mesh = settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel
		end)

		pcall(function()
			local terrain = Workspace.Terrain
			tbl.Decoration = terrain.Decoration
			tbl.WaterWaveSize = terrain.WaterWaveSize
			tbl.WaterWaveSpeed = terrain.WaterWaveSpeed
			tbl.WaterReflectance = terrain.WaterReflectance
		end)
	end

	local function fn2(arg)
		if arg.Name:sub(1, 2) == "Ry" then
			return true
		end
		local v
		local parent = v.Parent

		while parent and parent ~= Workspace do
			if parent.Name:sub(1, 2) == "Ry" then
				return true
			end
			parent = parent.Parent
		end

		return false
	end

	local function fn3(arg)
		local character = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
		return character and arg:IsDescendantOf(character)
	end

	local function fn4(arg)
		if fn2(arg) or fn3(arg) then
			return
		end

		return (pcall(function()
			if arg:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or arg:IsA("Trail") or arg:IsA("Beam") or arg:IsA("Fire") or arg:IsA("Smoke") or arg:IsA("Sparkles") or arg:IsA("PointLight") or arg:IsA("SpotLight") or arg:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
				if arg.Enabled then
					tbl3[#tbl3 + 1] = { o = arg, k = "Enabled", v = true }
					arg.Enabled = false
				end
			elseif arg:IsA("Decal") or arg:IsA("Texture") then
				if not (arg.Name == "face" and arg.Parent and arg.Parent.Name == "Head") then
					if arg.Transparency < 1 then
						tbl3[#tbl3 + 1] = { o = arg, k = "Transparency", v = arg.Transparency }
						arg.Transparency = 1
					end
				end
			elseif arg:IsA("BasePart") then
				if arg.CastShadow then
					tbl3[#tbl3 + 1] = { o = arg, k = "CastShadow", v = true }
					arg.CastShadow = false
				end

				if arg.Reflectance > 0 then
					tbl3[#tbl3 + 1] = { o = arg, k = "Reflectance", v = arg.Reflectance }
					arg.Reflectance = 0
				end
			end
		end))
	end

	local function fn5()
		fn()
		tbl3 = {}

		pcall(function()
			local level01 = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
			settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = level01
			local level012 = Enum.MeshPartDetailLevel.Level01
			settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel = level012
		end)

		pcall(function()
			settings().Physics.AllowSleep = true
			local skip = Enum.EnviromentalPhysicsThrottle.Skip
			settings().Physics.PhysicsEnvironmentalThrottle = skip
		end)

		pcall(function()
			Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
			Lighting.FogEnd = 9e9
			Lighting.FogStart = 9e9
			Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
			Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
		end)

		pcall(function()
			for _, child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Atmosphere") then
					tbl3[#tbl3 + 1] = { o = child, k = "Density", v = child.Density }
					child.Density = 0
				elseif child:IsA("Clouds") or child:IsA("PostEffect") then
					if child.Enabled then
						tbl3[#tbl3 + 1] = { o = child, k = "Enabled", v = true }
						child.Enabled = false
					end
				end
			end
		end)

		pcall(function()
			local terrain = Workspace.Terrain
			terrain.Decoration = false
			terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
			terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
			terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
		end)

		if _G.RyFpsMaterials ~= false then
			pcall(function()
				tbl2 = {}

				for _, v in ipairs(Enum.Material:GetEnumItems()) do
					pcall(function()
						local baseMaterialOverride = MaterialService:GetBaseMaterialOverride(v)

						if baseMaterialOverride ~= "" then
							tbl2[#tbl2 + 1] = { m = v, v = baseMaterialOverride }
							MaterialService:SetBaseMaterialOverride(v, "")
						end
					end)
				end
			end)
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			local ryFpsSwept = 0

			for _, descendant in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if not _G.RyFpsBoost then
					return
				end
				fn4(descendant)
				ryFpsSwept += 1

				if ryFpsSwept % (tonumber(_G.RyFpsBatch) or 400) == 0 then
					RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
				end
			end

			_G.RyFpsSwept = ryFpsSwept
		end)

		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
		end

		connection = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if _G.RyFpsBoost then
				fn4(descendant)
			end
		end)
	end

	local function fn6()
		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil
		end

		if tbl then
			pcall(function()
				if tbl.Quality then
					local quality = tbl.Quality
					settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = quality
				end

				if tbl.Mesh then
					local mesh = tbl.Mesh
					settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel = mesh
				end
			end)

			pcall(function()
				Lighting.GlobalShadows = tbl.GlobalShadows
				Lighting.FogEnd = tbl.FogEnd
				Lighting.FogStart = tbl.FogStart
				Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = tbl.EnvironmentDiffuseScale
				Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = tbl.EnvironmentSpecularScale
			end)

			pcall(function()
				local terrain = Workspace.Terrain

				if tbl.Decoration ~= nil then
					terrain.Decoration = tbl.Decoration
				end

				terrain.WaterWaveSize = tbl.WaterWaveSize or terrain.WaterWaveSize
				terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = tbl.WaterWaveSpeed or terrain.WaterWaveSpeed
				terrain.WaterReflectance = tbl.WaterReflectance or terrain.WaterReflectance
			end)
		end

		if tbl2 then
			for _, v in ipairs(tbl2) do
				pcall(function()
					MaterialService:SetBaseMaterialOverride(v.m, v.v)
				end)
			end

			tbl2 = nil
		end

		if tbl3 then
			for i = #tbl3, 1, -1 do
				local v = tbl3[i]

				if v.o and v.o.Parent then
					pcall(function()
						v.o[v.k] = v.v
					end)
				end
			end

			tbl3 = nil
		end
	end

	_G.RySetFpsBoost = function(arg)
		local ryFpsBoost = arg and true or false
		if ryFpsBoost == _G.RyFpsBoost == true then
			return ryFpsBoost
		end
		_G.RyFpsBoost = ryFpsBoost

		if ryFpsBoost then
			fn5()
		else
			fn6()
		end

		return ryFpsBoost
	end
end

local v, getNetRemote

do
	local packages = nil
	v = nil

	local function fn()
		if v then
			return v
		end

		if not packages then
			packages = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Packages", 5)

			if packages then
				packages = packages:WaitForChild("Net", 5)
			end
		end

		if not packages then
			return nil
		end
		local ok, result = pcall(require, packages)

		if ok and type(result) == "table" then
			v = result
		end

		return v
	end

	local getupvalues_ = debug.getupvalues or getupvalues
	local v2 = nil

	local function fn2()
		local v3 = fn()
		if not v3 or not getupvalues_ then
			return nil
		end
		local tbl = {}
		local v4 = nil
		local jobId = game.JobId
		local fn3 = nil

		fn3 = function(arg, arg2)
			if arg2 > 4 or v4 or tbl[arg] then
				return
			end
			tbl[arg] = true

			for _, v5 in pairs(arg) do
				if v4 then
					return
				end
				local kind = typeof(v5)
				if kind == "string" and #v5 == 36 and v5 ~= jobId and v5:match("^%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x$") then
					v4 = v5
					return
				end

				if kind == "table" then
					fn3(v5, arg2 + 1)
				elseif kind == "function" then
					local ok, result = pcall(getupvalues_, v5)

					if ok and type(result) == "table" then
						fn3(result, arg2 + 1)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		for _, v5 in ipairs({ "RemoteEvent", "RemoteFunction", "UnreliableRemoteEvent" }) do
			local value = rawget(v3, v5)

			if type(value) == "function" then
				local ok, result = pcall(getupvalues_, value)

				if ok and type(result) == "table" then
					fn3(result, 0)
				end
			end

			if not v4 then
				continue
			end
			break
		end

		return v4
	end

	local function fn3(arg)
		local jobId = game.JobId
		local tbl = {}
		local n = 1

		for i = 1, #arg do
			local v3 = arg:byte(i)

			if v3 == 47 then
				tbl[#tbl + 1] = "/"
			else
				tbl[#tbl + 1] = string.char((v3 - 32 + jobId:byte((n - 1) % 36 + 1) % 95) % 95 + 32)
				n += 1
			end
		end

		return table.concat(tbl)
	end

	local v3 = bit32

	if not v3 then
		v3 = debug.getupvalues(bit32 and bit32.band or print)
	end

	local bor = nil
	local bxor = nil
	local bnot = nil
	local band

	if v3 then
		band = v3.band
		bor = v3.bor
		bxor = v3.bxor
		bnot = v3.bnot
	else
		band = function(arg)
			return arg
		end
	end

	local rrotate = nil
	local rshift = nil
	local lshift = nil

	if v3 then
		rrotate = v3.rrotate
		rshift = v3.rshift
		lshift = v3.lshift
	end

	local tbl = {
		1116352408,
		1899447441,
		3049323471,
		3921009573,
		961987163,
		1508970993,
		2453635748,
		2870763221,
		3624381080,
		310598401,
		607225278,
		1426881987,
		1925078388,
		2162078206,
		2614888103,
		3248222580,
		3835390401,
		4022224774,
		264347078,
		604807628,
		770255983,
		1249150122,
		1555081692,
		1996064986,
		2554220882,
		2821834349,
		2952996808,
		3210313671,
		3336571891,
		3584528711,
		113926993,
		338241895,
		666307205,
		773529912,
		1294757372,
		1396182291,
		1695183700,
		1986661051,
		2177026350,
		2456956037,
		2730485921,
		2820302411,
		3259730800,
		3345764771,
		3516065817,
		3600352804,
		4094571909,
		275423344,
		430227734,
		506948616,
		659060556,
		883997877,
		958139571,
		1322822218,
		1537002063,
		1747873779,
		1955562222,
		2024104815,
		2227730452,
		2361852424,
		2428436474,
		2756734187,
		3204031479,
		3329325298,
	}

	local function fn4(arg)
		return band and band(arg, 4294967295) or arg
	end

	local function fn5(arg)
		local tbl2 = { 1779033703, 3144134277, 1013904242, 2773480762, 1359893119, 2600822924, 528734635, 1541459225 }
		local n = #arg
		local str = arg .. "\128"

		while #str % 64 ~= 56 do
			str ..= "\0"
		end

		local n2 = n * 8
		local tbl3 = {}

		for i = 8, 1, -1 do
			tbl3[i] = string.char(n2 % 256)
			n2 = math.floor(n2 / 256)
		end

		local str2 = str .. table.concat(tbl3)

		for i = 1, #str2, 64 do
			local tbl4 = {}

			for i2 = 0, 15 do
				local v4, v5, v6, v7 = string.byte(str2, i + i2 * 4, i + i2 * 4 + 3)
				tbl4[i2] = bor(lshift(v4, 24), lshift(v5, 16), lshift(v6, 8), v7)
			end

			for i2 = 16, 63 do
				local v4 = tbl4[i2 - 15]
				local v5 = tbl4[i2 - 2]
				local v6 = bxor
				tbl4[i2] = fn4(tbl4[i2 - 16] + bxor(rrotate(v4, 7), rrotate(v4, 18), rshift(v4, 3)) + tbl4[i2 - 7] + v6(rrotate(v5, 17), rrotate(v5, 19), rshift(v5, 10)))
			end

			local v4 = tbl2[1]
			local v5 = tbl2[2]
			local v6 = tbl2[3]
			local v7 = tbl2[4]
			local v8 = tbl2[5]
			local v9 = tbl2[6]
			local v10 = tbl2[7]
			local v11 = tbl2[8]

			for i2 = 0, 63 do
				local v12 = bxor
				local v13 = band
				local v14 = tbl[i2 + 1]
				local v15 = fn4(v11 + bxor(rrotate(v8, 6), rrotate(v8, 11), rrotate(v8, 25)) + v12(band(v8, v9), v13(bnot(v8), v10)) + v14 + tbl4[i2])
				local v16 = bxor
				local v17 = fn4(bxor(rrotate(v4, 2), rrotate(v4, 13), rrotate(v4, 22)) + v16(band(v4, v5), band(v4, v6), band(v5, v6)))
				local v18 = fn4(v7 + v15)
				v11 = v10
				v7 = v6
				v10 = v9
				v6 = v5
				v9 = v8
				v5 = v4
				v8 = v18
				v4 = fn4(v15 + v17)
			end

			tbl2[1] = fn4(tbl2[1] + v4)
			tbl2[2] = fn4(tbl2[2] + v5)
			tbl2[3] = fn4(tbl2[3] + v6)
			tbl2[4] = fn4(tbl2[4] + v7)
			tbl2[5] = fn4(tbl2[5] + v8)
			tbl2[6] = fn4(tbl2[6] + v9)
			tbl2[7] = fn4(tbl2[7] + v10)
			tbl2[8] = fn4(tbl2[8] + v11)
		end

		local tbl4 = {}

		for i = 1, 8 do
			local v4 = tbl2[i]
			local v5 = band
			tbl4[i] = string.char(band(rshift(v4, 24), 255), band(rshift(v4, 16), 255), v5(rshift(v4, 8), 255), band(v4, 255))
		end

		return table.concat(tbl4)
	end

	local tbl2 = {}

	local function fn6(arg)
		if tbl2[arg] then
			return tbl2[arg]
		end

		if not bit32 then
			return nil
		end
		local ok, result = pcall(fn5, arg)
		if not ok or type(result) ~= "string" then
			return nil
		end

		local str = result:gsub(".", function(arg2)
			return string.format("%02x", string.byte(arg2))
		end)

		tbl2[arg] = str
		return str
	end

	local tbl3 = {}

	getNetRemote = function(arg, arg2)
		local str = arg2 == "RemoteFunction" and "RemoteFunction" or arg2 == "UnreliableRemoteEvent" and "UnreliableRemoteEvent" or "RemoteEvent"
		if type(arg) ~= "string" or arg == "" then
			return nil
		end
		arg = arg:match("^R[EF]/(.+)$") or arg:match("^URE/(.+)$") or arg
		local str2 = str .. "|" .. arg
		local v4 = tbl3[str2]
		if v4 and v4.Parent then
			return v4
		end

		if not v2 then
			v2 = fn2()
		end

		if not v2 then
			return nil
		end
		local str3 = v2 .. game.JobId
		local v5 = fn6(fn3(arg) .. str3)
		if not v5 then
			return nil
		end
		local v6 = packages:FindFirstChild((str == "RemoteFunction" and "RF/" or str == "UnreliableRemoteEvent" and "URE/" or "RE/") .. v5)
		if v6 then
			tbl3[str2] = v6
			return v6
		end
		return nil
	end

	getgenv().GetNetRemote = getNetRemote

	getgenv().GetSecret = function()
		return v2
	end
end

local localPlayer
localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

for _, v2 in ipairs({
	{ "GoonPrivV6_Steal_Config.json", "rymogspriv_config.json" },
	{ "GoonPrivV6_Utility_Config.json", "rymogspriv_utility.json" },
	{ "SideTP.json", "rymogspriv_sidetp.json" },

	end)

local sharedKeybindsState, fn

do
	local function fn2(arg, arg2)
		if type(arg) == "string" and arg ~= "" then
			for _, v2 in ipairs(Enum.KeyCode:GetEnumItems()) do
				if v2.Name == arg then
					return v2
				end
			end
		end

		return arg2
	end

	local tbl2 = {
		{ "Carpet Speed", "Carpet Speed", Enum.KeyCode.Q },
		{ "Instant Clone", "Instant Clone", Enum.KeyCode.V },
		{ "Float", "Float", Enum.KeyCode.B },

		{ "Kick", "KTP", Enum.KeyCode.Y },
		{ "Auto Buy", "Auto Buy", Enum.KeyCode.F },
		{ "Teleport", "Teleport", Enum.KeyCode.T },
		{ "Menu", "Menu", Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl },
	}

	sharedKeybindsState = {}
	_G.SharedKeybindsState = sharedKeybindsState

	fn = function()
		local json = isfile and readfile and isfile("rymogspriv_utility.json")
		local keybinds = nil

		if json then
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(readfile("rymogspriv_utility.json"))
			end)

			local flag = ok and type(result) == "table" and type(result.Keybinds) == "table"
			keybinds = nil

			if flag then
				keybinds = result.Keybinds
			end
		end

		for _, v2 in ipairs(tbl2) do
			local v3 = v2[2]
			local v4 = v2[3]
			local v5

			if keybinds and keybinds[v3] then
				v5 = keybinds[v3]
			else
				local keybinds2 = tbl.Keybinds and tbl.Keybinds[v3]
				v5 = nil

				if keybinds2 then
					v5 = tbl.Keybinds[v3]
				end
			end

			sharedKeybindsState[v3] = fn2(v5, v4)
		end
	end
end

local fn3
local flag = false

fn3 = function()

do
	local tbl2 = {
		"RyPrivUI",
		"RyExtrasUI",
		"RyAdminPanel_v2",
		"RyAPNotifs",
		"RyUtilityUI",
		"RyOptimizerUI",
		"RyGriefDetector",
		"RyGriefHistory",
		"InvisStealUI_Ry",
		"RyBoostUI",
		"hudProgressLayer",
	}

	local tbl3 = { RyPrivUI = 0.55, RyUtilityUI = 0.55, InvisStealUI_Ry = 0.55 }
	local obj = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

	local function fn4(arg)
		if arg:IsA("TextButton") or arg:IsA("ImageButton") or arg:IsA("TextBox") then
			return true
		end

		if not arg:IsA("Frame") then
			return false
		end
		local parent = arg.Parent
		return parent ~= nil and not parent:IsA("ScreenGui") and not parent:IsA("LayerCollector")
	end

	local function fn5(arg)
		local absoluteSize = arg.AbsoluteSize
		return absoluteSize.Y >= 24 and absoluteSize.X >= 40
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			task.wait(2)
			local num = tonumber(_G.RyControlAlpha)
			local hui = gethui and gethui() or game:GetService("CoreGui")
			local playerGui = Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")

			for _, v2 in ipairs(tbl2) do
				local backgroundTransparency = num or tbl3[v2] or 0.12
				local v3 = hui and hui:FindFirstChild(v2) or playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild(v2)

				if v3 then
					for _, descendant in ipairs(v3:GetDescendants()) do
						if not obj[descendant] then
							pcall(function()
								if descendant:IsA("GuiObject") and fn4(descendant) and descendant.BackgroundTransparency < backgroundTransparency then
									if backgroundTransparency <= 0.2 then
										descendant.BackgroundTransparency = backgroundTransparency
										obj[descendant] = true
									elseif fn5(descendant) then
										descendant.BackgroundTransparency = backgroundTransparency
										obj[descendant] = true
									end
								else
									obj[descendant] = true
								end
							end)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

do
	local tbl2 = { lastSize = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) }
	local n = 14
	local n2 = 0.6

	local function fn4(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
		if arg2 <= 0 or arg3 <= 0 then
			return arg
		end
		local n3 = math.min((arg4.X - n) / arg2, (arg4.Y - n) / arg3)
		if n3 < arg then
			return math.max(n3, 0.35)
		end
		return arg
	end

	local function fn5(arg, arg2)
		local x = arg.AbsoluteSize.X
		local y = arg.AbsoluteSize.Y
		if x < 8 or y < 8 or x > arg2.X or y > arg2.Y then
			return
		end
		local x2 = arg.Position.X
		local y2 = arg.Position.Y
		local n3 = arg.AnchorPoint.X * x
		local n4 = arg.AnchorPoint.Y * y
		local n5 = x2.Scale * arg2.X + x2.Offset - n3
		local n6 = y2.Scale * arg2.Y + y2.Offset - n4
		local n7 = math.max(0, math.min(n5 + x, arg2.X) - math.max(n5, 0))
		local n8 = math.max(0, math.min(n6 + y, arg2.Y) - math.max(n6, 0))
		if n7 >= x * n2 and n8 >= y * n2 then
			return
		end
		local n9 = math.clamp(n5, 0, arg2.X - x)
		local n10 = math.clamp(n6, 0, arg2.Y - y)
		if math.abs(n9 - n5) < 2 and math.abs(n10 - n6) < 2 then
			return
		end

		local function fn6(arg3, arg4, arg5)
			if arg3.Scale ~= 0 and arg5 > 0 then
				return UDim.new(arg4 / arg5, 0)
			end
			return UDim.new(0, math.floor(arg4))
		end

		local y3 = arg2.Y
		arg.Position = UDim2.new(fn6(x2, n9 + n3, arg2.X), fn6(y2, n10 + n4, y3))
	end

	local function fn6(arg)
		local absoluteSize = arg.AbsoluteSize
		local v2 = tbl2.lastSize[arg]
		tbl2.lastSize[arg] = absoluteSize
		return v2 ~= nil and math.abs(v2.X - absoluteSize.X) < 1 and math.abs(v2.Y - absoluteSize.Y) < 1
	end

	_G.RyApplyUIScale = function(arg)
		local uiScale = math.clamp(tonumber(arg) or 1, 0.5, 1.5)
		tbl.UIScale = uiScale
		local hui = gethui and gethui() or game:GetService("CoreGui")
		local playerGui = Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
		local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

		for _, v2 in ipairs({
			"RyPrivUI",
			"RyExtrasUI",
			"RyAdminPanel_v2",
			"RyAPNotifs",
			"RyUtilityUI",
			"RyOptimizerUI",
			"RyGriefDetector",
			"RyGriefHistory",
			"InvisStealUI_Ry",
			"RyBoostUI",
			"hudProgressLayer",
		}) do
			local v3 = hui and hui:FindFirstChild(v2) or playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild(v2)

			if v3 then
				local absoluteSize = v3.AbsoluteSize

				if absoluteSize.X < 10 and currentCamera then
					absoluteSize = currentCamera.ViewportSize
				end

				for _, child in ipairs(v3:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("GuiObject") and not child.Name:find("^RyThemeBG") then
						local flag2 = child.Size.X.Scale >= 0.99 and child.Size.Y.Scale >= 0.99
						local scale = flag2 and 1 or fn4(uiScale, child.Size.X.Offset, child.Size.Y.Offset, absoluteSize)
						local ryUIScale = child:FindFirstChild("RyUIScale")

						if not ryUIScale then
							ryUIScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
							ryUIScale.Name = "RyUIScale"
							ryUIScale.Parent = child
						end

						if math.abs(ryUIScale.Scale - scale) > 0.0005 then
							ryUIScale.Scale = scale
						end

						if not flag2 and fn6(child) then
							fn5(child, absoluteSize)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

task.spawn(function()
	local now = tick()

	while true do
		pcall(_G.RyApplyUIScale, tbl.UIScale or 1)
		task.wait(tick() - now < 15 and 0.25 or 2)
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	local hui = gethui and gethui() or game:GetService("CoreGui")

	if hui then
		hui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child:IsA("ScreenGui") then
				task.delay(0.15, function()
					pcall(_G.RyApplyUIScale, tbl.UIScale or 1)
				end)
			end
		end)
	end

	local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	if currentCamera then
		currentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(function()
			task.wait(0.12)
			pcall(_G.RyApplyUIScale, tbl.UIScale or 1)
		end)
	end
end)

do
	local toggles = tbl.Toggles or {}
	local sliders = tbl.Sliders or {}

	_G.RyBoost = {
		on = toggles.Extras_CFrameBoost == true,
		speed = math.clamp(tonumber(sliders.RyBoost_Speed) or 400, 50, 1500),
		step = math.clamp(tonumber(sliders.RyBoost_Step) or 12, 2, 60),
		finalSkip = math.clamp(tonumber(sliders.RyBoost_FinalSkip) or 45, 0, 120),
		lagPause = math.clamp(tonumber(sliders.RyBoost_LagPause) or 0.5, 0, 3),
		autoLower = toggles.RyBoost_AutoLower ~= false,
		flatOnly = toggles.RyBoost_FlatOnly ~= false,
		flightAt = 0,
		hops = 0,
	}
end

do
	local function fn4(arg)
		arg = arg and arg:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
		if not arg then
			return
		end

		arg.Died:Connect(function()
			local ryBoost = _G.RyBoost
			if not (ryBoost and ryBoost.on and ryBoost.autoLower ~= false) then
				return
			end

			if os.clock() - (tonumber(ryBoost.flightAt) or 0) > 2.5 then
				return
			end
			local speed = ryBoost.speed
			ryBoost.speed = math.max(50, math.floor(speed * 0.85))
			tbl.Sliders = tbl.Sliders or {}
			tbl.Sliders.RyBoost_Speed = ryBoost.speed
			pcall(fn2)
			print(("[CFRAME BOOST] died right after a boosted flight -> speed %d -> %d st/s"):format(speed, ryBoost.speed))

			if _G.RyBoostRefresh then
				pcall(_G.RyBoostRefresh)
			end
		end)
	end

	local localPlayer2 = Players.LocalPlayer

	if localPlayer2.Character then
		task.spawn(fn4, localPlayer2.Character)
	end

	localPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
		task.spawn(fn4, character)
	end)
end

_G.StealWhy = "boot"
_G.StealLog = {}

_G.StealSay = function(arg)
	local stealWhy = tostring(arg)
	_G.StealWhy = stealWhy
	local stealLog = _G.StealLog
	local now = os.clock()
	local v4 = stealLog[#stealLog]

	if v4 and v4.msg == stealWhy then
		v4.n = v4.n + 1
		v4.t = now
	else
		stealLog[#stealLog + 1] = { msg = stealWhy, t = now, t0 = now, n = 1 }

		while #stealLog > 8 do
			table.remove(stealLog, 1)
		end
	end

	if _G.StealDebug then
		local n = now - (_G._mkLastSay or 0)

		if (tonumber(_G.StealDebugGap) or 0.5) <= n then
			_G._mkLastSay = now
			pcall(v3, "[steal] " .. stealWhy)
		end
	end

	if _G.StealFileLog ~= false and _G.RyFileLog then
		local flag2 = stealWhy ~= _G._sayLastFile
		local flag3

		if flag2 then
			flag3 = flag2
		else
			flag3 = now - (_G._sayLastFileAt or 0) >= (tonumber(_G.StealFileGap) or 2)
		end

		if flag3 then
			local v5 = _G
			_G._sayLastFile = stealWhy
			v5._sayLastFileAt = now
			pcall(_G.RyFileLog, "steal", stealWhy)
		end
	end
end

_G.StealDump = function()
	local stealLog = _G.StealLog or {}
	local tbl3 = {}

	for i = 1, #stealLog do
		local v4 = stealLog[i]
		tbl3[#tbl3 + 1] = string.format("%d) %s%s", i, tostring(v4.msg), v4.n and v4.n > 1 and " (x" .. v4.n .. ")" or "")
	end

	local str = #tbl3 > 0 and table.concat(tbl3, "\n") or "(nothing logged yet)"
	pcall(v3, "[steal dump]\n" .. str)

	if _G.RyFileLog then
		pcall(_G.RyFileLog, "steal", "DUMP\n" .. str)
	end

	return str
end

_G.ScanProfile = function(arg)
	if arg == "fast" then
		_G.ScanMode = "fast"
		_G.PanelScanGap = tonumber(_G.FastPanelGap) or 0.12
		_G.RepickGap = tonumber(_G.FastRepickGap) or 0.1
		_G.InRangeGap = tonumber(_G.FastInRangeGap) or 0.1
		_G.WatchdogTick = tonumber(_G.FastWatchdogTick) or 0.15
		_G.SyncScanGap = tonumber(_G.FastSyncGap) or 0.05
		_G.AutoTPPoll = tonumber(_G.FastTPPoll) or 0.03
	else
		_G.ScanMode = "normal"
		_G.PanelScanGap = tonumber(_G.NormPanelGap) or 0.6
		_G.RepickGap = tonumber(_G.NormRepickGap) or 0.5
		_G.InRangeGap = tonumber(_G.NormInRangeGap) or 0.3
		_G.WatchdogTick = tonumber(_G.NormWatchdogTick) or 0.3
		_G.SyncScanGap = tonumber(_G.NormSyncGap) or 0.25
		_G.AutoTPPoll = tonumber(_G.NormTPPoll) or 0.05
	end

	if _G.StealSay then
		_G.StealSay("scan profile -> " .. tostring(_G.ScanMode))
	end
end

do
	local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	local flag2 = false

	local function netSpoof()
		if _G.NetSpoofEnable ~= true then
			return false
		end

		return pcall(function()
			local Net = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Packages"):WaitForChild("Net"))
			local v4 = getfenv
			if type(v4) ~= "function" then
				return
			end

			for _, v5 in ipairs({ "RemoteEvent", "RemoteFunction", "UnreliableRemoteEvent" }) do
				local value = rawget(Net, v5)

				if type(value) == "function" then
					local v6 = v4(value)

					if type(v6) == "table" then
						v6.getfenv = function()
							return v4(value)
						end

						if type(v6.debug) == "table" then
							if setreadonly then
								pcall(setreadonly, v6.debug, false)
							end

							v6.debug.getmemorycategory = function()
								return "--"
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			flag2 = true
		end) and flag2
	end

	_G.NetSpoof = netSpoof

	_G.NetSpoofReady = function()
		return flag2
	end

	if _G.NetSpoofEnable == true then
		netSpoof()

		task.spawn(function()
			for i = 1, 80 do
				netSpoof()
				task.wait(0.1)
			end
		end)
	end
end

task.spawn(function()
	local Workspace2 = game:GetService("Workspace")
	local localPlayer2 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
	if not Workspace2.StreamingEnabled then
		return
	end
	local now = os.clock()
	local plots

	while true do
		plots = Workspace2:FindFirstChild("Plots")

		if not plots then
			task.wait(0.1)
		end

		if not (plots or os.clock() - now > 25) then
			continue
		end
		break
	end

	if not plots then
		return
	end

	local function fn13(arg)
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return arg:GetPivot().Position
		end)

		if ok and result and result.Magnitude > 1 then
			return result
		end

		if arg.PrimaryPart then
			return arg.PrimaryPart.Position
		end
		local basePart = arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
		return basePart and basePart.Position or nil
	end

	local n = 0

	for _, child in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
		local v4 = fn13(child)

		if v4 then
			n += 1

			task.spawn(function()
				pcall(function()
					localPlayer2:RequestStreamAroundAsync(v4)
				end)

				n -= 1
			end)
		end
	end

	local now2 = os.clock()

	while n > 0 and os.clock() - now2 < 10 do
		task.wait(0.05)
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	if _G.StreamWiden == false then
		return
	end

	if not Workspace.StreamingEnabled then
		return
	end
	local streamingMinRadius = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.StreamMinRadius) or 512, 64, 2000)
	local streamingTargetRadius = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.StreamTargetRadius) or 1024, streamingMinRadius, 4000)

	local function fn13()
		pcall(function()
			if Workspace.StreamingMinRadius < streamingMinRadius then
				Workspace.StreamingMinRadius = streamingMinRadius
			end
		end)

		pcall(function()
			if Workspace.StreamingTargetRadius < streamingTargetRadius then
				Workspace.StreamingTargetRadius = streamingTargetRadius
			end
		end)
	end

	fn13()
	local v4 = tostring
	local streamingTargetRadius2 = Workspace.StreamingTargetRadius
	_G.RyLog("stream", string.format("radius widened to min %d / target %d (was %s / %s)", streamingMinRadius, streamingTargetRadius, tostring(Workspace.StreamingMinRadius), v4(streamingTargetRadius2)))

	while _G.StreamWiden ~= false do
		task.wait(tonumber(_G.StreamWidenGap) or 5)
		fn13()
	end
end)

_streamReqAt = {}
_streamReqBusy = false

_streamAround = function(arg)
	if typeof(arg) ~= "Vector3" then
		return false
	end

	if not Workspace.StreamingEnabled then
		return false
	end

	if _G.StreamRequests == false or _streamReqBusy then
		return false
	end
	local floor = math.floor
	local n = arg.Z / 32
	local str = string.format("%d_%d_%d", math.floor(arg.X / 32), math.floor(arg.Y / 32), floor(n))
	local now = os.clock()
	if now - (_streamReqAt[str] or 0) < (tonumber(_G.StreamReqGap) or 3) then
		return false
	end
	_streamReqAt[str] = now
	_streamReqBusy = true

	task.spawn(function()
		local now2 = os.clock()
		local localPlayer2 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer

		if localPlayer2 then
			pcall(function()
				localPlayer2:RequestStreamAroundAsync(arg)
			end)
		end

		_G.StreamReqTook = os.clock() - now2
		_G.StreamReqN = (tonumber(_G.StreamReqN) or 0) + 1
		_streamReqBusy = false
	end)

	return true
end

_G.StreamAround = _streamAround

_G.PodiumDump = function()
	local tbl3 = {}

	local function fn13(arg)
		tbl3[#tbl3 + 1] = arg
	end

	local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
	local v4 = tostring
	local streamingTargetRadius = workspace.StreamingTargetRadius
	fn13(("StreamingEnabled = %s  (min %s / target %s)"):format(tostring(workspace.StreamingEnabled), tostring(workspace.StreamingMinRadius), v4(streamingTargetRadius)))

	pcall(function()
		local character = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character
		character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not (character and plots) then
			return
		end
		local huge = math.huge
		local n = 0

		for _, child in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return child:GetPivot().Position
			end)

			if ok and result then
				local magnitude = (result - character.Position).Magnitude

				if magnitude < huge then
					huge = magnitude
				end

				if magnitude > n then
					n = magnitude
				end
			end
		end

		if n > 0 then
			fn13(("plot distance from you: nearest %.0f, furthest %.0f studs"):format(huge, n))
		end
	end)

	if not plots then
		fn13("NO Workspace.Plots AT ALL")
	else
		local children = plots:GetChildren()
		fn13(("Plots: %d children"):format(#children))

		for _, child in ipairs(children) do
			local animalPodiums = child:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")

			if not animalPodiums then
				fn13(("  %s: NO AnimalPodiums"):format(child.Name:sub(1, 8)))
			else
				local children2 = animalPodiums:GetChildren()
				local tbl4 = { Base = true, Claim = true, Decorations = true, Spawn = true }
				local n = 0
				local n2 = 0

				for _, v5 in ipairs(children2) do
					local flag2 = false
					local flag3 = false

					for _, descendant in ipairs(v5:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
							flag2 = true
						end

						if descendant:IsA("Model") and not tbl4[descendant.Name] then
							flag3 = true
						end
					end

					if flag2 then
						n += 1
					end

					if flag3 then
						n2 += 1
					end
				end

				fn13(("  %s: %d podiums, %d with a prompt, %d with a BRAINROT"):format(child.Name:sub(1, 8), #children2, n, n2))

				pcall(function()
					local v5 = getPlotChannel(child.Name)
					local v6 = v5 and channelGet(v5, "AnimalList")
					local n3 = 0

					if type(v6) == "table" then
						for _, v7 in pairs(v6) do
							if type(v7) == "table" and v7.Index then
								n3 += 1
							end
						end
					end

					fn13(("    channel says %d brainrots on this plot"):format(n3))
				end)

				local v5 = children2[1]

				if v5 then
					local tbl5 = {}

					for _, child2 in ipairs(v5:GetChildren()) do
						tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = child2.ClassName .. ":" .. child2.Name
					end

					fn13(("    podium '%s' children: %s"):format(v5.Name, #tbl5 > 0 and table.concat(tbl5, ", ") or "(EMPTY)"))
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local str = table.concat(tbl3, "\n")
	pcall(v3, "[podium dump]\n" .. str)

	if _G.RyFileLog then
		pcall(_G.RyFileLog, "podium", str)
	end

	return str
end

_G.ToolWait = tonumber(_G.ToolWait) or 10

do
	local now = os.clock()

	local tbl3 = {
		"Flying Carpet",
		"Waverider",
		"Santa's Sleigh",
		"Witch's Broom",
		"Cupid's Wings",
		"Grapple Hook",
		"Grappling Hook",
		"Grapple",
		"Hook",
		"Web Slinger",
		"Grapple Gun",
		"GrappleHook",
	}

	local function fn13(arg)
		local localPlayer2 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
		if not localPlayer2 then
			return false
		end
		local character = localPlayer2.Character
		local backpack = localPlayer2:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		character = character and character:FindFirstChild(arg) or backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild(arg)
		return character ~= nil and character:IsA("Tool")
	end

	local function toolsReady()
		if type(_G.CarpetTool) == "string" and _G.CarpetTool ~= "" and fn13(_G.CarpetTool) then
			return true
		end

		for _, v4 in ipairs(tbl3) do
			if fn13(v4) then
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	_G.ToolsReady = toolsReady

	_G.HubUIWait = function(arg)
		local n = os.clock() + (tonumber(arg) or 5)
		local localPlayer2 = Players.LocalPlayer

		repeat
			local playerGui = localPlayer2:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
			local character = localPlayer2.Character
			if playerGui and character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				task.wait(0.15)
				return true
			end
			task.wait(0.1)
		until os.clock() > n

		return false
	end

	_G.BootWait = function()
		local n = tonumber(_G.BootDelay) or 0

		if n > 0 then
			while os.clock() - now < n do
				task.wait(0.1)
			end
		end

		if _G.WaitForTools == false then
			return
		end
		local n2 = tonumber(_G.ToolWait) or 10
		local now2 = os.clock()

		while os.clock() - now2 < n2 do
			local ok, result = pcall(toolsReady)
			if ok and result then
				task.wait(0.1)
				return
			end
			task.wait(0.1)
		end

		fn12("[GOON PRIVAT TP] tools never loaded within " .. n2 .. "s, continuing anyway")
	end
end

	local data = nil

			local json = readfile("rymogspriv_sidetp.json")

			if type(json) == "string" and #json > 0 then
				data = HttpService2:JSONDecode(json)
			end
		end)
	end

	pcall(function()
		local localPlayerTeleportData = TeleportService:GetLocalPlayerTeleportData()

		if localPlayerTeleportData and localPlayerTeleportData.SideTP then
			sideTP = localPlayerTeleportData.SideTP
		end

	local tbl3 = {}

	if type(sideTP) == "table" then
		for k, v4 in pairs(sideTP) do
			tbl3[k] = v4
		end
	end

	if type(data) == "table" then
		for k, v4 in pairs(data) do
			tbl3[k] = v4

				end
			end
		end

		return v9.Generation * n4
	end

GRAPPLE_ARG = 0.8

_grappleResolveUseItem = function()
	local useItem = _G.__UseItem
	if useItem and _G.__UseItemJob == game.JobId and useItem.Parent and useItem.ClassName == "RemoteEvent" then
		return useItem
	end
	_G.__UseItem = nil
	_G.__UseItemJob = game.JobId
	local packages = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages")
	packages = packages and packages:FindFirstChild("Net")
	if not packages then
		return nil
	end
	local tbl3 = {}
	local tbl4 = {}

	for i, child in ipairs(packages:GetChildren()) do
		local v4, v5 = string.match(child.Name, "^([%a_]+)/(.+)$")

		if v4 and #v5 == 64 and string.match(v5, "^%x+$") then
			tbl3[i] = child
			tbl4[v5] = tbl4[v5] or {}
			table.insert(tbl4[v5], { slot = i, class = child.ClassName })
		end
	end

	local n = 0
	local slot = nil

	for _, v4 in pairs(tbl4) do
		if #v4 > 1 then
			n += 1

			for _, v5 in ipairs(v4) do
				if v5.class == "RemoteEvent" then
					slot = v5.slot
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if n ~= 1 or not slot then
		return nil
	end
	local v4 = tbl3[slot - 1]
	if v4 and v4.ClassName == "RemoteEvent" and v4.Parent == packages then
		_G.__UseItem = v4
		return v4
	end
	return nil
end

_G.ResolveUseItem = _grappleResolveUseItem

	_G.__UseItem = nil
	v4.__UseItemJob = nil
end

end

					for i, child2 in ipairs(children) do
						if child2 == v4 then
							_G.XenUseItemIndex = i
							return
						end
					end
				end

				return
			end
		end
	end

end)

do
	local obj = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

		local v4 = obj[arg]
		local flag2

			local t = v4.t
			flag2 = os.clock() - t < 8
		else
			flag2 = v4
		end

			return v4
		end
		local tbl3 = {}

				end

				break
			end
		end)

		local tbl5 = { t = os.clock(), snd = tbl3, bm = tbl4 }
		obj[arg] = tbl5
		return tbl5
	end

	local function fn14(arg, arg2)
		if arg and arg.Parent then

					pcall(function()
						v5.Volume = 0

		end
	end

	local n = 0

			return

		end)
	end

	_grappleToolReady = function(arg, arg2)

	end

	_G.ToolReady = _grappleToolReady
	_gHookWarm = _gHookWarm or setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	_gHookGen = _gHookGen or 0

	_grappleHookWarm = function(arg)
		if not arg then
			return false

		return os.clock() - at <= (tonumber(_G.GrappleHookTTL) or 20)
	end

	_grappleHookMark = function(arg, arg2)
		if not arg then
			return
		end
		_gHookWarm[arg] = { gen = _gHookGen, at = os.clock(), ok = arg2 and true or false }
	end

	_grappleHookReady = function(arg, arg2)
		if _G.GrappleHookReady == false or not arg then
			return true
		end

		if _grappleHookWarm(arg) then
			_G.GrappleHookReadyTook = 0
			return true
		end
		local n2 = tonumber(arg2) or tonumber(_G.GrappleHookReadyMax) or 1.5
		if type(getconnections) ~= "function" then
			task.wait(math.min(0.35, n2))
			return true
		end
		local now = os.clock()

		while os.clock() - now < n2 do
			local ok, result = pcall(getconnections, arg.Activated)

			if ok and result and #result > 0 then
				_G.GrappleHookReadyTook = os.clock() - now
				_grappleHookMark(arg, true)
				return true
			end

			task.wait(0.05)
		end

		_G.GrappleHookReadyTook = -1
		return false
	end

	_G.GrappleHookReadyWait = _grappleHookReady

	_grapplePingWindow = function()
		local n2 = 0

		pcall(function()
			local value = Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()

			if type(value) == "number" then
				n2 = math.max(n2, value)
			end
		end)

		pcall(function()
			local n3 = localPlayer2:GetNetworkPing() * 2000

			if type(n3) == "number" then
				n2 = math.max(n2, n3)
			end
		end)

		return math.clamp(n2 / 1000 * (tonumber(_G.GrapplePingMult) or 1.25) + 0.05, 0.05, tonumber(_G.GrappleHoldMax) or 0.6)
	end

	_G.GrapplePingWindow = _grapplePingWindow

	_grappleDo = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
		if not arg or not arg2 then
			return false
		end

		if typeof(arg2) == "Instance" then
			arg2 = arg2.Position
		end

		if typeof(arg2) == "CFrame" then
			arg2 = arg2.Position
		end

		if typeof(arg2) ~= "Vector3" then
			_G.GrappleWhy = "bad target position"
			return false
		end
		arg4 = arg4 or localPlayer2.Character
		local humanoidRootPart = arg4 and arg4:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not humanoidRootPart then
			_G.GrappleWhy = "no hrp"
			return false
		end
		local flag2 = _G.GrappleQuiet ~= false

		if flag2 then
			fn14(arg, humanoidRootPart)
		end

		local function fn16()
			task.defer(function()
				if humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Parent then
					local flightPower = humanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("FlightPower")

					if flightPower then
						pcall(function()
							flightPower:Destroy()
						end)
					end
				end
			end)
		end

		local function fn17(arg5, grappleWhy)
			_G.GrappleWhy = grappleWhy

			if flag2 then
				fn14(arg, humanoidRootPart)
				fn15(arg, humanoidRootPart, tonumber(_G.GrappleQuietFor) or 0.6)
			end

			if arg5 then
				_G.LastGrappleAt = os.clock()
			end

			return arg5
		end

		if typeof(firesignal) ~= "function" then
			return (fn17(false, "no firesignal on this executor"))
		end
		local ok, result = pcall(require, ReplicatedStorage.Packages.PlayerMouse)
		if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" then
			return (fn17(false, "PlayerMouse unavailable"))
		end

		pcall(function()
			result.Hit = CFrame.new(arg2)
			result.Target = workspace.Terrain
		end)

		pcall(function()
			local Debounce = require(ReplicatedStorage.Packages.Debounce)

			if type(Debounce) == "function" and debug and debug.getupvalues then
				for _, v4 in ipairs(debug.getupvalues(Debounce)) do
					if type(v4) == "table" then
						for k in pairs(v4) do
							if type(k) == "string" and (k:find("rapple") or k:find("Grapple")) then
								v4[k] = nil
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)

		local ok2 = pcall(firesignal, arg.Activated)

		if ok2 then
			local n2 = os.clock() + _grapplePingWindow()

			local function fn18()
				pcall(function()
					result.Hit = CFrame.new(arg2)
					result.Target = workspace.Terrain
				end)
			end

					if connection2 then
						connection2:Disconnect()
					end

					return

			end)

			pcall(function()
				RunService:BindToRenderStep(str, Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value + 1, function()
					if os.clock() > n2 then
						pcall(function()
							RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(str)
						end)

						return
					end

					fn18()
				end)
			end)
		end

		fn16()
		return (fn17(ok2 == true, ok2 and "ok(firesignal)" or "firesignal threw"))
	end
end

_G.GrappleDo = _grappleDo

_fireGrapple = function(arg, arg2)
	local character = localPlayer2.Character
	if not character then
		return false
	end
	local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not humanoidRootPart then
		return false
	end

	if localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") and _G.NoGrappleWhileCarrying == true then
		_G.GrappleWhy = "refused: carrying a brainrot"
		return false
	end
	local grappleHook = localPlayer2:FindFirstChild("Backpack") and localPlayer2.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") or character:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") or findGrapple()
	if not grappleHook then
		_G.GrappleWhy = "no Grapple Hook"
		return false
	end

	if grappleHook.Parent ~= character then
		local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not humanoid then
			_G.GrappleWhy = "no humanoid to equip with"
			return false
		end
		local n = tonumber(_G.GrappleEquipTries) or 3

		for i = 1, n do
			pcall(function()
				humanoid:EquipTool(grappleHook)
			end)

			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			if grappleHook.Parent ~= character then
				continue
			end
			break
		end

		if grappleHook.Parent ~= character then
			_G.GrappleWhy = "grapple would not equip"
			return false
		end
	end

	local n = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.GrappleBoostDist) or 95, 12, 99)
	local n2

	if arg2 and typeof(arg) == "Vector3" then
		n2 = arg
	else
		local n3

		if typeof(arg) == "Vector3" then
			n3 = arg - humanoidRootPart.Position
		else
			local isBasePart = typeof(arg) == "Instance" and arg:IsA("BasePart")
			n3 = nil

			if isBasePart then
				n3 = arg.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position
			end
		end

		if not n3 or n3.Magnitude <= 1 then
			n3 = humanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector
		end

		if _G.GrappleFlatAim ~= false then
			local vector = Vector3.new(n3.X, 0, n3.Z)

			if vector.Magnitude > 0.01 then
				n3 = vector
			end
		end

		if n3.Magnitude < 0.01 then
			_G.GrappleWhy = "no aim direction"
			return false
		end
		n2 = humanoidRootPart.Position + n3.Unit * n
	end

	if _G.GrappleFireMode == "remote" then
		local v4 = _grappleRemoteGet()

		if v4 then
			if pcall(function()
				v4:FireServer(n / 120, n2)
			end) then
				_G.GrappleWhy = "ok (remote)"
				_G.LastGrappleAt = os.clock()
				return true
			end
		end

		_G.GrappleFireMode = "firesignal"
		_G.GrappleRemoteRevertWhy = v4 and "remote fire failed" or "remote not found"
	end

	if not _grappleHookReady(grappleHook) then
		_G.GrappleWhy = "hook handler never connected (tool.Activated has no listeners)"
		return false
	end
	local v4 = _grappleDo(grappleHook, n2, nil, character)
	local flag2 = false

	if v4 then
		local now = os.clock()

		while true do
			if os.clock() - now < (tonumber(_G.GrapplePreArmMax) or 0.5) then
				if character:GetAttribute("SpeedAllowance") ~= nil then
					flag2 = true
					break
				else
					RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
					continue
				end
			end

			break
		end

		if _G.GrappleRestoreCarpet ~= false then
			pcall(equipCarpet)
		end
	end

	if _G.RyFileLog then
		local ryFileLog = _G.RyFileLog
		local format = string.format
		local str = tostring(v4)
		local str2 = tostring(flag2)
		local n3 = tonumber(_G.GrappleHookReadyTook) or -1
		local v5 = tostring
		local grappleWhy = _G.GrappleWhy
		ryFileLog("grapple", format("fired=%s  boost=%s  handlerWait=%.2fs  quiet=%s  why=%s", str, str2, n3, tostring(_G.GrappleQuiet ~= false), v5(grappleWhy)))
	end

	return v4
end

_G.FireGrappleBoth = _fireGrapple

fireGrapple = function(arg, arg2)
	if not localPlayer2.Character then
		return false
	end
	return _fireGrapple(arg, arg2)
end

_G.FireGrapple = fireGrapple

_G.GrappleReady = function()
	if not findGrapple() then
		return false
	end

	if _G.GrappleFireMode == "remote" then
		return true
	end
	return typeof(firesignal) == "function"
end

ensureGrappleFired = function(arg, arg2)
	local n = tonumber(arg2) or tonumber(_G.GrappleEnsureTimeout) or 2
	local now = os.clock()
	if not localPlayer2.Character then
		return false
	end

	if not findGrapple() then
		_G.GrappleWhy = "no grapple tool"
		return false
	end
	local n2 = 0

	repeat
		n2 += 1
		local flag2 = false

		if arg then
			pcall(function()
				flag2 = fireGrapple(arg) and true or false
			end)
		end

		if not flag2 then
			pcall(function()
				flag2 = fireGrapple() and true or false
			end)
		end

		if flag2 then
			_G.GrappleWhy = "ok (ensure, try " .. n2 .. ")"
			_G.LastGrappleAt = os.clock()
			return true
		end

		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	until os.clock() - now > n

	_G.GrappleWhy = "ensure failed after " .. n2 .. " tries: " .. tostring(_G.GrappleWhy)
	return false
end

_G.EnsureGrappleFired = ensureGrappleFired

grappleRetryAsync = function(arg, arg2)
	local num = tonumber(arg2)
	local n

	if num then
		n = num
	else
		n = tonumber(_G.GrappleRetryFor) or 3
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		local now = os.clock()

		while os.clock() - now < n do
			if _G.TPStop then
				return
			end
			local flag2 = false

			if arg then
				pcall(function()
					flag2 = fireGrapple(arg) and true or false
				end)
			end

			if not flag2 then
				pcall(function()
					flag2 = fireGrapple() and true or false
				end)
			end

			if flag2 then
				_G.LastGrappleAt = os.clock()
				_G.StealSay("grapple fired on retry")
				return
			end

			task.wait(0.1)
		end
	end)
end

_G.GrappleRetryAsync = grappleRetryAsync
CARPET_SPEED = 280
INBASE_SPEED = 450
SKY_CLONE_WAIT = 0.35
CARPET_NAMES = { "Flying Carpet", "Waverider", "Santa's Sleigh", "Witch's Broom", "Cupid's Wings" }

findTool = function(arg)
	local character = localPlayer2.Character
	local backpack = localPlayer2:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	return character and character:FindFirstChild(arg) or backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild(arg)
end

GRAPPLE_NAMES = { "Grapple Hook", "Grappling Hook", "Grapple", "Hook", "Web Slinger", "Grapple Gun", "GrappleHook" }

findGrapple = function()
	for _, v4 in ipairs(GRAPPLE_NAMES) do
		local v5 = findTool(v4)
		if v5 and v5:IsA("Tool") then
			return v5, v4
		end
	end

	return nil
end

task.spawn(function()
	if _G.GrapplePreHook == false then
		return
	end

	if type(getconnections) ~= "function" then
		_G.GrapplePreHookTook = -1
		return
	end

	local function fn13()
		local now = os.clock()
		local n = tonumber(_G.GrapplePreHookMax) or 25

		while os.clock() - now < n do
			if _G.GrapplePreHook == false then
				return
			end
			local v4 = findGrapple()

			if v4 then
				local ok, result = pcall(getconnections, v4.Activated)

				if ok and result and #result > 0 then
					_grappleHookMark(v4, true)
					_G.GrapplePreHookTook = os.clock() - now
					return
				end
			end

			task.wait(tonumber(_G.GrapplePreHookPoll) or 0.1)
		end

		_G.GrapplePreHookTook = -1
	end

	fn13()

	localPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		_gHookGen = _gHookGen + 1
		task.wait(tonumber(_G.GrapplePreHookRespawnGap) or 0.3)
		fn13()
	end)
end)

_lastCarpetName = nil

equipCarpet = function()
	local character = localPlayer2.Character
	if not character then
		return nil
	end
	local ryFlyingTool = _G.RyFlyingTool or _G.CarpetTool or tbl and tbl.FlyingTool

	if ryFlyingTool and type(ryFlyingTool) == "string" and ryFlyingTool ~= "" and ryFlyingTool ~= _lastPreferredCarpet then
		_lastPreferredCarpet = ryFlyingTool
		_lastCarpetName = nil
		setCarpetTool(ryFlyingTool)
	end

	if _lastCarpetName then
		local v4 = character:FindFirstChild(_lastCarpetName)
		if v4 and v4.Parent == character then
			return _lastCarpetName
		end
	end

	local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not humanoid then
		return nil
	end

	for _, v4 in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
		local v5 = findTool(v4)

		if v5 and v5:IsA("Tool") then
			if v5.Parent ~= character then
				pcall(function()
					humanoid:EquipTool(v5)
				end)
			end

			_lastCarpetName = v4
			return v4
		end
	end

	return nil
end

setCarpetTool = function(carpetTool)
	if type(carpetTool) ~= "string" or carpetTool == "" then
		return
	end
	_G.CarpetTool = carpetTool
	_G.RyFlyingTool = carpetTool
	_lastCarpetName = nil

	for i = #CARPET_NAMES, 1, -1 do
		if CARPET_NAMES[i] == carpetTool then
			table.remove(CARPET_NAMES, i)
		end
	end

	table.insert(CARPET_NAMES, 1, carpetTool)
end

_G.SetCarpetTool = setCarpetTool

if tbl and type(tbl.FlyingTool) == "string" and tbl.FlyingTool ~= "" then
	setCarpetTool(tbl.FlyingTool)
elseif type(_G.RyFlyingTool) == "string" and _G.RyFlyingTool ~= "" then
	setCarpetTool(_G.RyFlyingTool)
elseif type(_G.CarpetTool) == "string" and _G.CarpetTool ~= "" then
	setCarpetTool(_G.CarpetTool)
end

_carpetEngaging = false

carpetEngage = function(arg)
	if not arg then
		local character = localPlayer2.Character

		if character then
			for _, v4 in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
				local v5 = character:FindFirstChild(v4)
				if v5 and v5:IsA("Tool") then
					_G.TPEngage = "carpet=" .. tostring(v4)
					return v4
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if _carpetEngaging then
		local now = os.clock()

		while true do
			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			if not (not _carpetEngaging or os.clock() - now > 6) then
				continue
			end
			break
		end

		local character = localPlayer2.Character

		if character then
			for _, v4 in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
				local v5 = character:FindFirstChild(v4)
				if v5 and v5:IsA("Tool") then
					return v4
				end
			end
		end
	end

	_carpetEngaging = true
	local now = os.clock()

	while not findTool("Grapple Hook") and os.clock() - now < 5 do
		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end

	local character = localPlayer2.Character
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not character or not humanoid then
		_carpetEngaging = false
		return nil
	end

	if not character:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then
		local v4 = findTool("Grapple Hook")

		if v4 then
			pcall(function()
				humanoid:EquipTool(v4)
			end)
		end
	end

	local now2 = os.clock()

	while not (localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook")) and os.clock() - now2 < 1.5 do
		local character2 = localPlayer2.Character
		local humanoid2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local v4 = findTool("Grapple Hook")

		if v4 and humanoid2 then
			pcall(function()
				humanoid2:EquipTool(v4)
			end)
		end

		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end

	if localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then
		_fireGrapple()
		local v4 = _grapplePingWindow()
		local now3 = os.clock()

		while os.clock() - now3 < v4 do
			if not _G.TPStop then
				RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
				continue
			end
			break
		end
	else
		task.wait(0.05)
	end

	local humanoid2 = localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

	if humanoid2 then
		pcall(function()
			humanoid2:UnequipTools()
		end)
	end

	task.wait(0.05)
	local now3 = os.clock()
	local v4

	while true do
		v4 = equipCarpet()
		local character2 = localPlayer2.Character

		if not (v4 and character2 and character2:FindFirstChild(v4)) then
			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			if not (os.clock() - now3 > 0.5) then
				continue
			end
		end

		break
	end

	_G.TPEngage = "carpet=" .. tostring(v4)
	_carpetEngaging = false
	return v4
end

_G.EquipCarpet = equipCarpet

_G.CarpetEngaging = function()
	return _carpetEngaging
end

if _G.KeepCarpet == nil then
	_G.KeepCarpet = true
end

localPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
	if _G.KeepCarpet == false then
		return
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
		task.wait(tonumber(_G.CarpetRespawnDelay) or 0.6)
		if _G.KeepCarpet == false then
			return
		end

		if _carpetEngaging or localPlayer2.Character ~= character then
			return
		end

		if localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
			return
		end

		for _, v4 in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
			if character:FindFirstChild(v4) then
				return
			end
		end

		pcall(equipCarpet)
	end)
end)

_normName = function(arg)
	return tostring(arg):lower():gsub("[%s%-_'%.]", "")
end

_priCacheVer = -1
_priCache = {}

_priLookup = function()
	local priVersion = _G.PriVersion or 0

	if _priCacheVer ~= priVersion then
		table.clear(_priCache)
		local sharedPriorityItems = _G.SHARED_PRIORITY_ITEMS

		if type(sharedPriorityItems) == "table" then
			for i = #sharedPriorityItems, 1, -1 do
				_priCache[_normName(sharedPriorityItems[i])] = i
			end
		end

		_priCacheVer = priVersion
	end

	return _priCache
end

_G.PriLookup = _priLookup

getPlotChannel = function(arg)
	local v4 = nil

	pcall(function()
		v4 = _G.SyncGet(arg)
	end)

	return v4
end

channelGet = function(arg, arg2)
	if not arg then
		return nil
	end
	local value = nil

	pcall(function()
		local value2 = rawget(arg, "CacheTable")

		if type(value2) == "table" then
			value = value2[arg2]
		end
	end)

	if value ~= nil then
		return value
	end

	pcall(function()
		local value2 = rawget(arg, "Data")

		if type(value2) == "table" then
			value = value2[arg2]
		end
	end)

	if value ~= nil then
		return value
	end

	pcall(function()
		value = rawget(arg, arg2)
	end)

	return value
end

isMyPlot = function(arg)
	if not arg then
		return false
	end
	local v4 = channelGet(arg, "Owner")
	if not v4 then
		return false
	end
	local flag2 = false

	pcall(function()
		if typeof(v4) == "Instance" and v4:IsA("Player") then
			flag2 = v4.UserId == localPlayer2.UserId
		elseif type(v4) == "table" and v4.UserId then
			flag2 = v4.UserId == localPlayer2.UserId
		elseif typeof(v4) == "Instance" then
			flag2 = v4 == localPlayer2
		elseif type(v4) == "string" then
			local name = localPlayer2.Name
			local flag3 = v4:lower() == name:lower()

			if not flag3 then
				flag3 = v4:lower() == (localPlayer2.DisplayName or localPlayer2.Name):lower()
			end

			flag2 = flag3
		end
	end)

	return flag2
end

ownerInGame = function(arg)
	if not arg then
		return false
	end
	local v4 = channelGet(arg, "Owner")
	if not v4 then
		return false
	end
	local flag2 = false

	pcall(function()
		if typeof(v4) == "Instance" and v4:IsA("Player") then
			flag2 = Players:FindFirstChild(v4.Name) ~= nil
		elseif type(v4) == "number" then
			flag2 = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(v4) ~= nil
		elseif type(v4) == "table" and v4.Name then
			flag2 = Players:FindFirstChild(tostring(v4.Name)) ~= nil
		elseif typeof(v4) == "Instance" and v4.Name then
			flag2 = Players:FindFirstChild(v4.Name) ~= nil
		elseif type(v4) == "string" then
			if Players:FindFirstChild(v4) then
				flag2 = true
			else
				local str = v4:lower()

				for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					local flag3 = player.Name:lower() == str

					if not flag3 then
						flag3 = (player.DisplayName or ""):lower() == str
					end

					if flag3 then
						flag2 = true
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	return flag2
end

_petModelCache = {}
_genCache = {}

do
	local tbl3 = { K = 1000, M = 1000000, B = 1e9, T = 1e12, Q = 1e15 }
	local tbl4 = {}
	_mpsMiss = _mpsMiss or {}

	_worldMPS = function(arg, arg2)
		local str = arg.Name .. "\0" .. tostring(arg2)
		local v4 = tbl4[str]

		if v4 and v4.Parent then
			local text = v4.Text

			if type(text) == "string" and text ~= "" then
				local match, v5 = text:match("%$%s*([%d%.]+)%s*([KMBTQ]?)%s*/s")

				if match then
					local num = tonumber(match)
					if num then
						return num * (tbl3[v5] or 1)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		arg = arg and arg:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
		arg = arg and arg:FindFirstChild(tostring(arg2))
		if not arg then
			return nil
		end
		local flag2 = _mpsMiss[str]

		if flag2 then
			flag2 = os.clock() - flag2 < (tonumber(_G.WorldMPSMissTTL) or 1)
		end

		if flag2 then
			return nil
		end
		local v5 = nil

		for _, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("TextLabel") or descendant:IsA("TextButton") then
				local text = descendant.Text

				if type(text) == "string" and text ~= "" then
					local match, v6 = text:match("%$%s*([%d%.]+)%s*([KMBTQ]?)%s*/s")

					if match then
						local num = tonumber(match)

						if num then
							local n = num * (tbl3[v6] or 1)

							if not v5 or n > v5 then
								tbl4[str] = descendant
								v5 = n
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if v5 then
			_mpsMiss[str] = nil
		else
			_mpsMiss[str] = os.clock()
		end

		return v5
	end
end

_petPosMiss = _petPosMiss or {}

getPetPosition = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
	local animalPodiums = arg:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
	if not animalPodiums then
		return nil
	end
	local v4 = animalPodiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(arg2))
	if not v4 then
		return nil
	end
	local str = arg.Name .. "\0" .. tostring(arg2)
	local v5 = _petModelCache[str]

	if v5 then
		local m = v5.m

		if m and m.Parent and (m.Parent == v4 or m:IsDescendantOf(v4)) then
			local p = v5.p
			local flag2

			if p then
				local t = v5.t
				flag2 = os.clock() - t < (tonumber(_G.PetPosTTL) or 3)
			else
				flag2 = p
			end

			if flag2 then
				return v5.p
			end

			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return m:GetBoundingBox()
			end)

			if ok then
				local position = result.Position
				local now = os.clock()
				v5.p = position
				v5.t = now
				return v5.p
			end
		end

		_petModelCache[str] = nil
	end

	local flag2 = _petPosMiss[str]

	if flag2 then
		flag2 = os.clock() - flag2 < (tonumber(_G.PetPosMissTTL) or 0.3)
	end

	if flag2 then
		if arg3 then
			return nil
		end

		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return v4:GetPivot()
		end)

		if ok then
			return result.Position
		end
		return v4.Position
	end

	local function fn13(arg4)
		if arg4:IsA("Model") and arg4.Name ~= "Claim" and arg4.Name ~= "Base" and arg4.Name ~= "Decorations" then
			if arg4:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("MeshPart", true) then
				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					return arg4:GetBoundingBox()
				end)

				if ok then
					_petModelCache[str] = { m = arg4, p = result.Position, t = os.clock() }
					_petPosMiss[str] = nil
					return result.Position
				end
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	for _, child in ipairs(v4:GetChildren()) do
		local v6 = fn13(child)
		if v6 then
			return v6
		end
	end

	for _, descendant in ipairs(v4:GetDescendants()) do
		local v6 = fn13(descendant)
		if v6 then
			return v6
		end
	end

	_petPosMiss[str] = os.clock()
	if arg3 then
		return nil
	end

	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return v4:GetPivot()
	end)

	if ok then
		return result.Position
	end
	return v4.Position
end

_stealingThieves = function()
	local tbl3 = {}

	for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if player ~= localPlayer2 and player:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
			local attribute = player:GetAttribute("StealingPet")

			if attribute ~= nil then
				local character = player.Character
				character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if character then
					tbl3[#tbl3 + 1] = { name = tostring(attribute), pos = character.Position }
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return tbl3
end

_markStolenPets = function(arg)
	if _G.SkipStolenTarget ~= true then
		return
	end
	local v4 = _stealingThieves()
	if #v4 == 0 then
		return
	end
	local n = tonumber(_G.StolenMatchRange) or 150
	local n2 = n * n

	for _, v5 in ipairs(arg) do
		if v5.position then
			for _, v6 in ipairs(v4) do
				if v6.name == v5.name or v6.name == v5.index then
					local n3 = v5.position - v6.pos
					if n3.X * n3.X + n3.Y * n3.Y + n3.Z * n3.Z <= n2 then
						v5.beingStolen = true
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

_parseGen = function(arg)
	if type(arg) == "number" then
		return arg
	end

	if type(arg) ~= "string" then
		return nil
	end
	local str = arg:gsub("[%s,]", ""):lower()
	if str == "" then
		return nil
	end
	local match, v4 = str:match("^([%d%.]+)([kmbt]?)$")
	if not match then
		return nil
	end
	local num = tonumber(match)
	if not num then
		return nil
	end
	return num * (({ k = 1000, m = 1000000, b = 1e9, t = 1e12 })[v4] or 1)
end

do
	local v4 = nil
	local n = 0

	_minGenForTp = function()
		local minGenForTp = _G.MinGenForTp

		if minGenForTp ~= v4 then
			v4 = minGenForTp
			n = _parseGen(minGenForTp) or 0
		end

		return n
	end
end

_podiumOkCache = {}
_podiumOkAt = 0

_petPodiumAlive = function(arg)
	if not arg or not arg.plot or arg.slot == nil then
		return true
	end
	local now = os.clock()

	if now - _podiumOkAt > (tonumber(_G.PodiumCheckTTL) or 0.5) then
		table.clear(_podiumOkCache)
		_podiumOkAt = now
	end

	local str = tostring(arg.plot) .. "|" .. tostring(arg.slot)
	local v4 = _podiumOkCache[str]
	if v4 ~= nil then
		return v4
	end
	local flag2 = false

	pcall(function()
		local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
		plots = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(arg.plot)
		plots = plots and plots:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
		flag2 = (plots and plots:FindFirstChild(tostring(arg.slot))) ~= nil
	end)

	_podiumOkCache[str] = flag2
	return flag2
end

_petEligible = function(arg)
	if not arg or arg.conveyor then
		return false
	end

	if _G.SkipStolenTarget == true and arg.beingStolen then
		return false
	end
	local v4 = _minGenForTp()
	local flag2 = v4 > 0

	if flag2 then
		flag2 = (tonumber(arg.mps) or 0) < v4
	end

	if flag2 then
		return false
	end

	if _G.RequirePodium ~= false and not _petPodiumAlive(arg) then
		return false
	end
	return true
end

scanAllPets = function()
	local tbl3 = {}
	if not loadModules() then
		return tbl3
	end
	local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
	if not plots then
		return tbl3
	end
	local syncAll = nil

	pcall(function()
		syncAll = _G.SyncAll and _G.SyncAll()
	end)

	local tbl4 = {}
	local n = 0
	local n2 = 0
	local n3 = 0

	for _, child in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
		n += 1
		local value = syncAll and rawget(syncAll, child.Name) or getPlotChannel(child.Name)

		if not value then
			tbl4[#tbl4 + 1] = "nochan"
		else
			n2 += 1

			if isMyPlot(value) then
				_G.MyPlot = child.Name
				tbl4[#tbl4 + 1] = "MINE"
			elseif _G.MyPlot == child.Name then
				tbl4[#tbl4 + 1] = "MINE"
			elseif not _G.MyPlot and channelGet(value, "Owner") == nil then
				tbl4[#tbl4 + 1] = "noowner?"
			elseif _G.RequireOwner ~= false and not ownerInGame(value) then
				tbl4[#tbl4 + 1] = "left"
			else
				n3 += 1
				local v4 = channelGet(value, "AnimalList")

				if not v4 then
					tbl4[#tbl4 + 1] = "nolist"
				else
					local v5, v6, v7 = pairs(v4)
					local n4 = 0

					for k, v8 in v5, v6, v7 do
						if type(v8) == "table" then
							local index = v8.Index

							if index then
								local displayName = AnimalsData and AnimalsData[index]

								if not (not displayName and _G.RequireKnown == true) then
									if not _IsFusing(v8) then
										local mutation = v8.Mutation or "None"
										local str = child.Name .. "\0" .. tostring(k)
										local v9 = _genCache[str]
										local g

										if v9 and v9.n == index and v9.m == v8.Mutation and v9.t == v8.Traits then
											g = v9.g
										else
											g = 0

											pcall(function()
												g = AnimalsShared:GetGeneration(index, v8.Mutation, v8.Traits, nil)
											end)

											if g and g > 0 then
												_genCache[str] = { n = index, m = v8.Mutation, t = v8.Traits, g = g }
											end
										end

										if (not g or g <= 0) and _G.WorldMPS ~= false then
											local v10 = _worldMPS(child, k)

											if v10 and v10 > 0 then
												g = v10
											end
										end

										displayName = displayName and displayName.DisplayName or index
										local v10 = getPetPosition(child, k, _G.RequireOwner == false or _G.RequireModel == true)

										if v10 then
											if g >= (tonumber(_G.MinMPS) or 0) then
												table.insert(tbl3, {
													name = displayName,
													index = index,
													mps = g,
													mutation = mutation,
													position = v10,
													plot = child.Name,
													slot = tostring(k),
												})

												n4 += 1
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					tbl4[#tbl4 + 1] = tostring(n4)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local v4 = tostring
	local scanMode = _G.ScanMode
	_G.ScanInfo = string.format("plots %d scanned of %d  pets %d  [%s]  %s", n3, n, #tbl3, table.concat(tbl4, " "), v4(scanMode))
	_markStolenPets(tbl3)
	local v5 = _priLookup()

	for _, v6 in ipairs(tbl3) do
		v6._pri = v5[_normName(v6.name)] or v6.index and v5[_normName(v6.index)] or nil
		v6._tier = TIER_LOOKUP and (TIER_LOOKUP[v6.name] or v6.index and TIER_LOOKUP[v6.index]) or nil
	end

	if _G.StealMode == "highest" then
		table.sort(tbl3, function(arg, arg2)
			return (arg.mps or 0) > (arg2.mps or 0)
		end)

		return tbl3
	end

	table.sort(tbl3, function(arg, arg2)
		local pri = arg._pri
		local pri2 = arg2._pri

		if pri and pri2 then
			if pri ~= pri2 then
				return pri < pri2
			end
		elseif pri ~= nil ~= pri2 ~= nil then
			return pri ~= nil
		end

		local mps = arg.mps or 0
		local mps2 = arg2.mps or 0
		if mps ~= mps2 then
			return mps > mps2
		end
		local tier = arg._tier
		local tier2 = arg2._tier

		if tier and tier2 then
			if tier ~= tier2 then
				return tier < tier2
			end
		elseif tier ~= nil ~= tier2 ~= nil then
			return tier ~= nil
		end

		return false
	end)

	return tbl3
end

_G.ScanAllPets = scanAllPets

task.spawn(function()
	if _G.ScanWarmup == false then
		return
	end
	local now = os.clock()
	local n = now + (tonumber(_G.ScanWarmMax) or 20)
	local scanWarmPasses = 0

	while true do
		scanWarmPasses += 1
		local ok, result = pcall(scanAllPets)

		if ok and type(result) == "table" and #result > 0 then
			_G.ScanWarmCount = #result
			break
		else
			task.wait(tonumber(_G.ScanWarmPoll) or 0.05)
			if not (n < os.clock()) then
				continue
			end
		end

		break
	end

	_G.ScanWarmPasses = scanWarmPasses
	_G.ScanWarmMs = (os.clock() - now) * 1000
end)

_scanTPCache = nil
_scanTPAt = -99

scanForTP = function(arg)
	local flag2 = not arg and _scanTPCache

	if flag2 then
		local v4 = _scanTPAt
		flag2 = os.clock() - v4 < (tonumber(_G.ScanTPTTL) or 0.12)
	end

	if flag2 then
		local create = table.create and table.create(#_scanTPCache) or {}

		for i = 1, #_scanTPCache do
			create[i] = _scanTPCache[i]
		end

		return create
	end

	local tbl3 = scanAllPets()

	if type(tbl3) ~= "table" then
		tbl3 = {}
	end

	if #tbl3 > 0 then
		local create = table.create and table.create(#tbl3) or {}

		for i = 1, #tbl3 do
			create[i] = tbl3[i]
		end

		local now = os.clock()
		_scanTPCache = create
		_scanTPAt = now
	end

	return tbl3
end

_petUid = function(arg)
	if not arg then
		return nil
	end
	return tostring(arg.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(arg.slot)
end

_findTPSyncedPet = function(arg)
	local stealTargetUID = _G.StealTargetUID
	if type(stealTargetUID) ~= "string" or stealTargetUID == "" then
		return nil
	end

	for _, v4 in ipairs(arg) do
		if _petUid(v4) == stealTargetUID then
			return v4
		end
	end

	return nil
end

_clearTPSync = function()
	_G.TPSyncActive = false
	_G.StealTargetUID = nil
	_G.StealTarget = nil
end

_armTPSync = function(stealTarget)
	if not stealTarget then
		return
	end
	_G.StealTargetUID = _petUid(stealTarget)
	_G.StealTarget = stealTarget
	_G.TPSyncActive = true

	if _streamAround then
		pcall(_streamAround, stealTarget.position)
	end

	local tpSyncGen = (_G._TPSyncGen or 0) + 1
	_G._TPSyncGen = tpSyncGen

	task.delay(12, function()
		if _G._TPSyncGen == tpSyncGen then
			_clearTPSync()
		end
	end)
end

_G.ClearTPSync = _clearTPSync

_findStealTarget = function(arg)
	if not _G.TPSyncActive then
		return nil
	end
	return _findTPSyncedPet(arg)
end

_approachTableFor = function(arg)
	if arg > 23.15 then
		return MK_UPPER
	end

	if arg > 8.9 and _G.Floor2ViaFloor1 == false then
		return MK_UPPER
	end
	return MK_LOWER
end

COLUMN_SPLIT_X = -410

SIDE_NEAR_Z = 45

getClosestBaseIdx = function(arg)
	local huge = math.huge
	local n = 1

	for i = 1, 8 do
		local v4 = BASES_LOW[i]
		local n2 = (arg.X - v4.X) ^ 2 + (arg.Z - v4.Z) ^ 2

		if n2 < huge then
			huge = n2
			n = i
		end
	end

	return n
end

buildFrontCandidate = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
	local v4 = arg2 and BASES_HIGH[arg] or BASES_LOW[arg]
	arg2 = arg2 and FRONT_Y_HIGH or FRONT_Y_LOW
	local z = v4.Z
	return Vector3.new(v4.X, arg2, math.clamp(arg3 - v4.Z, -FRONT_Z_CLAMP, FRONT_Z_CLAMP) + z), arg <= 4 and Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0) or Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
end

	end

end

_floor1LaserSolid = function(arg)
	local flag2 = false

	pcall(function()
		local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
		plots = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(arg)
		if not plots then
			return
		end

		for _, descendant in ipairs(plots:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and (descendant.Name == "LaserHitbox" or descendant.Name == "Laser") and descendant.CanCollide and descendant.Position.Y <= 9 then
				flag2 = true
				break
			end
		end
	end)

	return flag2
end

isPlotUnlocked = function(arg)
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		local v4 = getPlotChannel(arg)
		if not v4 then
			return false
		end

		if channelGet(v4, "BlockEndTimeFirstFloor") ~= nil then
			return false
		end
		return not _floor1LaserSolid(arg)
	end)

	return ok and result == true
end

findClosest = function(arg, arg2)
	local huge = math.huge
	local v4 = nil
	local v5 = nil

	for k, v6 in pairs(arg2) do
		for _, v7 in ipairs(v6) do
			local coord = v7.coord
			local v8 = math.sqrt((arg.X - coord.X) ^ 2 + (arg.Z - coord.Z) ^ 2)

			if v8 < huge then
				huge = v8
				v4 = v7
				v5 = k
			end
		end
	end

	return v4, v5
end

_vizParts = {}
_vizGen = 0

_vizEnsure = function()
	if _vizFolder and _vizFolder.Parent then
		return
	end
	_vizFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	_vizFolder.Name = "Effects"
	_vizFolder.Parent = workspace
	_vizAnchor = Instance.new("Part")
	_vizAnchor.Name = "Anchor"
	_vizAnchor.Anchored = true
	_vizAnchor.CanCollide = false
	_vizAnchor.CanQuery = false
	_vizAnchor.CanTouch = false
	_vizAnchor.Transparency = 1
	_vizAnchor.Size = Vector3.one
	_vizAnchor.CFrame = CFrame.new()
	_vizAnchor.Parent = _vizFolder
end

clearViz = function()
	if _vizFolder then
		pcall(function()
			_vizFolder:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	_vizFolder = nil
	_vizAnchor = nil
	table.clear(_vizParts)
end

local function fn13(cFrame, size, color, arg, transparency)
	_vizEnsure()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CastShadow = false
	part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
	part.Color = color
	part.Transparency = transparency or 0

	if arg then
		part.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
	end

	part.Size = size
	part.CFrame = cFrame
	part.Parent = _vizFolder
end

vizLine = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
	local n = arg2 - arg
	if n.Magnitude < 0.05 then
		return
	end
	fn13(CFrame.lookAt((arg + arg2) * 0.5, arg2), Vector3.new(0.25, 0.25, n.Magnitude), arg3, false, 0)
end

vizDot = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
	fn13(CFrame.new(arg), Vector3.new(arg3, arg3, arg3), arg2, true, 0)
end

vizPath = function(arg, arg2)
	if _G.ShowPath ~= true then
		return
	end

	if #arg2 == 0 then
		return
	end
	local color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 195, 45)

	for _, v4 in ipairs(arg2) do
		vizLine(arg, v4, color)
		arg = v4
	end

	vizDot(arg2[#arg2], color, 1.6)
end

		_vzL(arg)

_setFlightVel = function(arg, assemblyLinearVelocity)
	if not arg or not arg.Parent then
		return
	end
	local parent = arg.Parent

	if parent then
		parent = parent:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or parent:FindFirstChild("Torso")
	end

	parent = parent or arg

	if parent then
		parent.AssemblyLinearVelocity = assemblyLinearVelocity
	end
end

velMoveThrough = function(parent, arg, arg2, arg3)
	if not parent or not parent.Parent or #arg == 0 then
		return
	end
	local n = arg2 or _G.TPVelocity and math.clamp(_G.TPVelocity, 200, 600) or CARPET_SPEED
	pcall(vizPath, parent.Position, arg)
	local n2 = 1
	local flag2 = false
	local connection = nil
	local humanoid = parent.Parent and parent.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local autoRotate = nil
	local v4 = nil

	local function fn14()
		if flag2 then
			return
		end
		flag2 = true

		if humanoid and humanoid.Parent and autoRotate ~= nil then
			pcall(function()
				humanoid.AutoRotate = autoRotate
			end)
		end

		if parent and parent.Parent then
			_vzL(parent)
			_vzA(parent)
			local v5 = v4

			if not v4 then
				local v6
				v6, v5 = parent.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
			end

			parent.CFrame = CFrame.new(arg[#arg]) * CFrame.Angles(0, v5, 0)
		end

		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
		end
	end

	if _G.LockFlightYaw ~= false then
		if humanoid then
			autoRotate = humanoid.AutoRotate

			pcall(function()
				humanoid.AutoRotate = false
			end)
		end

		local v5 = arg[#arg]
		local vector = Vector3.new(v5.X - parent.Position.X, 0, v5.Z - parent.Position.Z)

		if vector.Magnitude > 0.5 then
			local unit = vector.Unit
			v4 = math.atan2(-unit.X, -unit.Z)
		else
			local v6
			v6, v4 = parent.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
		end
	end

	local huge = math.huge
	local n3 = 0
	os.clock()
	local n4 = 0
	local position = parent.Position
	local n5 = 0

	for _, v5 in ipairs(arg) do
		n5 += (position - v5).Magnitude
		position = v5
	end

	local flag3 = arg3 ~= false and _G.TPJump ~= false
	local flag4

	if flag3 then
		flag4 = n5 >= (tonumber(_G.JumpMinDist) or 0)
	else
		flag4 = flag3
	end

	local tbl3 = { on = false, bank = 0, lastT = os.clock(), pauseUntil = 0, startT = os.clock(), lagbacks = 0 }
	local ryBoost = _G.RyBoost

	if type(ryBoost) == "table" and ryBoost.on == true then
		tbl3.on = true
		tbl3.speed = math.clamp(tonumber(ryBoost.speed) or 400, 50, 1500)
		tbl3.step = math.clamp(tonumber(ryBoost.step) or 12, 2, 60)
		tbl3.rp = RaycastParams.new()
		tbl3.rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		tbl3.rp.IgnoreWater = true
		tbl3.skip = {}

		for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if player.Character then
				tbl3.skip[#tbl3.skip + 1] = player.Character
			end
		end

		local v5 = ipairs
		local tbl4 = _OTHER_CLONES or {}

		for _, v6 in v5(tbl4) do
			tbl3.skip[#tbl3.skip + 1] = v6
		end

		if workspace.CurrentCamera then
			tbl3.skip[#tbl3.skip + 1] = workspace.CurrentCamera
		end

		ryBoost.hops = 0
		ryBoost.frames = 0
		ryBoost.skip = {}
	end

	tbl3.clear = function(arg4, arg5)
		local n6 = arg5 - arg4
		if n6.Magnitude < 0.05 then
			return true
		end

		for _, v5 in ipairs({ 0, 2.5 }) do
			local n7 = arg4 + Vector3.new(0, v5, 0)
			local v6 = table.clone(tbl3.skip)

			for i = 1, 6 do
				tbl3.rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = v6
				local hit = workspace:Raycast(n7, n6, tbl3.rp)
				if not hit then
					break
				end

				if hit.Instance.CanCollide then
					return false
				end
				v6[#v6 + 1] = hit.Instance
			end
		end

		return true
	end

	local n6 = 0
	local flag5 = false
	local position2 = nil
	local n7 = 0
	local n8 = 0
	local magnitude = nil
	local v5 = nil
	local flag6 = nil
	local flag7 = false
	local n9 = 0
	local n10 = 1
	local v6 = nil
	local position3 = nil

		local n11 = deltaTime or 0.0166

			if connection then
				connection:Disconnect()
			end

		end

			return

					n13 = n12 - 6.2831853071795862
				else
					n13 = n12
				end

				end

			end)

		local flag8 = flag4

			flag8 = now - n4 >= (tonumber(_G.TPJumpGap) or 0.12)
		end

					humanoid2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
					humanoid2.Jump = true

		local v8 = n
		local n13 = n

								end

							pcall(function()
								if type(fireGrapple) == "function" then
									fireGrapple()
								end
							end)
						end)
					end
				end
			end

					end
				end

			end

		end

		position3 = parent.Position
		local n14 = math.max(MK_ARRIVE, n13 / 60 * 1.25)

				parent.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
