				local humanoidRootPart2 = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if not (not humanoidRootPart2 or not humanoidRootPart2.Parent) then
					if (Vector3.new(humanoidRootPart2.Position.X, 0, humanoidRootPart2.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(coord.X, 0, coord.Z)).Magnitude <= 3.5 and math.abs(humanoidRootPart2.Position.Y - coord.Y) <= 4 then
						n10 += 1
						if not (n10 >= 4) then
							RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
							continue
						end
					else
						pcall(function()
							humanoidRootPart2.CFrame = CFrame.new(coord, coord + face)
						end)

						_vzL(humanoidRootPart2)
						_vzA(humanoidRootPart2)
						n10 = 0
						RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
						continue
					end
				end
			end

			break
		end

		if n10 < 4 and not _G.TPStop and key and _G.ApproachFails then
			_G.ApproachFails[key] = os.clock()
		end

		local humanoidRootPart2 = localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local position4 = humanoidRootPart2 and humanoidRootPart2.Parent and humanoidRootPart2.Position or coord
		local part = Instance.new("Part")
		part.Size = Vector3.new(12, 1, 12)
		part.Position = Vector3.new(position4.X, position4.Y - 3, position4.Z)
		part.Anchored = true
		part.CanCollide = true
		part.Transparency = 1
		part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
		part.Parent = workspace

		if humanoidRootPart2 and humanoidRootPart2.Parent then
			_vzL(humanoidRootPart2)
			_vzA(humanoidRootPart2)
		end

		local character2 = localPlayer2.Character
		local humanoidRootPart3 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		humanoidRootPart3 = humanoidRootPart3 and humanoidRootPart3.Position or coord
		local flag6 = false

		local connection2 = localPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
			flag6 = true
		end)

		_G.StealHold = false
		task.wait(tonumber(_G.LandingDelay) or tonumber(_G.TPCloneDelay) or 0.15)

		if face and face.Magnitude > 0.1 then
			local humanoid2 = localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if humanoid2 then
				pcall(function()
					humanoid2.AutoRotate = false
				end)
			end

			for i = 1, 4 do
				local humanoidRootPart4 = localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if not (not humanoidRootPart4 or not humanoidRootPart4.Parent) then
					pcall(function()
						humanoidRootPart4.CFrame = CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart4.Position, humanoidRootPart4.Position + face)
						_vzL(humanoidRootPart4)
						_vzA(humanoidRootPart4)
					end)

					RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
					continue
				end

				break
			end
		end

		local v25 = doClone()

		if part then
			local v26 = part

			task.delay(1.5, function()
				pcall(function()
					v26:Destroy()
				end)
			end)
		end

		local now5 = os.clock()

		while not flag6 do
			if localPlayer2.Character == character2 then
				local humanoidRootPart4 = localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local n11, n12

				if humanoidRootPart4 then
					local n13 = humanoidRootPart4.Position.X - humanoidRootPart3.X
					local n14 = humanoidRootPart4.Position.Z - humanoidRootPart3.Z

					if not (n13 * n13 + n14 * n14 > 1) then
						RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
						n11 = os.clock() - now5
						n12 = tonumber(_G.CloneSettle) or 0.5
						if not (n12 < n11) then
							continue
						end
					end
				else
					RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
					n11 = os.clock() - now5
					n12 = tonumber(_G.CloneSettle) or 0.5
					if not (n12 < n11) then
						continue
					end
				end
			end

			break
		end

		if v25 and _G.CloneAnchor == true then
			local humanoidRootPart4 = localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if humanoidRootPart4 then
				pcall(function()
					humanoidRootPart4.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					humanoidRootPart4.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
					humanoidRootPart4.Anchored = true
				end)

				task.wait(tonumber(_G.CloneAnchorHold) or 0.1)
				local humanoidRootPart5 = localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if humanoidRootPart5 then
					pcall(function()
						humanoidRootPart5.Anchored = false
					end)
				end
			end
		end

		if connection2 then
			connection2:Disconnect()
		end

		pcall(function()
			local humanoid2 = localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if humanoid2 then
				humanoid2.AutoRotate = true
			end
		end)

		goToBrainrot(position, v6 and v6.slot)
		isTeleporting = false
		if _G.TPStop then
			return
		end

		if _G.ArmSteal then
			pcall(_G.ArmSteal, v6)
		end

		_G.StealSay("TP arrive -> " .. tostring(v6 and v6.name) .. " [" .. tostring(v6 and v6.plot) .. "/" .. tostring(v6 and v6.slot) .. "]")
	end)

	if not ok then
		fn12("[TP] doVelocityTP error: " .. tostring(result))
	end

	isTeleporting = false
	_G.StealHold = false
	_G.RyTPActive = false
end

_manualTPBusy = false

manualFullTP = function()
	if _manualTPBusy or isTeleporting then
		return
	end

	if localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
		return
	end
	_manualTPBusy = true

	if _G.ScanProfile and _G.ScanMode == "fast" then
		_G.ScanProfile("normal")
	end

	local ok = pcall(function()
		local now = os.clock()
		local n = -1
		local v4 = nil

		while true do
			local ok, result = pcall(scanForTP)
			local n2, n3

			if ok and result and #result > 0 then
				local str = tostring(result[1].plot) .. "_" .. tostring(result[1].slot)

				if not (#result == n and str == v4) then
					n = #result
					task.wait(tonumber(_G.ScanSettle) or 0.06)
					v4 = str
					n2 = os.clock() - now
					n3 = tonumber(_G.TPMaxWait) or 4
					if not (n3 < n2) then
						continue
					end
				end
			else
				task.wait(0.05)
				n2 = os.clock() - now
				n3 = tonumber(_G.TPMaxWait) or 4
				if not (n3 < n2) then
					continue
				end
			end

			break
		end

		doVelocityTP(true)
	end)

	_manualTPBusy = false
	return ok
end

_G.StartSideTP = manualFullTP

UserInputService2.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
	if gameProcessed then
		return
	end

	if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
		return
	end
	local stpTpKeyName = _G._stp_tpKeyName

	if type(stpTpKeyName) ~= "string" or stpTpKeyName == "" then
		stpTpKeyName = "T"
	end

	if input.KeyCode.Name == stpTpKeyName then
		task.spawn(function()
			pcall(manualFullTP)
		end)
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	pcall(loadModules)
	pcall(loadNet)
end)

_G.ChannelsReady = false

task.spawn(function()
	local now = os.clock()

	while true do
		pcall(loadModules)
		local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")

		if plots then
			local children = plots:GetChildren()

			if #children > 0 then
				local syncAll = nil

				pcall(function()
					syncAll = _G.SyncAll and _G.SyncAll()
				end)

				if syncAll then
					local flag2 = true

					for _, child in ipairs(children) do
						if not rawget(syncAll, child.Name) then
							flag2 = false
							break
						end
					end

					if flag2 then
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end

		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		if os.clock() - now > 25 then
			return
		end
	end

	_G.ChannelsReady = true
end)

task.spawn(function()
	local character = localPlayer2.Character or localPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Wait()
	character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
	character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
	pcall(loadModules)
	pcall(loadNet)

	if _G.AutoTP ~= false then
		task.spawn(function()
			local now = os.clock()

			while os.clock() - now < 12 do
				for _, v4 in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
					if findTool(v4) then
						pcall(carpetEngage)
						return
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.05)
			end
		end)
	end

	local function fn17()
		local character2 = localPlayer2.Character
		if not character2 then
			return false
		end

		for _, v4 in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
			local v5 = character2:FindFirstChild(v4)
			if v5 and v5:IsA("Tool") then
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	local now = os.clock()
	local n = tonumber(_G.StartScanSettle) or 1.5
	local n2 = tonumber(_G.StartCarpetWait) or 0.8
	local n3 = math.max(n, n2)
	local flag2 = false

	while true do
		if not flag2 then
			local ok, result = pcall(scanForTP)

			if ok and type(result) == "table" and #result > 0 then
				flag2 = true
			end
		end

		if not (flag2 and (fn17() or os.clock() - now >= n2)) then
			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			if not (n3 < os.clock() - now) then
				continue
			end
		end

		break
	end

	local n4 = tonumber(_G._stp_tpDelay) or tonumber(_G.TPDelay) or 0

	if n4 > 0 then
		task.wait(n4)
	end

	if _G.AutoTP == false then
		return
	end
	_G.StealTargetUID = nil
	_G.StealTarget = nil
	_G.TPSyncActive = false

	if _G.DisarmSteal then
		pcall(_G.DisarmSteal)
	end

	local n5 = tonumber(_G.StartTPTries) or 12
	local n6 = 0
	local v4

	while n6 < n5 do
		n6 += 1

		if _G.AutoTP ~= false then
			if localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
				local now2, n7, ok, result, v5, v6, v7, v8, v9, position, pri, flag3, flag4, flag5, mps, mps2, v10, v11, mps3, wait, n8, flag6, wait2, num, n9

				if isTeleporting then
					local now3 = os.clock()

					while isTeleporting and os.clock() - now3 < 15 do
						task.wait(0.2)
					end

					if localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
						now2 = os.clock()
						n7 = tonumber(_G.StartScanWait) or 6

						while true do
							ok, result = pcall(scanForTP)
							v4 = result
							ok = ok and type(v4) == "table" and #v4 > 0

							if not ok then
								RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
								v4 = nil
								if not (os.clock() - now2 > n7) then
									continue
								end
							end

							break
						end

						if not v4 then
							_G.StealSay("start-tp: noch keine Pets (Versuch " .. n6 .. ")")
							task.wait(0.5)
							continue
						else
							v5, v6, v7 = ipairs(v4)
							v8 = nil
							v9 = nil

							for _, v12 in v5, v6, v7 do
								position = _petEligible(v12) and v12.position

								if position then
									pri = v12._pri

									if pri then
										flag3 = not v8
										flag4 = flag3 or v12._pri < v8._pri
									else
										flag4 = pri
									end

									if flag4 then
										v8 = v12
									end

									flag5 = not v9

									if not flag5 then
										mps = v12.mps or 0
										mps2 = v9.mps or 0
										flag5 = mps > mps2
									end

									if flag5 then
										v9 = v12
									end
								end
							end

							v10 = v8 or v9

							if v10 then
								v11 = tostring
								mps3 = v10.mps
								_G.StealSay(string.format("start-tp Versuch %d -> %s  pri=%s  mps=%s", n6, tostring(v10.name), tostring(v10._pri), v11(mps3)))

								pcall(function()
									_armTPSync(v10)
								end)

								_G.NoApproachFor = nil

								task.spawn(function()
									pcall(function()
										doVelocityTP(true)
									end)
								end)

								wait = task.wait
								n8 = tonumber(_G.StartTPCheck) or 0.3
								wait(n8)
								flag6 = isTeleporting or localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true

								if not flag6 then
									_G.StealSay("start-tp Versuch " .. n6 .. " ist nicht angelaufen -> nochmal")
									_G.StealTargetUID = nil
									_G.StealTarget = nil
									_G.TPSyncActive = false

									if _G.NoApproachFor == v10.name then
										_G.StealSay("start-tp: " .. tostring(v10.name) .. " has no approach point -- backing off")
										wait2 = task.wait
										num = tonumber(_G.NoApproachBackoff)
										n9 = num or 2
										wait2(n9)
									else
										task.wait(math.min(0.25 * n6, 1.5))
									end

									continue
								end
							else
								task.wait(0.4)
								continue
							end
						end
					end
				else
					now2 = os.clock()
					n7 = tonumber(_G.StartScanWait) or 6

					while true do
						ok, result = pcall(scanForTP)
						v4 = result
						ok = ok and type(v4) == "table" and #v4 > 0

						if not ok then
							RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
							v4 = nil
							if not (os.clock() - now2 > n7) then
								continue
							end
						end

						break
					end

					if not v4 then
						_G.StealSay("start-tp: noch keine Pets (Versuch " .. n6 .. ")")
						task.wait(0.5)
						continue
					else
						v5, v6, v7 = ipairs(v4)
						v8 = nil
						v9 = nil

						for _, v12 in v5, v6, v7 do
							position = _petEligible(v12) and v12.position

							if position then
								pri = v12._pri

								if pri then
									flag3 = not v8
									flag4 = flag3 or v12._pri < v8._pri
								else
									flag4 = pri
								end

								if flag4 then
									v8 = v12
								end

								flag5 = not v9

								if not flag5 then
									mps = v12.mps or 0
									mps2 = v9.mps or 0
									flag5 = mps > mps2
								end

								if flag5 then
									v9 = v12
								end
							end
						end

						v10 = v8 or v9

						if v10 then
							v11 = tostring
							mps3 = v10.mps
							_G.StealSay(string.format("start-tp Versuch %d -> %s  pri=%s  mps=%s", n6, tostring(v10.name), tostring(v10._pri), v11(mps3)))

							pcall(function()
								_armTPSync(v10)
							end)

							_G.NoApproachFor = nil

							task.spawn(function()
								pcall(function()
									doVelocityTP(true)
								end)
							end)

							wait = task.wait
							n8 = tonumber(_G.StartTPCheck) or 0.3
							wait(n8)
							flag6 = isTeleporting or localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true

							if not flag6 then
								_G.StealSay("start-tp Versuch " .. n6 .. " ist nicht angelaufen -> nochmal")
								_G.StealTargetUID = nil
								_G.StealTarget = nil
								_G.TPSyncActive = false

								if _G.NoApproachFor == v10.name then
									_G.StealSay("start-tp: " .. tostring(v10.name) .. " has no approach point -- backing off")
									wait2 = task.wait
									num = tonumber(_G.NoApproachBackoff)
									n9 = num or 2
									wait2(n9)
								else
									task.wait(math.min(0.25 * n6, 1.5))
								end

								continue
							end
						else
							task.wait(0.4)
							continue
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		break
	end

	if _G.ScanProfile then
		_G.ScanProfile("normal")
	end
end)

local RunService2, localPlayer3
local Players2 = game:GetService("Players")
RunService2 = game:GetService("RunService")
localPlayer3 = Players2.LocalPlayer
local fn17, obj, n, n2, n3, n4, flag2, v4, n5, n6
local v5, name, n7, n8, n9, n10, v6, n11, n12, fn18
local fn19, fn20, fn21, n13, fn22, fn23, fn24, tbl4, n14, n15

do
	local playerGui = localPlayer3:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

	fn17 = function(arg)
		if not arg then
			return nil
		end

		if arg.plot and arg.slot then
			local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
			plots = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(arg.plot)
			plots = plots and plots:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
			plots = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(tostring(arg.slot))

			if plots then
				local base = plots:FindFirstChild("Base")
				base = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
				base = base and base:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")

				if base then
					for _, child in ipairs(base:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
							return child
						end
					end
				end

				for _, descendant in ipairs(plots:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
						return descendant
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	obj = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	n = 0
	n2 = 0
	n3 = 1.3
	local n16 = tonumber(_G.StealProximity) or 28
	n4 = 0
	flag2 = false

	_G.IsStealHoldActive = function()
		return flag2
	end

	v4 = nil
	n5 = 0
	n6 = 0
	v5 = nil
	name = nil

				local v17 = _ragUntil()
				local now = tick()
				local n18 = n17 + (tonumber(_G.RagdollHoldCap) or 35)
				local flag3

				while true do
					if v15 ~= n2 then
						flag3 = true
						break
					else
						if _G.RagdollStealSync ~= false then
							fn30(_ragBlockedNow())
							local v18 = _ragUntil()

							if v18 > v17 + 0.3 then
								local v19 = _ragRemaining()

								if v19 > 0.1 then
									n4 = math.max(n4, tick() + v19 - n17 + (tonumber(_G.RagdollLatePad) or 0.1))
									v17 = v18
								end
							end
						end

						local n19 = tick() - n4
						flag3 = false

						if not (n17 <= n19) then
							if not (tick() - now > n18) then
								fn32(math.clamp(n19 / n17, 0, 1))
								RunService2.Heartbeat:Wait()
								continue
							end
						end

						break
					end
				end

				fn30(false)
				fn32(1)

				if flag3 then
					pcall(function()
						arg:InputHoldEnd()
					end)

					_G.RyLog("steal", "claim hold abandoned (respawned mid-hold)")
				elseif arg and arg.Parent then
					local v18 = ipairs
					local triggerCallbacks = v14.triggerCallbacks or {}

					for _, triggerCallback in v18(triggerCallbacks) do
						task.spawn(triggerCallback)
					end

					pcall(function()
						arg:InputHoldEnd()
					end)

					if type(fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
						pcall(function()
							fireproximityprompt(arg, 1)
						end)

						pcall(function()
							fireproximityprompt(arg, 0)
						end)
					end
				end

				local v18 = ipairs
				local holdEndCallbacks = v14.holdEndCallbacks or {}

				for _, holdEndCallback in v18(holdEndCallbacks) do
					task.spawn(holdEndCallback)
				end
			end)

			flag2 = false

			pcall(function()
				fn30(false)
			end)

			if maxActivationDistance ~= nil then
				pcall(function()
					if arg and arg.Parent then
						arg.MaxActivationDistance = maxActivationDistance
					end
				end)
			end

			if not ok then
				_G.StealSay("steal hold errored -> " .. tostring(result))

				pcall(function()
					arg:InputHoldEnd()
				end)
			end

			task.wait(0.05)
			v14.ready = true
			task.wait(0.2)
			fn33()
		end)

		task.delay(n3 + 0.6, function()
			if not flag2 then
				fn33()
			end
		end)

		return true
	end

	local function fn39()
		if localPlayer3:GetAttribute("Stealing") or localPlayer3:GetAttribute("IsTrading") or localPlayer3:GetAttribute("IsDuelSelecting") or localPlayer3:GetAttribute("Web") then
			return -1
		end
		local v14 = _ragRemaining()
		if v14 > 0 then
			return v14
		end
		return 0
	end

	local flag3 = _G.StealMode ~= nil
	local n17 = 0

	local function fireSteal(arg)
		if not arg then
			return false
		end

		if _G.RyBaseGrabEnabled == true then
			return false
		end

		if fn24() then
			_G.StealSay("hold already running")
			return false
		end
		local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer3.Character and localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local magnitude = humanoidRootPart and arg.position and (arg.position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude or nil
		local flag4 = magnitude ~= nil and magnitude <= fn20() + 2 or false
		local n18 = fn21() + (tonumber(_G.VerifyDelay) or 0.5) + 0.15
		_G.StealSay("gates ok -> firing on " .. tostring(arg.name))

		if _G.RemoteStealOn ~= false then
			if fn37(arg, flag4) then
				_G.LastFire = os.clock()
				n13 = tick() + n18
				_G.StealSay("FIRE remote -> " .. tostring(arg.name))
				return true
			end

			_G.StealSay("remote not ready -> prompt path")
		end

		local v14 = fn17(arg)

		if not v14 or not v14.Parent then
			local str3 = "no prompt"

			pcall(function()
				local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
				local v15 = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(arg.plot)
				local animalPodiums = v15 and v15:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")

				if not v15 then
					str3 = "plot not streamed in (or stripped)"
				elseif not animalPodiums then
					str3 = "AnimalPodiums not streamed in"
				elseif not animalPodiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(arg.slot)) then
					str3 = "podium " .. tostring(arg.slot) .. " not streamed in"
				else
					str3 = "podium present but no prompt on it yet"
				end
			end)

			_streamAround(arg.position)
			_G.StealSay(str3 .. " at " .. tostring(arg.plot) .. "/" .. tostring(arg.slot) .. " -> stream requested, will retry")

			if _G.RyFileLog then
				local v15 = tostring
				local slot = arg.slot
				pcall(_G.RyFileLog, "steal", string.format("%s at %s/%s -> stream requested", str3, tostring(arg.plot), v15(slot)))
			end

			return false
		end

		local maxActivationDistance = nil

		pcall(function()
			maxActivationDistance = v14.MaxActivationDistance
		end)

		pcall(function()
			v14.MaxActivationDistance = math.huge
		end)

		pcall(function()
			v14.RequiresLineOfSight = false
		end)

		fn34(v14)

		if obj[v14] then
			if fn38(v14, arg.name, maxActivationDistance) then
				_G.LastFire = os.clock()
				n13 = tick() + n18
				fn23(arg, "prompt", flag4)
				_G.StealSay("FIRE prompt-callback -> " .. tostring(arg.name))
				return true
			end

			_G.StealSay("prompt-callback not ready")

			if maxActivationDistance ~= nil then
				pcall(function()
					v14.MaxActivationDistance = maxActivationDistance
				end)
			end

			return false
		end

		if fireproximityprompt then
			pcall(function()
				v14.MaxActivationDistance = math.huge
			end)

			pcall(function()
				v14.RequiresLineOfSight = false
			end)

			fn31(arg.name, 1)

			pcall(function()
				fireproximityprompt(v14)
			end)

			_G.LastFire = os.clock()
			n13 = tick() + (tonumber(_G.VerifyDelay) or 0.5) + 0.5
			fn23(arg, "prompt", flag4)
			_G.StealSay("FIRE proximityprompt -> " .. tostring(arg.name))
			task.delay(0.4, fn33)

			pcall(function()
				if maxActivationDistance ~= nil then
					v14.MaxActivationDistance = maxActivationDistance
				end
			end)

			return true
		end

		_G.StealSay("no steal method available")
		return false
	end

	_G.FireSteal = fireSteal

	local function fn40()
		_G.TickN = (_G.TickN or 0) + 1
		flag3 = _G.StealMode ~= nil
		if not flag3 then
			_G.StealSay("steal mode OFF")
			return
		end
		local now = os.clock()
		local priVersion = _G.PriVersion or 0

		if priVersion ~= n12 then
			n12 = priVersion
			v6 = nil
			n11 = 0
			tbl4 = nil
			n14 = 0
			v4 = nil
			n6 = 0
			n8 = 0
			_G.StealSay("priority list changed -> repick")
		end

		local stealTargetUID = _G.StealMode == "manual" and _G.StealTargetUID or nil
		local flag4 = type(stealTargetUID) == "string" and stealTargetUID ~= "" and stealTargetUID ~= v5
		local flag5 = not v4 or flag4

		if not flag5 then
			flag5 = now - n6 >= (tonumber(_G.RepickGap) or 0.35)
		end

		if tbl4 then
			local v14 = fn25(tbl4)

			if v14 then
				tbl4.position = v14
				n14 = 0
			elseif n14 == 0 then
				n14 = now
			else
				local n18 = now - n14

				if (tonumber(_G.ArmLostGrace) or 1.5) < n18 then
					tbl4 = nil
					n14 = 0
				end
			end

			local flag6 = tbl4

			if tbl4 then
				flag6 = now - n10 >= (tonumber(_G.ArmCheckGap) or 0.5)
			end

			if flag6 then
				n10 = now
				local ok, result = pcall(fn19, tbl4.plot, tbl4.slot)

				if ok and not result then
					_G.StealSay("armed pet gone -> repicking")
					tbl4 = nil
					n14 = 0
				end
			end

			if tbl4 and not isTeleporting and not fn24() then
				local character = localPlayer3.Character
				character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local position = character and tbl4.position

				if position then
					position = (tbl4.position - character.Position).Magnitude > (tonumber(_G.ArmFarDist) or 150)
				end

				if position then
					if n15 == 0 then
						n15 = now
					else
						local n18 = now - n15

						if (tonumber(_G.ArmFarGrace) or 3) < n18 then
							_G.RyLog("steal", "released the lock on " .. tostring(tbl4.name) .. ": you are far from it")
							tbl4 = nil
							n14 = 0
							n15 = 0
						end
					end
				else
					n15 = 0
				end

				local flag7 = tbl4

				if tbl4 then
					flag7 = now - (n5 or now) > (tonumber(_G.ArmMaxAge) or 20)
				end

				if flag7 then
					flag7 = os.clock() - (_G.LastFire or 0) > 3
				end

				if flag7 then
					_G.RyLog("steal", "lock on " .. tostring(tbl4.name) .. " expired with no claim landing -> repicking")
					tbl4 = nil
					n14 = 0
					n15 = 0
				end
			end

			if tbl4 then
				v4 = tbl4

				if type(tbl4.name) == "string" then
					name = tbl4.name
				end

				flag5 = false
			end
		end

		local n18 = n17 >= 3 and (tonumber(_G.StealIdleGap) or 0.3) or 0.01

		if not localPlayer3:GetAttribute("Stealing") and flag5 and now - n8 >= n18 then
			n8 = now
			n6 = now
			local character = localPlayer3.Character
			character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if character then
				local v14 = fn18(now, 0.05)
				local flag6 = v14 ~= nil

				if flag6 and v14 and #v14 > 0 then
					n17 = 0
				else
					n17 += 1
				end

				if flag6 and v14 and #v14 > 0 then
					local flag7 = type(stealTargetUID) == "string" and stealTargetUID ~= ""
					local v15 = nil

					if flag7 then
						v15 = nil

						for _, v16 in ipairs(v14) do
							if not v16.conveyor and _petUid(v16) == stealTargetUID then
								v15 = v16
								break
							else
								v15 = nil
							end
						end
					end

					if not v15 and _G.StealMode == "nearest" then
						local position = character.Position
						local huge = math.huge

						for _, v16 in ipairs(v14) do
							if _petEligible(v16) and v16.position and fn22(v16) then
								local magnitude = (v16.position - position).Magnitude

								if magnitude < huge then
									huge = magnitude
									v15 = v16
								end
							end
						end
					elseif not v15 then
						for _, v16 in ipairs(v14) do
							if _petEligible(v16) and fn22(v16) then
								v15 = v16
								break
							end
						end
					end

					if not v15 then
						for _, v16 in ipairs(v14) do
							if _petEligible(v16) and fn22(v16) then
								v15 = v16
								break
							end
						end
					end

					local flag8 = not v15 and v4 and not fn22(v4)

					if flag8 then
						flag8 = not (type(stealTargetUID) == "string" and stealTargetUID ~= "")
					end

					if flag8 then
						v4 = nil
					end

					if v15 then
						v4 = v15
						n5 = now
						v5 = stealTargetUID

						if type(v15.name) == "string" and v15.name ~= "" then
							name = v15.name
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local flag6 = _G.StealInRangeFirst ~= false

		if flag6 then
			flag6 = not (tbl4 and _G.TPTargetWins ~= false)
		end

		if flag6 then
			flag6 = not (type(stealTargetUID) == "string" and stealTargetUID ~= "")
		end

		if flag6 then
			local character = localPlayer3.Character
			character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if character then
				local v14 = v4
				local position = v4

				if v14 then
					position = v14.position
				end

				local flag7 = (position and (v14.position - character.Position).Magnitude or math.huge) > fn20()

				if flag7 then
					flag7 = now - n9 >= (tonumber(_G.InRangeGap) or 0.25)
				end

				if flag7 then
					n9 = now
					local v15 = fn18(now, 0.15)

					if v15 ~= nil and type(v15) == "table" then
						local position2 = character.Position
						local v16, v17, v18 = ipairs(v15)
						local huge = math.huge
						local v19 = nil

						for _, v20 in v16, v17, v18 do
							if _petEligible(v20) and v20.position and fn22(v20) then
								local magnitude = (v20.position - position2).Magnitude

								if magnitude < huge then
									huge = magnitude
									v19 = v20
								end
							end
						end

						if v19 and huge <= fn20() then
							v4 = v19
							tbl4 = nil
							n14 = 0
							n6 = now

							if type(v19.name) == "string" then
								name = v19.name
							end

							_G.StealSay(string.format("switch to in-range %s d=%.0f", tostring(v19.name), huge))
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local v14 = v4

		if not v14 then
			if _G.ActiveStealUid ~= nil then
				_G.ActiveStealUid = nil
			end

			_G.StealSay("no target")
			return
		end

		local v15 = _petUid(v14)

		if _G.ActiveStealUid ~= v15 then
			_G.ActiveStealUid = v15
		end

		if now - n7 < 0.033 then
			_G.ThrN = (_G.ThrN or 0) + 1
			return
		end
		n7 = now
		local v16 = fn39()

		if v16 == -1 then
			if localPlayer3:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
				v4 = nil
				tbl4 = nil
				n14 = 0
			end

			_G.StealSay("busy: stealing/trading/web")
			return
		end

		if v16 > 0 and v16 > fn21() then
			_G.StealSay(string.format("cooldown %.1fs", v16))
			return
		end
		local character = localPlayer3.Character
		character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not character then
			_G.StealSay("no character")
			return
		end

		if v14.position then
			local n19 = v14.position - character.Position
			local magnitude = n19.Magnitude
			_G.StealTgt = string.format("%s [%s/%s] d=%.0f", tostring(v14.name), tostring(v14.plot), tostring(v14.slot), magnitude)
			local n20 = tonumber(_G.StealHoldDuration) or 1.3
			local n21 = tonumber(_G.StealLead) or 0.27
			local n22 = magnitude > 0.001 and character.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Dot(n19) / magnitude or 0
			local v17 = getClosestBaseIdx(v14.position)
			v17 = v17 and BASES_LOW[v17]
			local flag7 = false
			local huge = math.huge

			if v17 then
				local n23 = character.Position.X - v17.X
				local n24 = character.Position.Z - v17.Z
				huge = math.sqrt(n23 * n23 + n24 * n24)

				if huge <= (tonumber(_G.CloseBaseRange) or 100) then
					flag7 = true
				end
			end

			local n23 = tonumber(_G.GrabStartRange) or 72

			if huge <= n23 then
				flag7 = true
			end

			if flag7 then
				if huge > n23 and magnitude > n23 then
					_G.StealSay(string.format("nearbase far d=%.0f base=%.0f (start ab %.0f)", magnitude, huge, n23))
					return
				end
			elseif n22 > (tonumber(_G.FastApproach) or 150) then
				if (tonumber(_G.StealLeadMax) or 223) < magnitude then
					_G.StealSay(string.format("lead far d=%.0f", magnitude))
					return
				end

				if magnitude / n22 > n20 * n21 then
					_G.StealSay(string.format("lead wait d=%.0f c=%.0f", magnitude, n22))
					return
				end
			elseif n22 > 5 then
				if (tonumber(_G.CloseProximity) or 24) < magnitude then
					_G.StealSay(string.format("moving far d=%.0f c=%.0f", magnitude, n22))
					return
				end
			elseif magnitude > fn20() then
				_G.StealSay(string.format("far d=%.0f", magnitude))
				return
			end
		end

		fireSteal(v14)
	end

	local n18 = 0
	local ryHubSession = _G.RyHubSession
	local n19 = 0
	local connection = nil

	connection = RunService2.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if _G.RyHubSession ~= ryHubSession then
			if connection then
				connection:Disconnect()
			end

			return
		end

		if _G.StealMode == nil then
			flag3 = false
			local now = os.clock()

			if now - n18 >= 0.5 then
				n18 = now
				_G.StealSay("steal mode OFF")
			end

			return
		end

		local ok, result = pcall(fn40)

		if not ok then
			_G.StealSay("ERROR " .. tostring(result))
			n19 += 1

			if n19 == 3 or n19 % 300 == 0 then
				_G.RyWarn("steal", string.format("steal loop error (x%d): %s -> target state reset", n19, tostring(result)))

				if _G.StealForceRepick then
					pcall(_G.StealForceRepick)
				end
			end
		else
			n19 = 0
		end
	end)

	localPlayer3.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		if _G.RyHubSession ~= ryHubSession then
			return
		end
		n += 1
		n2 += 1
		tbl4 = nil
		n14 = 0
		n15 = 0
		v4 = nil
		v6 = nil
		n11 = 0
		n13 = 0
		n6 = 0
		n8 = 0
		_G.RyLog("steal", "respawned -> steal target, lock and cached prompt handlers reset")
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		while _G.RyHubSession == ryHubSession do
			task.wait(tonumber(_G.WatchdogTick) or 0.2)
			local flag4 = _G.StealMode ~= nil and _G.StealWatchdog ~= false and not localPlayer3:GetAttribute("Stealing") and not fn24()
			local flag5

			if flag4 then
				flag5 = os.clock() - (_G.LastFire or 0) > (tonumber(_G.WatchdogGap) or 1.5)
			else
				flag5 = flag4
			end

			if flag5 then
				local character = localPlayer3.Character
				character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if character then
					local v14 = fn18(os.clock(), 0.15)

					if v14 ~= nil and type(v14) == "table" then
						local position = character.Position
						local v15, v16, v17 = ipairs(v14)
						local huge = math.huge
						local v18 = nil

						for _, v19 in v15, v16, v17 do
							if _petEligible(v19) and v19.position and fn22(v19) then
								local magnitude = (v19.position - position).Magnitude

								if magnitude < huge then
									huge = magnitude
									v18 = v19
								end
							end
						end

						if v18 and huge <= fn20() then
							_G.StealSay(string.format("WATCHDOG in range %s d=%.0f", tostring(v18.name), huge))
							pcall(fireSteal, v18)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

do
	local function fn25()
		if _G.invisibleStealEnabled then
			return true
		end
		local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not currentCamera then
			return false
		end

		for _, child in ipairs(currentCamera:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	local function fn26(arg)
		if not arg then
			return
		end

		if _G.UnwalkEnabled ~= true then
			return
		end

		if fn25() then
			return
		end
		local humanoid = arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local animator = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
		local animate = arg:FindFirstChild("Animate")

		if animate then
			animate.Disabled = true
		end

		if animator then
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
			end)

			if ok and result then
				for _, v7 in ipairs(result) do
					pcall(function()
						v7:Stop(0)
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function setDisableAnimations(arg)
		local unwalkEnabled = arg and true or false
		_G.UnwalkEnabled = unwalkEnabled
		local character = localPlayer3.Character

		if unwalkEnabled then
			if character then
				pcall(fn26, character)
			end
		else
			pcall(function()
				local animate = character and character:FindFirstChild("Animate")

				if animate then
					animate.Disabled = false
				end

				local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if humanoid then
					humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	_G.setDisableAnimations = setDisableAnimations
	_G.setUnwalk = setDisableAnimations

	local function fn27(character)
		task.spawn(function()
			character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
			task.wait(0.05)

			for i = 1, 8 do
				if localPlayer3.Character == character then
					fn26(character)
					task.wait(0.25)
					continue
				end

				break
			end
		end)
	end

	if localPlayer3.Character then
		fn27(localPlayer3.Character)
	end

	localPlayer3.CharacterAdded:Connect(fn27)

	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait(1)
		if _G.UnwalkEnabled == true then
			return
		end
		local character = localPlayer3.Character
		local animate = character and character:FindFirstChild("Animate")

		if animate and animate.Disabled then
			pcall(function()
				animate.Disabled = false
			end)
		end
	end)

	local n16 = 0

	RunService2.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if _G.UnwalkEnabled ~= true then
			return
		end
		local now = os.clock()
		if now - n16 < 1.5 then
			return
		end
		n16 = now

		if localPlayer3.Character then
			fn26(localPlayer3.Character)
		end
	end)
end

local executeBrainrotTP

executeBrainrotTP = function(arg)
	if isTeleporting then
		return
	end

	if arg then
		pcall(function()
			_armTPSync(arg)
		end)
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		pcall(function()
			doVelocityTP(true)
		end)
	end)
end

_G.executeBrainrotTP = executeBrainrotTP

_G.isTPExecuting = function()
	return isTeleporting
end

local fn25 = nil

fn25 = function()
	local ok, result = pcall(scanForTP)
	if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" then
		return
	end

	for _, child in ipairs(ScrollingFrame:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Frame") then
			child:Destroy()
		end
	end

	if #result == 0 then
		return
	end

	for i, v7 in ipairs(result) do
		if not (i > 15) then
			local n16 = tonumber(v7.mps) or 0
			local str = n16 >= 1e12 and string.format("$%.1fT/s", n16 / 1e12) or n16 >= 1e9 and string.format("$%.1fB/s", n16 / 1e9)
			local str2

			if str then
				str2 = str
			else
				str2 = n16 >= 1000000 and string.format("$%.1fM/s", n16 / 1000000)
			end

			str2 = str2 or n16 >= 1000 and string.format("$%.1fK/s", n16 / 1000) or string.format("$%d/s", math.floor(n16))
			local v8 = _petUid(v7)
			local flag3 = _G.StealTargetUID == v8
			local flag4 = _G.ActiveStealUid ~= nil and _G.ActiveStealUid == v8
			local autoTP = flag3 or flag4 or not _G.StealTargetUID and i == 1 and _G.AutoTP
			local v9 = fn6

			local tbl5 = {
				Parent = ScrollingFrame,
				Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 36),
				BackgroundColor3 = autoTP and Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 28) or tbl2.CardBg,
			}

			do
				local values = table.pack(fn6("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 0) }))
				table.move(values, 1, values.n, 1, tbl5)
			end

			local Frame = v9("Frame", tbl5)
			local uiStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", Frame)

			if flag4 or flag3 then
				uiStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(85, 85, 100)
				uiStroke.Thickness = 1.5
			else
				uiStroke.Color = tbl2.CardBorder
				uiStroke.Thickness = 1
			end

			local Frame2 = fn6("Frame", {
				Parent = Frame,
				Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 22),
				Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -11),
				BackgroundColor3 = autoTP and Color3.fromRGB(85, 85, 100) or Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 60),
				BorderSizePixel = 0,
			})

			fn6("TextLabel", {
				Parent = Frame,
				Text = v7.name == "__PLACEHOLDER__" and "Scanning..." or v7.name,
				Font = tbl2.FontMain,
				TextSize = 11,
				TextColor3 = autoTP and Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 235) or tbl2.TextMain,
				Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0),
				Size = UDim2.new(0.65, 0, 1, 0),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				TextXAlignment = 0,
				TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
			})

			fn6("TextLabel", {
				Parent = Frame,
				Text = str2,
				Font = tbl2.FontMain,
				TextSize = 10,
				TextColor3 = autoTP and Color3.fromRGB(160, 200, 160) or Color3.fromRGB(130, 170, 130),
				Position = UDim2.new(0.65, 0, 0, 0),
				Size = UDim2.new(0.33, 0, 1, 0),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				TextXAlignment = 2,
			})

			fn6("TextButton", { Parent = Frame, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "" }).MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				if v2 and v2 ~= Frame then
					fn16(v2, { 0.15 }, { BackgroundColor3 = tbl2.CardBg })

					pcall(function()
						local uiStroke2 = v2:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")

						if uiStroke2 then
							fn7(uiStroke2)
							uiStroke2.Thickness = 1
							uiStroke2.Color = tbl2.CardBorder
						end
					end)
				end

				v2 = Frame
				_G.SelectedPet = v7
				_G.StealTargetUID = v8
				_G.StealTarget = v7
				_G.TPSyncActive = true
				_G.ActiveStealUid = v8
				_G.StealMode = "manual"

				if setPriorityState then
					pcall(setPriorityState, false, false)
				end

				if setNearestState then
					pcall(setNearestState, false, false)
				end

				fn16(Frame, { 0.15 }, { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 28) })
				uiStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(85, 85, 100)
				uiStroke.Thickness = 1.5
				Frame2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(85, 85, 100)

				task.spawn(function()
					if type(_G.StealForceRepick) == "function" then
						pcall(_G.StealForceRepick)
					end

					if type(_G.isTPExecuting) == "function" and _G.isTPExecuting() then
						_G.TPStop = true
						local now = os.clock()

						while _G.isTPExecuting() and os.clock() - now < 0.75 do
							task.wait(0.03)
						end
					end

					_G.TPStop = false
					_G.StealTargetUID = v8
					_G.StealTarget = v7
					_G.TPSyncActive = true

					if _G.RetargetOnSelect ~= false then
						executeBrainrotTP(v7)
					end

					pcall(fn25)
				end)
			end)

			continue
		end

		break
	end
end

task.spawn(function()
	local n16 = 0
	local v7 = nil
	local v8 = nil

	while true do
		pcall(fn25)

		if n16 < 6 then
			n16 += 1
			task.wait(0.25)
		else
			local now = os.clock()

			repeat
				task.wait(0.1)
				local activeStealUid = _G.ActiveStealUid
				local stealTargetUID = _G.StealTargetUID

				if activeStealUid ~= v7 or stealTargetUID ~= v8 then
					v7 = activeStealUid
					v8 = stealTargetUID
					break
				end
			until os.clock() - now >= 1
		end
	end
end)

local function fn26()
	local ryHubSession = _G.RyHubSession

	local function fn27()
		return _G.RyHubSession ~= ryHubSession
	end

	local function fn28(arg, arg2, arg3)
		_G.RyLog("invis", arg, arg2, arg3)
	end

	local function fn29(arg, arg2, arg3)
		_G.RyWarn("invis", arg, arg2, arg3)
	end

	local toggles = tbl.Toggles or {}
	local sliders = tbl.Sliders or {}
	_G.RyInvisAuto = toggles.Invis_AutoInvis == true

	if type(toggles.Invis_RyAutoRecover) == "boolean" then
		_G.RyInvisAutoRecover = toggles.Invis_RyAutoRecover
	elseif _G.RyInvisAutoRecover == nil then
		_G.RyInvisAutoRecover = true
	end

	if type(sliders.Invis_RyAngle) == "number" then
		_G.RyInvisAngle = sliders.Invis_RyAngle
	end

	if type(sliders.Invis_RyDepth) == "number" then
		_G.RyInvisDepth = math.clamp(sliders.Invis_RyDepth, 0, 10)
	end

	local flag3 = false
	local tbl5 = {}
	local v7 = nil
	local v8 = nil
	local v9 = nil
	local hipHeight = nil
	local tbl6 = nil
	local tbl7 = nil
	local v10 = nil
	local v11 = nil
	local flag4 = false
	local flag5 = false
	local n16 = 0
	local n17 = 0
	local tbl8 = {}
	local flag6 = false
	local flag7 = false
	local tbl9 = {}

	local function fn30()
		return 0.01 + math.clamp(tonumber(_G.RyInvisDepth) or 4.2, 0, 10) / 10 * 0.19
	end

	local function fn31()
		return math.clamp(tonumber(_G.RyInvisAngle) or 180, 0, 360)
	end

	local function fn32(arg)
		tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = arg
	end

	local function fn33(arg, arg2, part0)
		if not (arg and arg2 and part0) then
			return
		end

		for _, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("Weld") or descendant:IsA("Motor6D") then
				if descendant.Part0 == arg2 then
					descendant.Part0 = part0
				end

				if descendant.Part1 == arg2 then
					descendant.Part1 = part0
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function fn34()
		if _G.RyInvisStealRepaint then
			pcall(_G.RyInvisStealRepaint, flag3)
		end
	end

	local function fn35(arg, arg2)
		local model = Instance.new("Model")

		pcall(function()
			model.Parent = game
		end)

		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			arg.Parent = model
			arg2()
		end)

		if arg.Parent ~= workspace then
			pcall(function()
				arg.Parent = workspace
			end)
		end

		pcall(function()
			model:Destroy()
		end)

		return ok, result
	end

	local function fn36(arg)
		local n18 = 0

		for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" and child:IsA("BasePart") then
				n18 += 1
			end
		end

		return n18
	end

	local function fn37()
		local v12 = v10
		local v13 = v11
		local v14 = v7
		local v15 = v8
		local flag8 = v12 ~= nil and localPlayer2.Character == v12 and v12.Parent ~= nil

		if flag8 and v14 and v14:IsDescendantOf(game) then
			local cFrame = v15 and v15.Parent == v12 and v15.CFrame or nil

			local v16, v17 = fn35(v12, function()
				v14.Parent = v12
				v12.PrimaryPart = v14
			end)

			pcall(function()
				v14.CanCollide = true
			end)

			pcall(fn33, v12, v15, v14)

			if v15 and v15 ~= v14 then
				pcall(function()
					v15:Destroy()
				end)
			end

			if cFrame then
				pcall(function()
					v14.CFrame = cFrame
				end)
			end

			if v13 and hipHeight then
				pcall(function()
					v13.HipHeight = hipHeight
				end)
			end

			if not v16 then
				fn29("restore hit an error (" .. tostring(v17) .. ") -- character put back in the workspace anyway")
			end

			return true
		end

		if flag8 then
			fn29("the real HumanoidRootPart was removed while invisible -- nothing to restore (stand-in root kept so the body stays in one piece)")
			return false
		end

		if v14 and v14.Parent then
			pcall(function()
				v14:Destroy()
			end)
		end

		if v15 and v15.Parent then
			pcall(function()
				v15:Destroy()
			end)
		end

		return false
	end

	local function fn38(arg)
		n16 += 1
		local v12 = flag3
		flag3 = false
		flag4 = false
		flag6 = false

		for _, v13 in ipairs(tbl5) do
			pcall(function()
				v13:Disconnect()
			end)
		end

		tbl5 = {}

		if tbl6 then
			for k, v13 in pairs(tbl6) do
				if k and k.Parent then
					pcall(function()
						k.LocalTransparencyModifier = v13
					end)
				end
			end

			tbl6 = nil
		end

		if v9 then
			local v13 = v9
			v9 = nil

			pcall(function()
				v13:AdjustSpeed(1)
				v13:Stop(0)
				v13:Destroy()
			end)
		end

		if v7 or v8 then
			pcall(fn37)
		end

		if tbl7 and v11 and v11.Parent then
			for k, v13 in pairs(tbl7) do
				pcall(function()
					v11:SetStateEnabled(k, v13)
				end)
			end
		end

		tbl7 = nil
		v7 = nil
		v8 = nil
		v10 = nil
		v11 = nil
		_G.RyInvisActive = false

		if v12 then
			fn28("OFF (" .. tostring(arg or "stop") .. ")", nil, 0)
		end

		fn34()
	end

	local fn39 = nil

	fn39 = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
		if not (arg and arg2 and arg3 and arg2.Health > 0) then
			return false, "no humanoid / animator"
		end
		local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
		animation.AnimationId = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=18537363391"

		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return arg3:LoadAnimation(animation)
		end)

		animation:Destroy()
		if not ok or not result then
			return false, tostring(result)
		end
		v9 = result

		pcall(function()
			result.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
			result.Looped = true
			result:Play(0, 1, 0)
		end)

		fn32(result.Stopped:Connect(function()
			if not (flag3 and v9 == result and v10 == arg) then
				return
			end
			local now = os.clock()

			for i = #tbl9, 1, -1 do
				if now - tbl9[i] > 1 then
					table.remove(tbl9, i)
				end
			end

			if #tbl9 >= 8 then
				fn29("the invis pose keeps being stopped by something else (8x in 1s)", "replay", 3)
				return
			end
			tbl9[#tbl9 + 1] = now

			task.defer(function()
				if flag3 and v9 == result and v10 == arg then
					pcall(fn39, arg, arg2, arg3)
				end
			end)
		end))

		task.defer(function()
			if v9 ~= result then
				return
			end

			pcall(function()
				result.TimePosition = 0.7
			end)

			task.delay(0.1, function()
				if v9 == result then
					pcall(function()
						result:AdjustSpeed(math.huge)
					end)
				end
			end)
		end)

		return true
	end

	local function fn40(arg)
		if not arg then
			return
		end

		for _, v12 in ipairs({ "DoubleRig", "Constraints" }) do
			local v13 = arg:FindFirstChild(v12)

			if v13 then
				pcall(function()
					v13:Destroy()
				end)
			end
		end

		fn32(arg.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if flag3 and (child.Name == "DoubleRig" or child.Name == "Constraints") then
				pcall(function()
					child:Destroy()
				end)
			end
		end))
	end

	local fn41 = nil

	local function fn42(arg)
		if fn27() then
			return false, "a newer run of the hub owns invis"
		end

		if flag3 then
			return true
		end

		if _G.RyTPActive == true and _G.RyInvisDuringTP ~= true then
			return false, "a teleport is in the air", true
		end

		if _G.RyAltInvisActive == true or _G.invisibleStealEnabled == true then
			return false, "another invis engine already has the root parked"
		end
		local character = localPlayer2.Character
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not (character and humanoid) then
			return false, "no character yet", true
		end

		if character.Parent ~= workspace then
			return false, "the character is not in the workspace", true
		end

		if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
			return false, "the character is dead", true
		end
		local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not (humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart:IsA("BasePart")) then
			return false, "no HumanoidRootPart yet", true
		end
		local v12 = fn36(character)
		if v12 > 1 then
			return false, string.format("the character has %d HumanoidRootParts (an old swap was never undone)", v12)
		end
		local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
		if not animator then
			return false, "the humanoid has no Animator yet (character still loading)", true
		end
		local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not currentCamera then
			return false, "no camera yet", true
		end

		if _G.RyStripAC then
			pcall(_G.RyStripAC, humanoidRootPart)
		end

		local tbl10 = {}

		for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == localPlayer2.Name and child ~= character then
				tbl10[#tbl10 + 1] = child
			end
		end

		if #tbl10 == 0 then
			tbl10[1] = character
		end

		for _, v13 in ipairs(tbl10) do
			fn40(v13)
		end

		fn32(workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if flag3 and child.Name == localPlayer2.Name and child ~= character then
				fn40(child)
			end
		end))

		tbl7 = {}

		for _, v13 in ipairs({ "Dead", "FallingDown", "Ragdoll" }) do
			local v14 = Enum.HumanoidStateType[v13]

			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return humanoid:GetStateEnabled(v14)
			end)

			tbl7[v14] = not ok or result == true

			pcall(function()
				humanoid:SetStateEnabled(v14, false)
			end)
		end

		hipHeight = humanoid.HipHeight
		local clone = nil

		local v13, v14 = fn35(character, function()
			clone = humanoidRootPart:Clone()
			clone.Parent = character
			humanoidRootPart.Parent = currentCamera
			clone.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
			character.PrimaryPart = clone
		end)

		if not (v13 and clone and clone.Parent == character and humanoidRootPart.Parent == currentCamera) then
			if humanoidRootPart.Parent ~= character then
				fn35(character, function()
					humanoidRootPart.Parent = character
					character.PrimaryPart = humanoidRootPart
				end)
			end

			if clone and clone ~= humanoidRootPart then
				pcall(function()
					clone:Destroy()
				end)
			end

			for _, v15 in ipairs(tbl5) do
				pcall(function()
					v15:Disconnect()
				end)
			end

			tbl5 = {}

			for k, v15 in pairs(tbl7) do
				pcall(function()
					humanoid:SetStateEnabled(k, v15)
				end)
			end

			tbl7 = nil
			return false, "root swap failed (" .. tostring(v14) .. ") -- rolled back"
		end

		pcall(fn33, character, humanoidRootPart, clone)
		v7 = humanoidRootPart
		v8 = clone
		v10 = character
		v11 = humanoid
		flag3 = true
		flag4 = false
		_G.RyInvisActive = true

		if _G.RyStripAC then
			pcall(_G.RyStripAC, humanoidRootPart)
		end

		local position = nil
		local vector = Vector3.zero
		local n18 = 0
		local v15 = nil
		local n19 = 60

		fn32(RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function(deltaTime)
			if not flag3 or v10 ~= character then
				return
			end
			local v16 = v7
			if not v16 then
				return
			end

			if not humanoid.Parent or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
				return
			end
			local primaryPart = character.PrimaryPart or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not primaryPart or primaryPart == v16 then
				return
			end
			local n20 = tonumber(deltaTime) or 0.016666666666666666

			if n19 > 0 then
				n19 -= 1
			elseif not flag4 and position then
				local position2 = v16.Position
				local n21 = 0.5 * workspace.Gravity * n18 * n18
				local n22 = math.min((position2 - position).Magnitude, (position2 - position + vector * n18).Magnitude)
				local n23 = 0

				if v15 then
					local position3 = primaryPart.Position
					n23 = math.min((position3 - v15).Magnitude, (position3 - v15 + primaryPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity * n18).Magnitude)
				end

				local flag8 = n22 > (tonumber(_G.RyInvisDriftTol) or 2.25) + n21
				local flag9

				if flag8 then
					flag9 = flag8
				else
					flag9 = n23 > (tonumber(_G.RyInvisJumpTol) or 8)
				end

				if flag9 then
					flag4 = true
					task.spawn(fn41, n22, n23)
				end
			end

			local n21 = primaryPart.CFrame - Vector3.new(0, humanoid.HipHeight + primaryPart.Size.Y / 2 - 1 + fn30(), 0)
			local assemblyLinearVelocity = primaryPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity

			if not pcall(function()
				v16.CFrame = n21 * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(fn31()), 0, 0)
				v16.AssemblyLinearVelocity = assemblyLinearVelocity
				v16.CanCollide = false
				local currentCamera2 = workspace.CurrentCamera

				if currentCamera2 and v16.Parent ~= currentCamera2 then
					v16.Parent = currentCamera2
				end
			end) and not v16:IsDescendantOf(game) then
				task.spawn(function()
					if flag3 and v7 == v16 then
						fn38("real root lost")
					end
				end)

				return
			end

			local position2 = primaryPart.Position
			position = n21.Position
			vector = assemblyLinearVelocity
			n18 = n20
			v15 = position2
		end))

		local now = os.clock()

		fn32(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not flag3 or not humanoid.Parent then
				return
			end

			pcall(function()
				humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
				humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
				local state = humanoid:GetState()

				if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll then
					humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
				end
			end)

			local now2 = os.clock()

			if now2 - now >= 1 then
				now = now2

				if _G.RyStripAC then
					pcall(_G.RyStripAC, v7)
				end
			end
		end))

		local v16, v17 = fn39(character, humanoid, animator)
		if not v16 then
			fn38("pose failed")
			return false, "the invis pose did not load (" .. tostring(v17) .. ")"
		end

		if _G.RyInvisHideLocal ~= false and _G.OptiKeepAvatarVisible ~= true then
			tbl6 = {}

			for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
					tbl6[descendant] = descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier
					descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
				end
			end

			fn32(character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
				if not (flag3 and tbl6 and descendant:IsA("BasePart")) then
					return
				end

				if tbl6[descendant] == nil then
					tbl6[descendant] = descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier
				end

				pcall(function()
					descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
				end)
			end))
		end

		fn28("ON (" .. tostring(arg or "manual") .. ")", nil, 0)
		fn34()
		return true
	end

	fn41 = function(arg, arg2)
		if fn27() then
			return
		end
		local v12 = flag6
		local v13 = flag7

		if _G.RyInvisAutoRecover == false then
			fn29(string.format("the server pulled the real root back (drift %.1f, jump %.1f) -- Auto Recover is off, stopping", arg, arg2))
			fn38("lagback")
			return
		end

		if flag5 then
			return
		end
		flag5 = true
		n17 += 1

		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			local now = os.clock()

			for i = #tbl8, 1, -1 do
				if now - tbl8[i] > 10 then
					table.remove(tbl8, i)
				end
			end

			tbl8[#tbl8 + 1] = now
			local n18 = #tbl8
			local n19 = n18 <= 2 and 0.5 or math.min(0.5 * 2 ^ (n18 - 2), 3)

			if n18 >= 3 then
				fn29(string.format("%d lagbacks in 10s -- the server keeps pulling the real root back; re-engaging in %.1fs", n18, n19), "backoff", 1)
			else
				fn28(string.format("lagback (drift %.1f, jump %.1f) -> restarting", arg, arg2), "drift", 0.5)
			end

			fn38("lagback")
			local v14 = n16
			task.wait(n19)
			if v14 ~= n16 or fn27() or flag3 then
				return
			end

			if v12 then
				if not (_G.RyInvisAuto == true and localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true) then
					return
				end
			elseif not v13 then
				return
			end

			if _G.RyTPActive == true and _G.RyInvisDuringTP ~= true then
				return
			end
			local v15, v16 = fn42(v12 and "auto recover" or "recover")

			if not v15 then
				task.wait(1)
				if v14 ~= n16 or fn27() or flag3 then
					return
				end
				v15, v16 = fn42(v12 and "auto recover" or "recover")
			end

			if v15 then
				flag6 = v12
			else
				fn29("could not re-engage after a lagback: " .. tostring(v16))
			end
		end)

		if n17 == n17 then
			flag5 = false
		end

		if not ok then
			fn29("auto recover errored: " .. tostring(result))
		end
	end

	local function fn43(arg, arg2)
		local now = os.clock()
		local v12, v13, flag8 = fn42(arg)

		while true do
			flag8 = not v12 and flag8

			if flag8 then
				flag8 = os.clock() - now < (arg2 or 3)
			end

			if flag8 then
				task.wait(0.2)
				if fn27() then
					return false, "superseded"
				end

				if flag3 then
					return true
				end
				v12, v13, flag8 = fn42(arg)
				continue
			end

			break
		end

		return v12, v13
	end

	_G.RyInvisStart = function()
		local api, v12 = fn42("api")

		if api then
			flag7 = true
		else
			fn29("start refused: " .. tostring(v12), "refused", 1)
		end

		return api
	end

	_G.RyInvisStop = function(arg)
		if arg == nil then
			arg = _G.RyTPActive == true and "tp" or "external"
		end

		if arg ~= "tp" then
			flag7 = false
		end

		fn38(arg)
	end

	_G.RyInvisToggle = function()
		if flag3 then
			flag7 = false
			fn38("toggle")
			return
		end

		n16 += 1
		n17 += 1
		flag5 = false
		flag7 = true

		task.spawn(function()
			local manual, v12 = fn43("manual", 3)

			if not manual then
				if _G.RyTPActive == true and _G.RyInvisDuringTP ~= true then
					fn28("switching on as soon as the teleport lands")
				else
					flag7 = false
					fn29("could not switch on: " .. tostring(v12))
				end
			end

			fn34()
		end)
	end

	local n18 = 0

	local function fn44()
		return _G.RyInvisAuto == true and localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
	end

	local function fn45()
		if fn27() then
			return
		end
		n18 += 1
		local v12 = n18

		if not fn44() then
			if flag3 and flag6 then
				task.delay(tonumber(_G.RyInvisStealGrace) or 0.4, function()
					if v12 ~= n18 or fn27() then
						return
					end

					if flag3 and flag6 and not fn44() then
						fn38("steal ended")
					end
				end)
			end

			return
		end

		if flag3 then
			return
		end

		task.delay(tonumber(_G.RyInvisAutoDelay) or 1.5, function()
			if v12 ~= n18 or fn27() or flag3 or not fn44() then
				return
			end
			local now = os.clock()

			while _G.RyTPActive == true and _G.RyInvisDuringTP ~= true and os.clock() - now < 15 do
				task.wait(0.1)
				if v12 ~= n18 or fn27() or flag3 then
					return
				end
			end

			if not fn44() then
				return
			end
			local auto, v13 = fn43("auto", 3)

			if auto then
				flag6 = true
			elseif v12 == n18 and not flag3 then
				fn29("Auto Invis could not switch on: " .. tostring(v13))
			end
		end)
	end

	_G.RyInvisSync = function()
		return fn45()
	end

	localPlayer2:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
		if not fn27() then
			fn45()
		end
	end)

	localPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		if fn27() then
			return
		end
		local flag8 = flag3 or v7 ~= nil or v8 ~= nil or flag5
		flag7 = false
		n16 += 1
		n17 += 1
		flag5 = false
		local v12 = flag3
		local v13

		if flag3 then
			v13 = v12
		else
			v13 = v7
		end

		if v13 or v8 then
			fn38("respawn")
		end

		if flag8 then
			fn28("respawned while invisible -> old root swap cleaned up; invis is off on the new character", nil, 0)
		end

		task.delay(1, function()
			if not fn27() then
				pcall(fn45)
			end
		end)
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		local flag8 = _G.RyTPActive == true

		while not fn27() do
			task.wait(0.1)
			local flag9 = _G.RyTPActive == true
			flag8 = flag8 and not flag9

			if flag8 then
				if flag7 and not flag3 then
					task.spawn(function()
						local now = os.clock()

						while _G.IsStealHoldActive and _G.IsStealHoldActive() and os.clock() - now < 4 do
							task.wait(0.1)
						end

						if flag7 and not flag3 and not fn27() then
							local v12, v13 = fn43("resume after TP", 3)

							if not v12 then
								fn29("could not re-engage after the teleport: " .. tostring(v13))
							end
						end
					end)
				end

				pcall(fn45)
				flag8 = flag9
			else
				flag8 = flag9
			end
		end
	end)

	task.defer(function()
		if not fn27() then
			pcall(fn45)
		end
	end)

	fn28(string.format("engine ready (auto=%s recover=%s angle=%s depth=%s)", tostring(_G.RyInvisAuto), tostring(_G.RyInvisAutoRecover ~= false), tostring(_G.RyInvisAngle or 180), tostring(_G.RyInvisDepth or 4.2)), nil, 0)
end

fn26()

local function fn27()
	local v7 = getinfo
	local getinfo_

	if v7 then
		getinfo_ = v7
	else
		getinfo_ = debug and debug.getinfo
	end

	local ryHubSession = _G.RyHubSession
	local flag3 = false

	local function fn28(arg)
		if not (arg and arg:IsA("BasePart")) then
			return 0
		end
		local n16 = 0

		for _, v8 in ipairs({ "CFrame", "Position" }) do
			pcall(function()
				for _, v9 in ipairs(getconnections(arg:GetPropertyChangedSignal(v8))) do
					if v9.Function and v9.Enabled then
						local ok, result = pcall(getinfo_, v9.Function)

						if ok and result and tostring(result.source) == "=ReplicatedFirst.test" then
							pcall(function()
								v9:Disable()
							end)

							n16 += 1
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end

		if n16 > 0 then
			_G.RyStripACHits = (tonumber(_G.RyStripACHits) or 0) + n16
		end

		return n16
	end

	local function ryStripAC(arg)
		if type(getconnections) ~= "function" or type(getinfo_) ~= "function" then
			if not flag3 then
				flag3 = true
				_G.RyWarn("invis", "anti-cheat stripper unavailable (executor has no getconnections/getinfo) -- the game's client AC can see invis root writes")
			end

			return 0
		end

		local n16 = 0
		local tbl5 = {}

		local function fn29(arg2)
			if arg2 and not tbl5[arg2] then
				tbl5[arg2] = true
				n16 += fn28(arg2)
			end
		end

		local character = localPlayer2.Character
		fn29(character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))

		if typeof(arg) == "Instance" then
			fn29(arg)
		end

		local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

		if currentCamera then
			for _, child in ipairs(currentCamera:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" and child:IsA("BasePart") then
					fn29(child)
				end
			end
		end

		return n16
	end

	_G.RyStripAC = ryStripAC

	task.spawn(function()
		local n16 = 0

		while _G.RyHubSession == ryHubSession do
			if (_G.RyInvisActive == true and 1 or 6) <= os.clock() - n16 then
				n16 = os.clock()
				pcall(ryStripAC)
			end

			task.wait(0.5)
		end
	end)

	localPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		if _G.RyHubSession ~= ryHubSession then
			return
		end

		for _, v8 in ipairs({ 0.3, 1.5, 4 }) do
			task.delay(v8, function()
				pcall(ryStripAC)
			end)
		end
	end)
end

fn27()

if _G.RyAltInvisDepth == nil then
	_G.RyAltInvisDepth = 4.2
end

if _G.RyAltInvisAngle == nil then
	_G.RyAltInvisAngle = 225
end

local function fn28()
	local flag3 = false
	local humanoidRootPart = nil
	local clone = nil
	local hipHeight = nil
	local tbl5 = {}
	local connection = nil
	local tbl6 = {}
	local n16 = 0
	local n17 = 0
	local n18 = 0

	local function fn29()
		n18 = 0
		n17 = 0
	end

	local function fn30()
		local v7 = workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer2.Name)
		if not v7 then
			return
		end
		local doubleRig = v7:FindFirstChild("DoubleRig")

		if doubleRig then
			doubleRig:Destroy()
		end

		local constraints = v7:FindFirstChild("Constraints")

		if constraints then
			constraints:Destroy()
		end

		local connection2 = v7.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child.Name == "DoubleRig" then
				task.defer(function()
					pcall(function()
						child:Destroy()
					end)
				end)
			elseif child.Name == "Constraints" then
				child:Destroy()
			end
		end)

		table.insert(tbl6, connection2)
	end

	local tbl7 = {
		LowerTorso = { "Root", "HumanoidRootPart" },
		UpperTorso = { "Waist", "LowerTorso" },
		Head = { "Neck", "UpperTorso" },
		LeftUpperArm = { "LeftShoulder", "UpperTorso" },
		LeftLowerArm = { "LeftElbow", "LeftUpperArm" },
		LeftHand = { "LeftWrist", "LeftLowerArm" },
		RightUpperArm = { "RightShoulder", "UpperTorso" },
		RightLowerArm = { "RightElbow", "RightUpperArm" },
		RightHand = { "RightWrist", "RightLowerArm" },
		LeftUpperLeg = { "LeftHip", "LowerTorso" },
		LeftLowerLeg = { "LeftKnee", "LeftUpperLeg" },
		LeftFoot = { "LeftAnkle", "LeftLowerLeg" },
		RightUpperLeg = { "RightHip", "LowerTorso" },
		RightLowerLeg = { "RightKnee", "RightUpperLeg" },
		RightFoot = { "RightAnkle", "RightLowerLeg" },
	}

	local flag4 = false

	local function fn31()
		local character = localPlayer2.Character
		if not character then
			return
		end
		local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
			return
		end

		if humanoid.RigType ~= Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
			return
		end
		local n19 = 0

		for k, v7 in pairs(tbl7) do
			local v8 = v7[1]
			local v9 = v7[2]
			local v10 = character:FindFirstChild(k)
			local v11 = character:FindFirstChild(v9)

			if v10 and v11 then
				local v12 = nil
				local v13 = nil

				for _, child in ipairs(v10:GetChildren()) do
					if child.Name == v8 then
						if child:IsA("Motor6D") then
							v13 = child
						elseif child:IsA("AnimationConstraint") then
							v12 = child
						end
					end
				end

				if not v13 then
					local v14 = v11:FindFirstChild(v8 .. "RigAttachment")
					local v15 = v10:FindFirstChild(v8 .. "RigAttachment")

					if v14 and v15 then
						if v12 then
							pcall(function()
								v12.Enabled = false
							end)
						end

						pcall(function()
							local motor6D = Instance.new("Motor6D")
							motor6D.Name = v8
							motor6D.Part0 = v11
							motor6D.Part1 = v10
							motor6D.C0 = v14.CFrame
							motor6D.C1 = v15.CFrame
							motor6D.Parent = v10
							n19 += 1
						end)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if n19 > 0 and _G.RyAltRigLog then
			fn11(("[rig] rebuilt %d joint(s)"):format(n19))
		end
	end

	local function fn32()
		if flag4 or _G.RyAltRigFix == false then
			return false
		end
		flag4 = true
		local ok = pcall(fn31)
		flag4 = false
		return ok
	end

	_G.RyAltRebuildRig = function()
		return fn32()
	end

	local function fn33()
		local character = localPlayer2.Character

		if character and character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
			hipHeight = character.Humanoid.HipHeight
			humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoidRootPart.Parent then
				return false
			end

			for _, child in pairs(humanoidRootPart:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Attachment") and (child.Name:find("Beam") or child.Name:find("Attach")) then
					child:Destroy()
				end
			end

			for _, child in pairs(humanoidRootPart:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Beam") then
					child:Destroy()
				end
			end

			local model = Instance.new("Model")
			model.Parent = game
			character.Parent = model
			clone = humanoidRootPart:Clone()
			clone.Parent = character
			humanoidRootPart.Parent = workspace.CurrentCamera
			clone.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
			character.PrimaryPart = clone
			character.Parent = workspace

			for _, descendant in pairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("Weld") or descendant:IsA("Motor6D") then
					if descendant.Part0 == humanoidRootPart then
						descendant.Part0 = clone
					end

					if descendant.Part1 == humanoidRootPart then
						descendant.Part1 = clone
					end
				end
			end

			model:Destroy()
			task.defer(fn32)
			return true
		end

		return false
	end

	local function fn34()
		local character = localPlayer2.Character
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoidRootPart:IsDescendantOf(workspace) or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
			return
		end
		local model = Instance.new("Model")
		model.Parent = game
		character.Parent = model
		humanoidRootPart.Parent = character
		character.PrimaryPart = humanoidRootPart
		character.Parent = workspace
		humanoidRootPart.CanCollide = true

		for _, descendant in pairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("Weld") or descendant:IsA("Motor6D") then
				if descendant.Part0 == clone then
					descendant.Part0 = humanoidRootPart
				end

				if descendant.Part1 == clone then
					descendant.Part1 = humanoidRootPart
				end
			end
		end

		if clone then
			local cFrame = clone.CFrame
			clone:Destroy()
			clone = nil
			humanoidRootPart.CFrame = cFrame
		end

		humanoidRootPart = nil

		if humanoid and hipHeight then
			humanoid.HipHeight = hipHeight
		end

		task.defer(fn32)
		fn29()
	end

	local fn35 = nil

	fn35 = function()
		local character = localPlayer2.Character

		if character and character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
			local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
			animation.AnimationId = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=18537363391"
			local humanoid = character.Humanoid
			local v7 = (humanoid:FindFirstChild("Animator") or Instance.new("Animator", humanoid)):LoadAnimation(animation)
			v7.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
			v7:Play(0, 1, 0)
			animation:Destroy()
			table.insert(tbl5, v7)

			v7.Stopped:Connect(function()
				if flag3 then
					fn35()
				end
			end)

			task.delay(0, function()
				v7.TimePosition = 0.7

				task.delay(0.3, function()
					if v7 then
						v7:AdjustSpeed(math.huge)
					end
				end)
			end)
		end
	end

	local function ryAltInvisStop()
		fn29()
		if not flag3 and not (humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Parent == workspace.CurrentCamera) then
			return
		end
		local character = localPlayer2.Character
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		flag3 = false
		_G.RyAltInvisActive = false

		for _, v7 in pairs(tbl5) do
			pcall(function()
				v7:Stop(0)
			end)
		end

		tbl5 = {}

		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil
		end

		for _, v7 in ipairs(tbl6) do
			if v7 then
				v7:Disconnect()
			end
		end

		tbl6 = {}
		fn34()
		fn29()

		if humanoid then
			pcall(function()
				local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")

				if animator then
					for _, v7 in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
						if v7.Priority == Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4 or v7.Priority == Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3 then
							v7:Stop(0)
						end
					end
				end

				humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)

				task.defer(function()
					if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
						humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
					end
				end)
			end)
		end

		n16 = tick()

		if _G.RyInvisStealRepaint then
			pcall(_G.RyInvisStealRepaint, false)
		end
	end

	local function ryAltInvisStart()
		if (_G.InvisEngine or "ry") ~= "ryalt" then
			return
		end

		if flag3 then
			return
		end
		local character = localPlayer2.Character
		if not character then
			return
		end

		if not character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
			return
		end
		flag3 = true
		_G.RyAltInvisActive = true

		if _G.RyInvisStealRepaint then
			pcall(_G.RyInvisStealRepaint, true)
		end

		tbl5 = {}
		fn30()

		if fn33() then
			task.wait(0.05)
			fn35()
			local position = nil
			local n19 = 5

			connection = RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function()
				if character and character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and character.Humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoidRootPart then
					local primaryPart = character.PrimaryPart or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if primaryPart then
						if n19 > 0 then
							n19 -= 1
							position = nil
						elseif position then
							if (humanoidRootPart.Position - position).Magnitude > 6 and not _G.RecoveryInProgress and localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
								position = nil

								if _G.RyAltInvisHardRecover == true and _G._forceInvisToggle then
									_G.RecoveryInProgress = true

									task.spawn(function()
										pcall(_G._forceInvisToggle)
										task.wait(0.6)

										if localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
											pcall(_G._forceInvisToggle)
										end

										_G.RecoveryInProgress = false
									end)
								end
							end
						end

						if clone then
							clone.CanCollide = true
						end

						if humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Parent then
							for _, child in pairs(humanoidRootPart:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("Attachment") or child:IsA("Beam") then
									child:Destroy()
								end
							end

							local n20 = (tonumber(_G.RyAltInvisDepth) or 4.2) * 0.5
							local n21 = tonumber(_G.RyAltInvisAngle) or 225
							local cframe = CFrame.Angles
							humanoidRootPart.CFrame = (primaryPart.CFrame - Vector3.new(0, n20, 0)) * cframe(math.rad(n21), 0, 0)
							humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = primaryPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
							humanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
							position = humanoidRootPart.Position
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		else
			flag3 = false
			_G.RyAltInvisActive = false
		end
	end

	_G.RyAltInvisStart = ryAltInvisStart
	_G.RyAltInvisStop = ryAltInvisStop

	_G.RyAltForceUninvis = function()
		pcall(ryAltInvisStop)

		pcall(function()
			local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
			local character = localPlayer2.Character
			if not currentCamera or not character then
				return
			end
			local v7 = nil

			for _, child in ipairs(currentCamera:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then
					v7 = child
					break
				end
			end

			if v7 then
				local primaryPart = character.PrimaryPart or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if primaryPart and v7 ~= primaryPart then
					pcall(function()
						v7:Destroy()
					end)
				end
			end

			_G.RyAltInvisActive = false
		end)
	end

	_G.RyAltInvisToggle = function()
		if tick() - n16 < 0.3 then
			return
		end

		if flag3 then
			ryAltInvisStop()
		else
			ryAltInvisStart()
		end
	end

	_G._forceInvisToggle = function()
		if flag3 then
			ryAltInvisStop()
		else
			ryAltInvisStart()
		end
	end

	localPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
		task.wait(0.1)

		if (_G.InvisEngine or "ry") == "ryalt" then
			pcall(function()
				for _, child in pairs(workspace.CurrentCamera:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then
						child:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end)
		end

		if humanoidRootPart then
			pcall(function()
				humanoidRootPart:Destroy()
			end)

			humanoidRootPart = nil
		end

		if clone then
			pcall(function()
				clone:Destroy()
			end)

			clone = nil
		end

		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil
		end

		for _, v7 in ipairs(tbl6) do
			if v7 then
				v7:Disconnect()
			end
		end

		tbl6 = {}
		flag3 = false
		tbl5 = {}
		_G.RyAltInvisActive = false
		fn29()
		n18 = 0
		local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

		if currentCamera and character then
			local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if humanoid then
				currentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid
				currentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			end
		end
	end)

	local flag5 = false
	local flag6 = false

	_G.RyAltInvisSetAutoOn = function(arg)
		flag6 = arg

		if not arg and flag3 then
			pcall(ryAltInvisStop)
		end
	end

	local n19 = 0

	local function ryAltInvisSync()
		local flag7 = _G.RyTPActive == true and _G.RyInvisDuringTP ~= true
		local flag8 = _G.RyAltInvisAuto == true

		if not (flag8 and localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true and not flag7) then
			flag5 = false

			if flag3 and flag6 then
				if not flag8 or flag7 then
					n19 += 1
					flag6 = false

					if _G.RyInvisStealRepaint then
						pcall(_G.RyInvisStealRepaint, false)
					end

					pcall(ryAltInvisStop)
				else
					n19 += 1
					local v7 = n19

					task.delay(tonumber(_G.RyAltInvisStealGrace) or 0.6, function()
						if v7 ~= n19 then
							return
						end

						if flag3 and flag6 and (_G.RyAltInvisAuto ~= true or localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true) then
							flag6 = false

							if _G.RyInvisStealRepaint then
								pcall(_G.RyInvisStealRepaint, false)
							end

							pcall(ryAltInvisStop)
						end
					end)
				end
			end

			return
		end

		n19 += 1
		if flag3 or flag5 then
			return
		end
		flag5 = true

		task.delay(tonumber(_G.RyAltInvisAutoDelay) or 0, function()
			flag5 = false
			local flag9 = _G.RyAltInvisAuto == true and not flag3 and localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true

			if flag9 then
				flag9 = not (_G.RyTPActive == true and _G.RyInvisDuringTP ~= true)
			end

			if flag9 then
				flag6 = true
				pcall(ryAltInvisStart)

				if _G.RyInvisStealRepaint then
					pcall(_G.RyInvisStealRepaint, true)
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	_G.RyAltInvisSync = ryAltInvisSync
	localPlayer2:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(ryAltInvisSync)

	task.spawn(function()
		local v7

		while true do
			local flag7 = _G.RyTPActive == true

			if flag7 ~= v7 then
				pcall(ryAltInvisSync)
				v7 = flag7
			end

			task.wait(0.1)
		end
	end)
end
