task.spawn(function()
	local Players3 = game:GetService("Players")

	while not Players3.LocalPlayer do
		task.wait()
	end

	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		local Players4 = game:GetService("Players")
		local RunService3 = game:GetService("RunService")
		local Workspace2 = game:GetService("Workspace")
		local ReplicatedStorage2 = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
		local localPlayer4 = Players4.LocalPlayer
		local tbl5 = {}
		local v7 = nil
		local humanoid = nil
		local humanoidRootPart = nil
		local animator = nil
		local vector = Vector3.zero
		local n16 = 40
		local n17 = 25

		local function fn29()
			if not v7 then
				return false
			end

			if not v7:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool") then
				return false
			end
			local humanoidRootPart2 = v7:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if humanoidRootPart2 then
				for _, child in ipairs(humanoidRootPart2:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("BodyVelocity") or child:IsA("BodyPosition") or child:IsA("BodyGyro") then
						return true
					end
				end
			end

			return false
		end

		local function fn30()
			if not humanoid then
				return false
			end
			local state = humanoid:GetState()
			return state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp
		end

		local function fn31()
			pcall(function()
				local playerModule = localPlayer4:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts"):WaitForChild("PlayerModule", 10)
				require(playerModule):GetControls():Enable()
			end)
		end

		local function fn32()
			if not v7 then
				return
			end
			local v8 = fn29()

			local function fn33(arg)
				for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or child:IsA("NoCollisionConstraint") or child:IsA("HingeConstraint") or child:IsA("Attachment") and (child.Name == "A" or child.Name == "B") then
						child:Destroy()
					elseif child:IsA("BodyVelocity") or child:IsA("BodyPosition") or child:IsA("BodyGyro") then
						if not v8 then
							child:Destroy()
						end
					elseif child:IsA("Motor6D") then
						child.Enabled = true
					elseif child:IsA("BasePart") then
						for _, child2 in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do
							if child2:IsA("Motor6D") then
								child2.Enabled = true
							elseif child2:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or child2:IsA("NoCollisionConstraint") or child2:IsA("HingeConstraint") then
								child2:Destroy()
							elseif child2:IsA("Attachment") and (child2.Name == "A" or child2.Name == "B") then
								child2:Destroy()
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			pcall(function()
				fn33(v7)
			end)

			if animator then
				for _, v9 in pairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
					local str = v9.Animation and v9.Animation.Name:lower() or ""

					if str:find("rag") or str:find("fall") or str:find("hurt") or str:find("down") then
						v9:Stop(0)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local function fn33(arg)
			pcall(function()
				arg.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
			end)

			pcall(function()
				arg.RequiresNeck = false
			end)

			pcall(function()
				arg:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
			end)

			pcall(function()
				arg:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
			end)
		end

		local function fn34(arg)
			pcall(function()
				arg.Health = arg.MaxHealth
			end)

			pcall(function()
				arg:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
			end)
		end

		local function fn35(arg)
			v7 = arg
			humanoid = arg:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
			humanoidRootPart = arg:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
			animator = humanoid and humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 10)
			vector = Vector3.zero

			if humanoid then
				fn33(humanoid)
			end
		end

		local function fn36()
			for _, v8 in pairs(tbl5) do
				pcall(function()
					v8:Disconnect()
				end)
			end

			tbl5 = {}
		end

		local function fn37()
			fn36()
			if not humanoid or not humanoidRootPart then
				return
			end
			fn33(humanoid)

			table.insert(tbl5, humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
				if _G.AntiDieDisabled then
					return
				end

				if humanoid and humanoid.Parent and humanoid.Health <= 0 then
					fn34(humanoid)
				end
			end))

			table.insert(tbl5, humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
				if _G.AntiDieDisabled then
					return
				end

				if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
					fn34(humanoid)
				end
			end))

			local n18 = 0

			table.insert(tbl5, RunService3.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if not humanoid or not humanoid.Parent then
					return
				end

				if not _G.AntiDieDisabled then
					local now = os.clock()

					if now - n18 >= 1 then
						n18 = now
						fn33(humanoid)
					end

					if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
						fn34(humanoid)
					end

					if _G.RyStealHold and humanoid.Health < humanoid.MaxHealth then
						pcall(function()
							humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
						end)
					end
				end

				if (_G.RyAntiRagdollEnabled or _G.RyAntiKnockbackEnabled) and fn30() then
					if _G.StealHold == true then
						return
					end

					if humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 60 then
						vector = humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
						return
					end
					fn32()
					local assemblyLinearVelocity = humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity

					if (assemblyLinearVelocity - vector).Magnitude > n16 and assemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > n17 then
						humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = assemblyLinearVelocity.Unit * math.min(assemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude, 15)
					end

					vector = assemblyLinearVelocity
				end
			end))

			table.insert(tbl5, humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function()
				if (_G.RyAntiRagdollEnabled or _G.RyAntiKnockbackEnabled) and fn30() then
					if not fn29() then
						humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
					end

					fn32()

					pcall(function()
						Workspace2.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid
					end)

					fn31()
				end
			end))

			pcall(function()
				local packages = ReplicatedStorage2:FindFirstChild("Packages")
				packages = packages and packages:FindFirstChild("Net")
				packages = packages and packages:FindFirstChild("RE/CombatService/ApplyImpulse")

				if packages then
					table.insert(tbl5, packages.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
						if (_G.RyAntiRagdollEnabled or _G.RyAntiKnockbackEnabled) and fn30() then
							humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						end
					end))
				end
			end)

			table.insert(tbl5, v7.DescendantAdded:Connect(function()
				if (_G.RyAntiRagdollEnabled or _G.RyAntiKnockbackEnabled) and fn30() then
					fn32()
				end
			end))

			fn31()
			fn32()
		end

		local function fn38()
			_G.RyAntiRagdollEnabled = true
			_G.RyAntiKnockbackEnabled = true

			if localPlayer4.Character then
				fn35(localPlayer4.Character)
				fn37()
			end
		end

		local function fn39()
			_G.RyAntiRagdollEnabled = false
			_G.RyAntiKnockbackEnabled = false
			fn36()
		end

		_G.RyToggleAntiRagdoll = function(arg)
			if arg then
				fn38()
			else
				fn39()
			end
		end

		_G.RyEnableAntiKnockback = function()
			fn38()
		end

		_G.RyDisableAntiKnockback = function()
			fn39()
		end

		localPlayer4.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
			fn36()
			v7 = nil
			humanoid = nil
			humanoidRootPart = nil
			animator = nil
			local humanoid2 = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
			local humanoidRootPart2 = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
			if not humanoid2 or not humanoidRootPart2 then
				return
			end
			task.wait(0.2)
			fn35(character)

			if _G.RyAntiRagdollEnabled or _G.RyAntiKnockbackEnabled then
				fn37()
			end
		end)

		if localPlayer4.Character then
			fn35(localPlayer4.Character)

			if _G.RyAntiRagdollEnabled or _G.RyAntiKnockbackEnabled then
				fn37()
			end
		end

		fn38()
	end)

	if not ok then
		_G.RyAntiDieError = tostring(result)
	end
end)

do
	local RunService3 = game:GetService("RunService")
	local localPlayer4 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
	local tbl5 = {}
	local n16 = 400
	local now = os.clock()

	local function invisTraceLog(arg)
		tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = string.format("%7.2f  %s", os.clock() - now, arg)

		if #tbl5 > n16 then
			table.remove(tbl5, 1)
		end
	end

	_G.InvisTraceLog = invisTraceLog

	local function fn29()
		local traceback = debug and debug.traceback and debug.traceback("", 3) or ""
		local tbl6 = {}

		for match in traceback:gmatch("[^\n]+") do
			local match2 = match:match(":(%d+)")

			if match2 then
				tbl6[#tbl6 + 1] = "L" .. match2
			end

			if not (#tbl6 >= 4) then
				continue
			end
			break
		end

		return #tbl6 > 0 and table.concat(tbl6, "<") or "?"
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		for i = 1, 40 do
			if not _G.RyInvisStop then
				task.wait(0.25)
				continue
			end
			break
		end

		for _, v7 in ipairs({ "RyInvisStart", "RyInvisStop", "RyInvisToggle" }) do
			local v8 = _G[v7]

			if type(v8) == "function" then
				_G[v7] = function(...)
					invisTraceLog(("%s  from %s"):format(v7:gsub("RyInvis", ""):upper(), fn29()))
					return v8(...)
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	local tbl6 = {}
	local v7 = nil

	RunService3.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		local character = localPlayer4.Character
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local n17 = 0

		if currentCamera then
			for _, child in ipairs(currentCamera:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then
					n17 += 1
				end
			end
		end

		local primaryPart = character and character.PrimaryPart
		local flag3 = false

		pcall(function()
			local animator = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")

			if animator then
				for _, v8 in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
					if v8.Animation and tostring(v8.Animation.AnimationId):find("18537363391", 1, true) then
						flag3 = true
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end)

		local tbl7 = { act = tostring(_G.RyInvisActive == true), roots = tostring(n17) }
		local prim

		if primaryPart then
			prim = primaryPart.Name .. (primaryPart.Parent == character and "" or "(!notInChar)")
		else
			prim = primaryPart
		end

		tbl7.prim = prim or "nil"
		tbl7.st = humanoid and tostring(humanoid:GetState()):gsub("Enum.HumanoidStateType.", "") or "nohum"
		tbl7.steal = tostring(localPlayer4:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true)
		tbl7.ws = tostring(character and character.Parent == workspace)
		tbl7.trk = tostring(flag3)
		tbl7.hp = humanoid and tostring(math.floor(humanoid.Health)) or "?"
		local tbl8 = {}

		for _, v8 in ipairs({ "act", "roots", "prim", "st", "steal", "ws", "trk", "hp" }) do
			if tbl6[v8] ~= tbl7[v8] then
				tbl8[#tbl8 + 1] = v8 .. "=" .. tbl7[v8]
			end
		end

		if #tbl8 > 0 then
			invisTraceLog(table.concat(tbl8, "  "))
		end

		tbl6 = tbl7
		primaryPart = primaryPart or character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if primaryPart then
			local position = primaryPart.Position

			if v7 and (position - v7).Magnitude > 8 then
				invisTraceLog(("LAGBACK  jumped %.1f studs"):format((position - v7).Magnitude))
			end

			v7 = position
		end
	end)

	_G.InvisDump = function()
		local str = table.concat(tbl5, "\n")
		pcall(v3, "===== INVIS TRACE (" .. #tbl5 .. " lines) =====\n" .. str)
		local v8 = setclipboard or toclipboard

		if type(v8) == "function" then
			pcall(v8, str)
			pcall(v3, "===== copied to clipboard =====")
		end

		return str
	end
end

_G.AutoTP = false
local fn29
fn29 = nil
local fn30
fn30 = nil

do
	local function fn31(arg)
		if arg then
			if fn30 then
				pcall(fn30, false, false)
			end

			_G.StealTargetUID = nil
			_G.StealMode = "priority"
			_G.AutoTP = true
		elseif _G.StealMode == "priority" then
			_G.AutoTP = false
		end
	end

	local function fn32(arg)
		if arg then
			if fn29 then
				pcall(fn29, false, false)
			end

			_G.StealTargetUID = nil
			_G.StealMode = "nearest"
			_G.AutoTP = true
		elseif _G.StealMode == "nearest" then
			_G.AutoTP = false
			_G.StealMode = "priority"
		end
	end

	_G.StealNearestOn = false
	_G.StealPriorityOn = false

	_G.setStealNearest = function(arg)
		local stealNearestOn = arg and true or false
		_G.StealNearestOn = stealNearestOn
		fn32(stealNearestOn)

		if stealNearestOn then
			_G.StealPriorityOn = false
		end
	end

	_G.setStealPriority = function(arg)
		local stealPriorityOn = arg and true or false
		_G.StealPriorityOn = stealPriorityOn
		fn31(stealPriorityOn)

		if stealPriorityOn then
			_G.StealNearestOn = false
		end
	end
end

_G.RyLog("init", string.format("steal mode from config: %s, auto TP %s", tostring(_G.StealMode), _G.AutoTP == true and "on" or "off"))

do
	local flag3 = false
	local flag4 = false

	local function fn31()
		if flag4 then
			return
		end
		flag4 = true

		pcall(function()
			game:Shutdown()
		end)

		pcall(function()
			localPlayer:Kick("\n[Auto Kick] Stolen item secured!")
		end)
	end

	local function fn32(arg)
		if not flag3 then
			return
		end

		if type(arg) ~= "string" or arg == "" then
			return
		end

		if string.find(string.lower(arg), "you stole", 1, true) then
			fn31()
		end
	end

	local obj2 = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

	local function fn33(arg)
		if obj2[arg] then
			return
		end
		obj2[arg] = true
		fn32(arg.Text)

		arg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
			fn32(arg.Text)
		end)
	end

	local function fn34(arg)
		return arg:IsA("TextLabel") or arg:IsA("TextButton") or arg:IsA("TextBox")
	end

	local function fn35(arg)
		if not arg then
			return
		end

		arg.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if fn34(descendant) then
				fn33(descendant)
			end
		end)

		for _, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
			if fn34(descendant) then
				fn33(descendant)
			end
		end
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		local playerGui = localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)
		if not playerGui then
			return
		end

		for _, child in ipairs(playerGui:GetChildren()) do
			pcall(fn35, child)
		end

		playerGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			pcall(fn35, child)
		end)
	end)

	_G.setAutoKickOnSteal = function(autoKickOnSteal)
		flag3 = autoKickOnSteal
		_G.AutoKickOnSteal = autoKickOnSteal
	end
end

do
	local autoBuy = false
	local v7 = nil
	local tbl5 = {}
	local color = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 175)
	local v8 = nil
	local part = nil
	local model = nil

		if not arg or not arg.Parent or not arg.Enabled then
			return

		_G.__abLastFire = _G.__abLastFire or setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

		local flag5 = _G.__abLastRemote[arg]

		if flag5 then
			flag5 = now - flag5 < (tonumber(_G.AutoBuyRemoteGap) or 0.5)
		end

		if flag5 then
			return
		end

		if _G.__abInFlight[arg] then
			return
		end
		_G.__abLastRemote[arg] = now
		_G.__abInFlight[arg] = true

		task.spawn(function()
			local v12 = fn37()

			if v12 then
				pcall(function()
					if v12:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
						v12:InvokeServer(arg.Parent)
					elseif v12:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
						v12:FireServer(arg.Parent)
					end
				end)
			end

			_G.__abInFlight[arg] = nil
		end)
	end

	local function createBodyPosition(parent)
		local v11 = fn32()

			v9.P = v11 * 8000

			v9 = nil

	RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()

	local function ryBuySetAutoBuy(arg)

			autoBuy = arg and true or false
		else
			autoBuy = not autoBuy
		end

		_G.RyBuyAutoBuy = autoBuy

			model = nil
		end

			pcall(_G.RyBuyPaintAutoBuy)
		end
	end

		end

if _G.AntiDieDisabled == nil then

task.spawn(function()
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		local connection = nil
		local connection2 = nil
		local connection3 = nil

		local function fn31(arg)
			pcall(function()
				arg.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
			end)

			pcall(function()
				arg.RequiresNeck = false
			end)

			pcall(function()
				arg:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
			end)

			pcall(function()
				arg:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
			end)

			pcall(function()
				arg:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
			end)

			pcall(function()
				arg:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics, false)
			end)
		end

		local function fn32(arg)
			pcall(function()
				arg.Health = arg.MaxHealth
			end)

			pcall(function()
				arg:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
			end)
		end

		local function fn33()
			local character = localPlayer.Character
			local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if not humanoid then
				return
			end
			fn31(humanoid)

			if connection then
				pcall(function()
					connection:Disconnect()
				end)
			end

			if connection2 then
				pcall(function()
					connection2:Disconnect()
				end)
			end

			if connection3 then
				pcall(function()
					connection3:Disconnect()
				end)
			end

			connection = humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
				if _G.AntiDieDisabled then
					return
				end

				if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
					fn32(humanoid)
				end
			end)

			connection2 = humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
				if _G.AntiDieDisabled then
					return
				end
				fn32(humanoid)
			end)

			local n16 = 0

			connection3 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if _G.AntiDieDisabled or not humanoid or not humanoid.Parent then
					return
				end

				if _G.RyInvisActive == true then
					return
				end
				local now = os.clock()

				if now - n16 >= 0.5 then
					n16 = now
					fn31(humanoid)
				end

				if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
					fn32(humanoid)
				end

				local parent = humanoid.Parent
				local humanoidRootPart = parent and parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if parent and humanoidRootPart then
					local state = humanoid:GetState()
					local flag3 = state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown

					if not flag3 then
						local num = tonumber(localPlayer:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime"))

						if num and num - workspace:GetServerTimeNow() > 0 then
							flag3 = true
						end
					end

					if flag3 then
						pcall(function()
							local num = tonumber(localPlayer:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime"))
							local serverTimeNow = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()

							if num and num - serverTimeNow > 0 then
								_G.RagdollUntil = math.max(tonumber(_G.RagdollUntil) or 0, num)
							end
						end)

						if not isTeleporting then
							_G.RagdollPhysLastT = tick()
						end

						pcall(function()
							localPlayer:SetAttribute("RagdollEndTime", workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
						end)

						pcall(function()
							humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
						end)

						if not _G.__ResetBusy and localPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
							pcall(function()
								humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
							end)
						end

						local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

						if currentCamera and currentCamera.CameraSubject ~= humanoid then
							pcall(function()
								currentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid
							end)
						end

						for _, descendant in ipairs(parent:GetDescendants()) do
							if descendant:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or descendant.Name and descendant.Name:find("RagdollAttachment") then
								pcall(function()
									descendant:Destroy()
								end)
							end
						end
					end
				end

				local dead = Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead

				if humanoid:GetState() == dead then
					pcall(function()
						humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
					end)
				end
			end)
		end

		fn33()

		localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
			local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)

			if humanoid then
				fn31(humanoid)
			end

			task.wait(0.1)
			fn33()
		end)
	end)

	if not ok then
		fn12("[ANTI-DIE] BLOC EN ERREUR: " .. tostring(result))
	end
end)

			end)

local executeInstaReset

executeInstaReset = function()
	local character = localPlayer.Character
	if not character then
		return
	end

	if _G.__ResetBusy then
		return
	end
	_G.AntiDieDisabled = true
	_G.__ResetBusy = true
	_G.StealHold = false

	pcall(function()
		if _G.RyInvisActive and _G.InvisStop then
			_G.InvisStop()
		end
	end)

	local flag3 = false

	local function fn31()
		if flag3 then
			return
		end
		flag3 = true
		_G.AntiDieDisabled = false
		_G.__ResetBusy = false
	end

	local connection = nil

	connection = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character2)
		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil
		end

		task.defer(function()
			pcall(function()
				character2:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
			end)

			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			fn31()
		end)
	end)

	task.delay(8, function()
		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil
		end

		fn31()
	end)

	local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

	pcall(function()
		if humanoid then
			humanoid:UnequipTools()
		end
	end)

	for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Tool") then
			pcall(function()
				child.Parent = localPlayer.Backpack
			end)
		end
	end

	if humanoid then
		pcall(function()
			humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
		end)

		pcall(function()
			humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = true
		end)

		pcall(function()
			humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
		end)

		pcall(function()
			humanoid.Health = 0
		end)

		if humanoid.Health > 0 then
			pcall(function()
				humanoid:TakeDamage(humanoid.MaxHealth * 99)
			end)
		end
	end

	pcall(function()
		character:BreakJoints()
	end)

	task.delay(0.1, function()
		if localPlayer.Character ~= character then
			return
		end
		local humanoid2 = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if humanoid2 and humanoid2.Health <= 0 then
			return
		end
		local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if humanoidRootPart then
			pcall(function()
				humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				humanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart.Position.X, workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight - 500, humanoidRootPart.Position.Z)
			end)
		end
	end)
end

_G.executeInstaReset = executeInstaReset
_G.instaReset = executeInstaReset
_G.InstaReset = executeInstaReset
local flag3, setCarpetSpeed, instantClone, flag4, setFloat, executeKickToPS

do
	local function equipCarpetTool()
		local character = localPlayer.Character
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not humanoid or not character then
			return false
		end
		local backpack = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		local ryFlyingTool = _G.RyFlyingTool or _G.CarpetTool or tbl and tbl.FlyingTool or "Flying Carpet"

		if setCarpetTool then
			setCarpetTool(ryFlyingTool)
		end

		local tbl5 = { ryFlyingTool, "Flying Carpet", "Cupid's Wings", "Santa's Sleigh", "Witch's Broom", "Waverider" }
		local tool = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

		if tool then
			local exitTo = nil

			for _, v7 in ipairs(tbl5) do
				if tool.Name == v7 then
					exitTo = 1
					break
				end
			end

			if exitTo == 1 then
				if not (tool.Name ~= ryFlyingTool and backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild(ryFlyingTool)) then
					return true
				end
			end
		end

		local v7 = nil

		if backpack then
			for _, v8 in ipairs(tbl5) do
				local v9 = backpack:FindFirstChild(v8)
				if v9 and v9:IsA("Tool") then
					v7 = v9
					break
				end
			end

			if not v7 then
				for _, child in ipairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Tool") then
						local str = child.Name:lower()
						if not str:find("grapp") and not str:find("hook") then
							v7 = child
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if not v7 then
			for _, v8 in ipairs(tbl5) do
				local v9 = character:FindFirstChild(v8)
				if v9 and v9:IsA("Tool") then
					v7 = v9
					break
				end
			end
		end

		if v7 then
			if v7.Parent == character then
				return true
			end

			pcall(function()
				humanoid:EquipTool(v7)
			end)

			return true
		end

		return false
	end

	_G.equipCarpetTool = equipCarpetTool
	_G.CarpetSpeedValue = _G.CarpetSpeedValue or 140
	flag3 = false
	local connection = nil

	setCarpetSpeed = function(carpetSpeed)
		flag3 = carpetSpeed
		_G.CarpetSpeed = carpetSpeed

		if connection then
			pcall(function()
				connection:Disconnect()
			end)

			connection = nil
		end

		if not carpetSpeed then
			return
		end
		pcall(equipCarpetTool)

		connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if _G.StealHold == true then
				return
			end
			local character = localPlayer.Character
			local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if character then
				character = character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			end

			if not humanoid or not character then
				return
			end
			pcall(equipCarpetTool)
			local n16 = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.CarpetSpeedValue) or 140, 20, 400)
			local moveDirection = humanoid.MoveDirection
			local y = character.Velocity.Y

				character.Velocity = Vector3.new(moveDirection.X * n16, y, moveDirection.Z * n16)
			else
				character.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, y, 0)
			end
		end)
	end

		flag5 = false

	local function dropBrainrot()
		if flag5 then
			return
		end
		flag5 = true
		local character = localPlayer.Character
		if not character then
			flag5 = false
			return
		end
		local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		for _, child in pairs(workspace.CurrentCamera:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then
				humanoidRootPart = child
				break
			end
		end

		if not humanoidRootPart then
			flag5 = false
			return
		end

		table.insert(tbl5, RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
			if not flag5 then
				return
			end

			for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
					for _, child in ipairs(player.Character:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("BasePart") then
							child.CanCollide = false
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end))

		task.spawn(function()
			if _G.InvisActive then
				humanoidRootPart.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
			end

			while flag5 do
				RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()

				if not (not humanoidRootPart or not humanoidRootPart.Parent) then
					local velocity = humanoidRootPart.Velocity
					humanoidRootPart.Velocity = velocity * 10000 + Vector3.new(0, 10000, 0)
					RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()

					if humanoidRootPart then
						humanoidRootPart.Velocity = velocity
					end

					RunService.Stepped:Wait()

					if humanoidRootPart then
						humanoidRootPart.Velocity = velocity + Vector3.new(0, 0.1, 0)
					end

					continue
				end

				break
			end
		end)

		task.delay(0.4, fn32)
	end

	local function applyDarkBrightness(arg)
		if not _G.DarkModeOn then
			return
		end
		local n16 = math.clamp(tonumber(arg) or 0.4, 0, 1)

		pcall(function()
			Lighting.Brightness = (tbl6 and tbl6.Brightness or 2) * (1 - n16)
			Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(1, 1, 1):Lerp(tbl6 and tbl6.Ambient or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 70), 1 - n16)
			Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(1, 1, 1):Lerp(tbl6 and tbl6.OutdoorAmbient or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 70), 1 - n16)
			Lighting.ExposureCompensation = (tbl6 and tbl6.Exposure or 0) - n16
		end)
	end

	_G.applyDarkBrightness = applyDarkBrightness

		_G.DarkModeOn = arg and true or false

		if arg then
			if not tbl6 then
				tbl6 = {}

				pcall(function()
					tbl6.Brightness = Lighting.Brightness
					tbl6.Ambient = Lighting.Ambient
					tbl6.OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient
					tbl6.Exposure = Lighting.ExposureCompensation
					tbl6.FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd
				end)
			end

			applyDarkBrightness(_G.DarkBrightness or 0.4)
		elseif tbl6 then
			pcall(function()
				Lighting.Brightness = tbl6.Brightness
				Lighting.Ambient = tbl6.Ambient

		end
	end

	local function fn35()
		local match = TextBox.Text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if not match or match == "" then
			return
		end

		local function fn36(arg)
			return tostring(arg):lower():gsub("[%s%-_'%.]", "")
		end

		local v9 = fn36(match)

		for _, sharedPriorityItem in ipairs(_G.SHARED_PRIORITY_ITEMS) do
			if fn36(sharedPriorityItem) == v9 then
				TextBox.Text = ""
				return
			end
		end

		table.insert(_G.SHARED_PRIORITY_ITEMS, 1, match)
		TextBox.Text = ""
		fn33()
		fn34()
	end

		if enterPressed then
			fn35()

	fn()

	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
		if not gameProcessed and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
			if input.KeyCode == (sharedKeybindsState["Insta Reset"] or Enum.KeyCode.X) and input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Space and input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
				executeInstaReset()
			end

			if input.KeyCode == (sharedKeybindsState["Carpet Speed"] or Enum.KeyCode.Q) then
				if localPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
					setCarpetSpeed(not flag3)
				end
			end

			if input.KeyCode == (sharedKeybindsState["Instant Clone"] or Enum.KeyCode.V) then
				instantClone()
			end

			if input.KeyCode == (sharedKeybindsState.Float or Enum.KeyCode.B) then
				if localPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
					setFloat(not flag4)
				end
			end

			if input.KeyCode == (sharedKeybindsState.Kick or Enum.KeyCode.Y) then
				executeKickToPS()
			end

			if input.KeyCode == (sharedKeybindsState["Invisible Steal"] or Enum.KeyCode.U) then
				if _G.InvisToggle then
					pcall(_G.InvisToggle)
				end
			end

			if input.KeyCode == (sharedKeybindsState.Walkspeed or Enum.KeyCode.H) then
				if _G.setWalkSpeedEnabled then
					_G.RyWSEnabled = not (_G.RyWSEnabled == true)
					pcall(_G.setWalkSpeedEnabled, _G.RyWSEnabled)
				end
			end

			if input.KeyCode == (sharedKeybindsState["Drop Brainrot"] or Enum.KeyCode.R) then
				dropBrainrot()
			end

			if input.KeyCode == (sharedKeybindsState.Teleport or Enum.KeyCode.T) then
				if localPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
					task.spawn(function()
						local now = os.clock()
						local result

						while true do
							local ok
							ok, result = pcall(scanAllPets)

							if not (ok and type(result) == "table" and #result > 0) then
								task.wait(0.05)
								result = nil
								if not (os.clock() - now > 1.5) then
									continue
								end
							end

							break
						end

						if not result or #result == 0 then
							_G.StealSay("TP key: no pets found")
							return
						end
						local v9 = nil
						local selectedPet = _G.SelectedPet

						if selectedPet then
							local v10 = _petUid(selectedPet)

							for _, v11 in ipairs(result) do
								if _petUid(v11) == v10 then
									v9 = v11
									break
								end
							end

							if not v9 then
								_G.SelectedPet = nil
								_G.StealTargetUID = nil
								_G.StealTarget = nil
								_G.TPSyncActive = false
							end
						end

						if not v9 then
							for _, v10 in ipairs(result) do
								if _petEligible(v10) and v10.position then
									v9 = v10
									break
								end
							end
						end

						if not (v9 and v9.position) then
							_G.StealSay("TP key: target has no position")
							return
						end

						if type(_G.isTPExecuting) == "function" and _G.isTPExecuting() then
							_G.TPStop = true
							local now2 = os.clock()

							while _G.isTPExecuting() and os.clock() - now2 < 0.75 do
								task.wait(0.03)
							end
						end

						_G.TPStop = false

						if _G.RequireGrapple ~= false then
							_G.GrappleShotToken = nil

							task.spawn(function()
								local flag6 = false

								pcall(function()
									flag6 = ensureGrappleFired(v9.position, tonumber(_G.GrappleKeyTimeout) or 2)
								end)

								if flag6 then
									_G.GrappleShotToken = os.clock()
									_G.GrappleShots = (_G.GrappleShots or 0) + 1
								end
							end)

							RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()

							if not _G.GrappleShotToken then
								_G.GrappleShotToken = os.clock()
							end
						end

						executeBrainrotTP(v9)
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

do
	local RunService3 = game:GetService("RunService")
	local UserInputService3 = game:GetService("UserInputService")
	local localPlayer4 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
	_G.InfiniteJumpEnabled = false
	local n16 = 0
	local connection = nil

	_G.setInfiniteJump = function(infiniteJumpEnabled)
		_G.InfiniteJumpEnabled = infiniteJumpEnabled

		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil
		end

		if not infiniteJumpEnabled then
			return
		end

		connection = RunService3.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not _G.InfiniteJumpEnabled then
				return
			end

			if _G.ClaimBusy and _G.ClaimBusy() then
				return
			end

			if not UserInputService3:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
				return
			end
			local now = tick()
			if now - n16 < 0.1 then
				return
			end
			local character = localPlayer4.Character
			if not character then
				return
			end
			local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
				return
			end
			n16 = now
			humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 55, humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
		end)
	end
end

do
	local RunService3 = game:GetService("RunService")
	local localPlayer4 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
	local tbl5 = {}
	local v7 = nil
	local humanoid = nil
	local humanoidRootPart = nil

	local function fn31()
		if not humanoid then
			return false
		end
		local state = humanoid:GetState()
		return state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
	end

	local function fn32()
		if not v7 then
			return
		end

		for _, descendant in ipairs(v7:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or descendant:IsA("HingeConstraint") or descendant:IsA("NoCollisionConstraint") then
				pcall(function()
					descendant:Destroy()
				end)
			elseif descendant:IsA("Motor6D") then
				pcall(function()
					descendant.Enabled = true
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	local function fn33()
		for _, v8 in ipairs(tbl5) do
			pcall(function()
				v8:Disconnect()
			end)
		end

		tbl5 = {}
		if not humanoid or not humanoidRootPart then
			return
		end

		tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function()
			if not _G.AntiRagdollEnabled then
				return
			end

			if _G.RyInvisActive == true then
				return
			end

			if fn31() then
				pcall(function()
					humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
				end)

				fn32()
			end
		end)

		tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = RunService3.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not _G.AntiRagdollEnabled then
				return
			end

			if _G.RyInvisActive == true then
				return
			end

			if fn31() then
				fn32()

				pcall(function()
					humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
				end)
			end
		end)
	end

	local function fn34(character)
		v7 = character
		humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
		humanoidRootPart = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)

		if _G.AntiRagdollEnabled then
			fn33()
		end
	end

	_G.AntiRagdollEnabled = false

	_G.toggleAntiRagdoll = function(antiRagdollEnabled)
		_G.AntiRagdollEnabled = antiRagdollEnabled

		if antiRagdollEnabled then
			if localPlayer4.Character then
				fn34(localPlayer4.Character)
			end

			fn33()
		else
			for _, v8 in ipairs(tbl5) do
				pcall(function()
					v8:Disconnect()
				end)
			end

			tbl5 = {}
		end
	end

	localPlayer4.CharacterAdded:Connect(fn34)

	if localPlayer4.Character then
		fn34(localPlayer4.Character)
	end
end

do

				arg:SetAttribute("BackpackReady", true)

			end
		end

			pcall(function()

			end)

		end

				v7:Destroy()

			v7 = nil

		fn31()

			end
		end)
	end

	localPlayer4:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
		if flag5 and _G.PauseFeaturesWhileCarrying == true and localPlayer4:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
			flag5 = false
			fn31()

do
	local Workspace2 = game:GetService("Workspace")
	_G.XRayEnabled = false
	local tbl5 = {}
	local obj2 = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	local n16 = 0
	local tbl6 = { "Base", "PlotSign", "FriendPanel", "Cash", "Decorations", "Skin", "Unlock", "Purchases" }

	local function fn31(arg, arg2, arg3)
		if arg3 ~= n16 then
			return
		end

		if arg:IsA("BasePart") then
			if obj2[arg] == nil then
				obj2[arg] = arg.Transparency == arg2 and 0 or arg.Transparency
			end

			local v7 = obj2[arg]

			if v7 < 1 then
				local transparency = v7 + (1 - v7) * arg2

				if math.abs(arg.Transparency - transparency) > 0.01 then
					arg.Transparency = transparency
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local tbl7 = {}

	for _, v7 in ipairs(tbl6) do
		tbl7[v7] = true
	end

	local obj3 = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	local v7 = nil

	local function fn32(arg, arg2, arg3)
		if not arg or arg3 ~= n16 or obj3[arg] == arg3 then
			return
		end
		obj3[arg] = arg3

		tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = arg.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if arg3 == n16 then
				fn31(descendant, arg2, arg3)
			end
		end)

		fn31(arg, arg2, arg3)
		local n17 = 0

		for _, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
			if arg3 ~= n16 then
				return
			end
			fn31(descendant, arg2, arg3)
			n17 += 1

			if n17 % 1500 == 0 then
				task.wait()
			end
		end
	end

	local function fn33(arg, arg2, arg3)
		if not arg or arg3 ~= n16 or obj3[arg] == arg3 then
			return
		end
		obj3[arg] = arg3

		tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = arg.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if arg3 == n16 and tbl7[child.Name] then
				task.spawn(fn32, child, arg2, arg3)
			end
		end)

		for _, v8 in ipairs(tbl6) do
			if arg3 ~= n16 then
				return
			end
			fn32(arg:FindFirstChild(v8), arg2, arg3)
		end
	end

	local function fn34(arg, arg2, arg3)
		if not arg or arg3 ~= n16 or obj3[arg] == arg3 then
			return
		end
		obj3[arg] = arg3

		tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = arg.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if arg3 == n16 then
				task.spawn(fn33, child, arg2, arg3)
			end
		end)

		for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
			if arg3 ~= n16 then
				return
			end
			fn33(child, arg2, arg3)
		end
	end

	_G.setXRay = function(arg)
		local xRayEnabled = arg and true or false
		local n17 = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.XRayAlpha) or 0.6, 0, 1)
		if xRayEnabled and _G.XRayEnabled == true and v7 == n17 and #tbl5 > 0 then
			return
		end
		_G.XRayEnabled = xRayEnabled

		for _, v8 in ipairs(tbl5) do
			if typeof(v8) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
				v8:Disconnect()
			end
		end

		tbl5 = {}
		n16 += 1
		local v8 = n16

		if xRayEnabled then
			v7 = n17

			tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = Workspace2.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				if v8 == n16 and child.Name == "Plots" then
					task.spawn(fn34, child, n17, v8)
				end
			end)

			task.spawn(function()
				local ok, result = pcall(fn34, Workspace2:FindFirstChild("Plots"), n17, v8)

				if not ok and _G.RyWarn then
					_G.RyWarn("xray", "apply failed: " .. tostring(result))
				end
			end)

			if _G.RyLog then
				_G.RyLog("xray", string.format("on (alpha %.2f)%s", n17, Workspace2:FindFirstChild("Plots") and "" or " -- waiting for Plots to stream in"), nil, 0)
			end
		else
			v7 = nil
			local v9 = obj2
			obj2 = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

			for k, v10 in pairs(v9) do
				pcall(function()
					if k:IsA("BasePart") then
						k.Transparency = v10
					end
				end)
			end

			if _G.RyLog then
				_G.RyLog("xray", "off", nil, 0)
			end
		end
	end
end

_G.toggleXRay = function()
	_G.setXRay(not _G.XRayEnabled)
end

do
	local RunService3 = game:GetService("RunService")
	local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
	local localPlayer5 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
	local tbl5 = {}
	_G.EffetsRemover = false
	local tbl6 = { Blue = true, DiscoEffect = true, BeeBlur = true, ColorCorrection = true }

	local tbl7 = {
		BlurEffect = true,
		BloomEffect = true,
		SunRaysEffect = true,
		ColorCorrectionEffect = true,
		DepthOfFieldEffect = true,
	}

	local function fn31(arg)
		if not arg or not arg.Parent then
			return
		end

		if tbl6[arg.Name] then
			pcall(function()
				arg:Destroy()
			end)

			return
		end

		if tbl7[arg.ClassName] then
			pcall(function()
				arg.Enabled = false
			end)
		end
	end

	_G.setEffetsRemover = function(effetsRemover)
		_G.EffetsRemover = effetsRemover

		for _, v7 in ipairs(tbl5) do
			if typeof(v7) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
				v7:Disconnect()
			end
		end

		tbl5 = {}
		if not effetsRemover then
			return
		end

		for _, descendant in ipairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do
			fn31(descendant)
		end

		tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = Lighting.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if _G.EffetsRemover then
				fn31(descendant)
			end
		end)

		tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = RunService3.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not _G.EffetsRemover then
				return
			end

			pcall(function()
				local playerScripts = localPlayer5:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
				playerScripts = playerScripts and playerScripts:FindFirstChild("Bee", true)

				if playerScripts then
					local buzzing = playerScripts:FindFirstChild("Buzzing")

					if buzzing and buzzing:IsA("Sound") then

				end
			end)
		end)

do
	local RunService3 = game:GetService("RunService")
	local localPlayer5 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
	_G.LineToBase = false
	local beam = nil
	local attachment = nil
	local attachment2 = nil
	local part = nil
	local position = nil

	local function fn31()
		for _, v7 in ipairs({ beam, attachment, attachment2, part }) do
			if v7 then
				pcall(function()
					v7:Destroy()
				end)
			end
		end

		beam = nil
		attachment = nil
		attachment2 = nil
		part = nil
		position = nil
	end

	local function fn32()
		local plots = game:GetService("Workspace"):FindFirstChild("Plots")
		if not plots then
			return nil
		end

		for _, child in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
			local plotSign = child:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")

			if plotSign then
				local surfaceGui = plotSign:FindFirstChild("SurfaceGui", true)
				surfaceGui = surfaceGui and surfaceGui:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)

				if surfaceGui and surfaceGui.Text then
					local str = surfaceGui.Text:lower()
					if str:find(localPlayer5.DisplayName:lower(), 1, true) or str:find(localPlayer5.Name:lower(), 1, true) then
						return child
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	local n16 = 0

	RunService3.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not _G.LineToBase then
			if beam then
				fn31()
			end

			return
		end

		local character = localPlayer5.Character
		character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not character then
			if beam then
				pcall(function()
					beam:Destroy()
				end)

				beam = nil
			end

			return
		end

		n16 += 1

		if n16 >= 12 then
			n16 = 0
			local v7 = fn32()

			if v7 then
				local basePart = v7:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)

				if basePart then
					position = basePart.Position
				end
			end
		end

		if position then
			if not part or not part.Parent then
				part = Instance.new("Part")
				part.Anchored = true
				part.CanCollide = false
				part.CanQuery = false
				part.CanTouch = false
				part.Transparency = 1
				part.Size = Vector3.one
				part.Parent = game:GetService("Workspace")
			end

			part.Position = position

			if not attachment or attachment.Parent ~= character then
				if attachment then
					pcall(function()
						attachment:Destroy()
					end)
				end

				attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
				attachment.Parent = character
			end

			if not attachment2 or attachment2.Parent ~= part then
				if attachment2 then
					pcall(function()
						attachment2:Destroy()
					end)
				end

				attachment2 = Instance.new("Attachment")
				attachment2.Position = Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
				attachment2.Parent = part
			end

			if not beam or not beam.Parent then
				if beam then
					pcall(function()
						beam:Destroy()
					end)
				end

				beam = Instance.new("Beam")
				beam.FaceCamera = true
				beam.LightEmission = 1
				beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
				beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)
				beam.Width0 = 0.45
				beam.Width1 = 0.45
				beam.Parent = character
			end

			beam.Attachment0 = attachment
			beam.Attachment1 = attachment2
		end
	end)

	_G.toggleLineToBase = function(arg)
		_G.LineToBase = arg == nil and not _G.LineToBase or arg and true or false

		if not _G.LineToBase then
			fn31()
		end
	end
end

do
	local RunService3 = game:GetService("RunService")
	local localPlayer5 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
	_G.FlingActive = false

	_G.FlingUp = function()
		local character = localPlayer5.Character
		local primaryPart = character and (character.PrimaryPart or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not primaryPart or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
			return
		end
		_G.FlingActive = true

		pcall(function()
			humanoid.PlatformStand = true
			primaryPart.Anchored = false
		end)

		local now = os.clock()

		while os.clock() - now < 0.15 do
			local character2 = localPlayer5.Character

			if character2 then
				primaryPart = character2.PrimaryPart or character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			else
				primaryPart = character2
			end

			if primaryPart then
				primaryPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 99999, 0)
				RunService3.Heartbeat:Wait()
				continue
			end

			break
		end

		pcall(function()
			if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
				humanoid.PlatformStand = false
			end
		end)

		_G.FlingActive = false
	end
end

do
	local Players4 = game:GetService("Players")
	game:GetService("RunService")
	local localPlayer5 = Players4.LocalPlayer
	_G.RyPlayerESP = false
	local tbl5 = {}

	local function fn31(arg)
		if arg == localPlayer5 then
			return
		end
		local humanoidRootPart = arg.Character and arg.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not humanoidRootPart then
			return
		end
		local userId = arg.UserId
		local v7 = tbl5[userId]
		if v7 and v7.bb and v7.bb.Parent then
			return
		end

		if v7 and v7.bb then
			pcall(function()
				v7.bb:Destroy()
			end)
		end

		local billboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		billboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 170, 0, 28)
		billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
		billboardGui.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = Vector3.new(0, 2.8, 0)
		billboardGui.LightInfluence = 0
		billboardGui.Adornee = humanoidRootPart
		billboardGui.Parent = humanoidRootPart
		local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", billboardGui)
		textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		textLabel.TextSize = 13
		textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
		textLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
		textLabel.Text = arg.DisplayName .. " (@" .. arg.Name .. ")"
		tbl5[userId] = { bb = billboardGui, lbl = textLabel, plr = arg }
	end

	local function fn32()
		for k, v7 in pairs(tbl5) do
			if v7.bb then
				pcall(function()
					v7.bb:Destroy()
				end)
			end

			tbl5[k] = nil
		end
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			task.wait(3)

			if _G.RyPlayerESP then
				for _, player in ipairs(Players4:GetPlayers()) do
					pcall(fn31, player)
				end
			elseif next(tbl5) then
				fn32()
			end
		end
	end)

	_G.setPlayerESP = function(ryPlayerESP)
		_G.RyPlayerESP = ryPlayerESP

		if not ryPlayerESP then
			fn32()
		end
	end
end

local function fn31()
	local Players4 = game:GetService("Players")
	local RunService3 = game:GetService("RunService")
	local localPlayer5 = Players4.LocalPlayer
	local name2 = "RymogsESP"

	local function fn32()
		local rymogsESP = workspace:FindFirstChild("RymogsESP")

		if not rymogsESP then
			local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
			folder.Name = name2
			folder.Parent = workspace
			rymogsESP = folder
		end

		return rymogsESP
	end

	for _, v7 in ipairs({ "ESP_Stealer", "ESP_BaseOwner", "ESP_Brainrot", "ESP_Turret", "ESP_Trap", "ESP_BrainrotBox" }) do
		if _G[v7] == nil then
			_G[v7] = false
		end
	end

	local tbl5 = {
		stealer = Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90),
		owner = Color3.fromRGB(90, 170, 255),
		brainrot = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 245),
		turret = Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 40),
		trap = Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 20),
	}

	local tbl6 = {}

	local function fn33(arg)
		local v7 = tbl6[arg]
		if not v7 then
			return
		end

		if v7.hl and v7.hl.Parent then
			pcall(function()
				v7.hl:Destroy()
			end)
		end

		if v7.bb and v7.bb.Parent then
			pcall(function()
				v7.bb:Destroy()
			end)
		end

		tbl6[arg] = nil
	end

	local function clearAllESP()
		for k in pairs(tbl6) do
			fn33(k)
		end

		local rymogsESP = workspace:FindFirstChild("RymogsESP")

		if rymogsESP then
			pcall(function()
				rymogsESP:ClearAllChildren()
			end)
		end
	end

	local function fn34(arg, adornee, fillColor, text, arg2)
		if not adornee or not adornee.Parent then
			fn33(arg)
			return
		end
		local v7 = tbl6[arg]

		if v7 and v7.adornee == adornee and v7.bb and v7.bb.Parent then
			if v7.text ~= text then
				v7.text = text

				if v7.lbl then
					v7.lbl.Text = text
				end
			end

			v7.seen = true
			return
		end

		fn33(arg)
		local v8 = fn32()
		local highlight

		if adornee:IsA("Model") then
			highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
			highlight.Adornee = adornee
			highlight.FillColor = fillColor
			highlight.FillTransparency = 0.6
			highlight.OutlineColor = fillColor
			highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
			highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
			highlight.Parent = v8
		else
			highlight = nil

			if adornee:IsA("BasePart") then
				highlight = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
				highlight.Adornee = adornee
				highlight.Color3 = fillColor
				highlight.LineThickness = 0.06
				highlight.SurfaceColor3 = fillColor
				highlight.SurfaceTransparency = 0.75
				highlight.Parent = v8
			end
		end

		local isBasePart = adornee:IsA("BasePart") and adornee or adornee:FindFirstChild("Head") or adornee:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or adornee:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
		local textLabel = nil
		local billboardGui = nil

		if isBasePart then
			billboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
			billboardGui.Adornee = isBasePart
			billboardGui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(190, 36)
			billboardGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, arg2 or 3, 0)
			billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
			billboardGui.MaxDistance = tonumber(_G.ESP_MaxDistance) or 700
			billboardGui.Parent = v8
			textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", billboardGui)
			textLabel.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			textLabel.TextSize = 12
			textLabel.TextColor3 = fillColor
			textLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
			textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
			textLabel.Text = text
		end

		tbl6[arg] = { hl = highlight, bb = billboardGui, lbl = textLabel, adornee = adornee, text = text, seen = true }
	end

	local find = string.find
	local tbl7 = { "trap", "mine", "hive" }

	local function fn35(arg)
		local str = arg:lower()
		if find(str, "sentrybullet", 1, true) then
			return nil
		end

		if find(str, "tripmine", 1, true) then
			return "trap"
		end

		if find(str, "sentry", 1, true) then
			return "turret"
		end

		if find(str, "turret", 1, true) then
			return "turret"
		end

		for _, v7 in ipairs(tbl7) do
			if find(str, v7, 1, true) then
				return "trap"
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	local function fn36()
		local flag5 = _G.ESP_Stealer == true
		local flag6 = _G.ESP_BaseOwner == true
		if not flag5 and not flag6 then
			return
		end
		local getCurrentBaseOwnerId = flag6 and _G.__getCurrentBaseOwnerId
		local v7 = nil

		if getCurrentBaseOwnerId then
			v7 = _G.__getCurrentBaseOwnerId()
		end

		for _, player in ipairs(Players4:GetPlayers()) do
			if player ~= localPlayer5 then
				local character = player.Character

				if character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
					local stealer, str, str2

					if flag5 and player:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
						stealer = tbl5.stealer
						local attribute = player:GetAttribute("StealingPet")
						str = "STEALER" .. (attribute and "  " .. tostring(attribute) or "")
						str2 = "stealer"
					else
						local flag7 = flag6 and v7 and player.UserId == v7
						str2 = nil
						stealer = nil
						str = nil

						if flag7 then
							stealer = tbl5.owner
							str = "BASE OWNER"
							str2 = "owner"
						end
					end

					if str2 then
						fn34("plr_" .. player.UserId, character, stealer, str, 3)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local n16 = 0
	local v7 = nil

	local function fn37()
		local now = os.clock()
		local flag5 = not v7 or now - n16 > 1.5

		if flag5 then
			flag5 = not (isTeleporting and v7)
		end

		if flag5 then
			local ok, result = pcall(scanAllPets)

			if ok and type(result) == "table" then
				v7 = result
				n16 = now
			end
		end

		return v7
	end

	local function fn38()
		if _G.ESP_Brainrot ~= true then
			return
		end

		if not fn37() then
			return
		end

		local function fn39(arg)
			local n17 = tonumber(arg) or 0
			if n17 >= 1e9 then
				return string.format("%.1fB", n17 / 1e9)
			end

			if n17 >= 1000000 then
				return string.format("%.1fM", n17 / 1000000)
			end

			if n17 >= 1000 then
				return string.format("%.1fK", n17 / 1000)
			end
			return tostring(math.floor(n17))
		end

		local n17 = 0

		for _, v8 in ipairs(v7) do
			if not ((tonumber(_G.ESP_BrainrotTop) or 5) <= n17) then
				if v8.position and v8.plot and v8.slot then
					n17 += 1
					local str = "br_" .. tostring(v8.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(v8.slot)
					local v9 = tbl6[str]
					local mps = v8.mps
					local text = string.format("%s  [%s/s]", tostring(v8.name), fn39(mps))

					if v9 and v9.bb and v9.bb.Parent then
						if v9.text ~= text then
							v9.text = text

							if v9.lbl then
								v9.lbl.Text = text
							end
						end

						v9.seen = true
					else
						fn33(str)
						local v10 = fn32()
						local part = Instance.new("Part")
						part.Name = "BRAnchor"
						part.Anchored = true
						part.CanCollide = false
						part.CanQuery = false
						part.CanTouch = false
						part.Transparency = 1
						part.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
						part.CFrame = CFrame.new(v8.position)
						part.Parent = v10
						local billboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
						billboardGui.Adornee = part
						billboardGui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(190, 34)
						billboardGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
						billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
						billboardGui.MaxDistance = tonumber(_G.ESP_MaxDistance) or 700
						billboardGui.Parent = v10
						local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", billboardGui)
						textLabel.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
						textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
						textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
						textLabel.TextSize = 11
						textLabel.TextColor3 = tbl5.brainrot
						textLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
						textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
						textLabel.Text = text
						tbl6[str] = { hl = part, bb = billboardGui, lbl = textLabel, adornee = part, text = text, seen = true }
					end
				end

				continue
			end

			break
		end
	end

	local function fn39()
		local flag5 = _G.ESP_Turret == true
		local flag6 = _G.ESP_Trap == true
		if not flag5 and not flag6 then
			return
		end
		local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
		if not plots then
			return
		end
		local n17 = (tonumber(_G.ESP_BudgetMs) or 3) / 1000
		local now = os.clock()
		local n18 = 0

		for _, descendant in ipairs(plots:GetDescendants()) do
			n18 += 1

			if n18 >= 400 or os.clock() - now >= n17 then
				RunService3.Heartbeat:Wait()
				now = os.clock()
				n18 = 0
			end

			if descendant:IsA("Model") or descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
				local v8 = fn35(descendant.Name)

				if v8 and (v8 == "turret" and flag5 or v8 == "trap" and flag6) then
					fn34(descendant, descendant, tbl5[v8], v8 == "turret" and "TURRET" or "TRAP", 4)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function fn40()
		return _G.ESP_Stealer == true or _G.ESP_BaseOwner == true or _G.ESP_Brainrot == true or _G.ESP_Turret == true or _G.ESP_Trap == true
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			if not fn40() then
				if next(tbl6) then
					clearAllESP()
				end

				task.wait(0.5)
			else
				for _, v8 in pairs(tbl6) do
					v8.seen = false
				end

				pcall(fn36)
				pcall(fn38)
				pcall(fn39)

				for k, v8 in pairs(tbl6) do
					if not v8.seen then
						fn33(k)
					end
				end

				task.wait(tonumber(_G.ESP_Interval) or 0.5)
			end
		end
	end)

	Players4.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
		if player then
			fn33("plr_" .. player.UserId)
		end
	end)

	local function fn41(arg)
		return function(arg2)
			_G[arg] = arg2 and true or false

			if not fn40() then
				clearAllESP()
			end
		end
	end

	_G.setStealerESP = fn41("ESP_Stealer")
	_G.setBaseOwnerESP = fn41("ESP_BaseOwner")
	_G.setBrainrotESP = fn41("ESP_Brainrot")
	_G.setTurretESP = fn41("ESP_Turret")
	_G.setTrapESP = fn41("ESP_Trap")
	_G.clearAllESP = clearAllESP
end

fn31()

if _G.ESP_BrainrotBox == nil then
	_G.ESP_BrainrotBox = false
end

do
	local folder = nil
	local tbl5 = {}

	local function fn32()
		if folder and folder.Parent then
			return
		end
		folder = Instance.new("Folder")
		folder.Name = "Effects"
		folder.Parent = workspace
		table.clear(tbl5)
	end

	local function fn33()
		if folder then
			pcall(function()
				folder:Destroy()
			end)
		end

		folder = nil
		table.clear(tbl5)
	end

	_G.setBrainrotBoxESP = function(arg)
		_G.ESP_BrainrotBox = arg and true or false

		if not arg then
			fn33()
		end
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		if not game:IsLoaded() then
			game.Loaded:Wait()
		end

		local str = nil

		while true do
			task.wait(tonumber(_G.ESP_BoxGap) or 0.75)

			if _G.ESP_BrainrotBox ~= true then
				if folder then
					fn33()
				end

				str = nil
				continue
			end

			if isTeleporting and folder then
				continue
			end
			local ok, result = pcall(scanAllPets)

			if not ok then
				if str == "err" then
					continue
				end
				fn12("[rymogs boxes] pet scan error: " .. tostring(result))
				str = "err"
				continue
			end

			if type(result) ~= "table" then
				continue
			end
			fn32()
			local tbl6 = {}
			local n16 = tonumber(_G.ESP_BoxSize) or 4.5
			local transparency = tonumber(_G.ESP_BoxTransp) or 0.5
			local espBoxColor = _G.ESP_BoxColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			local n17 = 0

			for _, v7 in ipairs(result) do
				if v7 and v7.position then
					local str2 = tostring(v7.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(v7.slot)
					tbl6[str2] = true
					n17 += 1
					local tbl7 = tbl5[str2]

					if not (tbl7 and tbl7.anchor and tbl7.anchor.Parent) then
						local part = Instance.new("Part")
						part.Anchored = true
						part.CanCollide = false
						part.CanQuery = false
						part.CanTouch = false
						part.Transparency = 1
						part.Size = Vector3.one
						part.Parent = folder
						local boxHandleAdornment = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
						boxHandleAdornment.Adornee = part
						boxHandleAdornment.AlwaysOnTop = true
						boxHandleAdornment.ZIndex = 0

						pcall(function()
							boxHandleAdornment.Shading = Enum.AdornShading.XRayShaded
						end)

						boxHandleAdornment.Parent = part
						tbl7 = { anchor = part, adorn = boxHandleAdornment }
						tbl5[str2] = tbl7
					end

					pcall(function()
						tbl7.anchor.CFrame = CFrame.new(v7.position)
					end)

					pcall(function()
						tbl7.adorn.Size = Vector3.new(n16, n16, n16)
						tbl7.adorn.Transparency = transparency
						tbl7.adorn.Color3 = espBoxColor
					end)
				end
			end

			for k, v7 in pairs(tbl5) do
				if not tbl6[k] then
					pcall(function()
						v7.anchor:Destroy()
					end)

					tbl5[k] = nil
				end
			end

			if n17 == str then
				continue
			end
			fn11(string.format("[rymogs boxes] %d boxes", n17))
			str = n17
		end
	end)
end

do
	local Players4 = game:GetService("Players")
	local flag5 = false
	local tbl5 = {}
	local obj2 = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

	local function fn32(arg)
		tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = arg
	end

	local function fn33(arg)
		if not flag5 or not arg or obj2[arg] then
			return
		end
		obj2[arg] = true

		pcall(function()
			for _, v7 in ipairs(arg:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
				pcall(v7.Stop, v7, 0)
			end
		end)

		fn32(arg.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(arg2)
			if not flag5 then
				return
			end
			pcall(arg2.Stop, arg2, 0)
		end))
	end

	local function fn34(arg)
		if not flag5 or not arg then
			return
		end
		local humanoid = arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		humanoid = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")

		if humanoid then
			fn33(humanoid)
		end

		fn32(arg.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if flag5 and descendant:IsA("Animator") then
				fn33(descendant)
			end
		end))
	end

	local function fn35(arg)
		if not flag5 or arg == Players4.LocalPlayer then
			return
		end

		if arg.Character then
			fn34(arg.Character)
		end

		fn32(arg.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
			if flag5 then
				fn34(character)
			end
		end))
	end

	_G.setAntiFlash = function(arg)
		local antiFlashEnabled = arg and true or false
		if antiFlashEnabled == flag5 then
			return
		end
		flag5 = antiFlashEnabled
		_G.AntiFlashEnabled = antiFlashEnabled

		if not antiFlashEnabled then
			for _, v7 in ipairs(tbl5) do
				pcall(function()
					v7:Disconnect()
				end)
			end

			tbl5 = {}
			obj2 = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
			return
		end

		for _, player in ipairs(Players4:GetPlayers()) do
			pcall(fn35, player)
		end

		fn32(Players4.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
			if flag5 then
				pcall(fn35, player)
			end
		end))
	end
end

_G.AntiFlashEnabled = false

local function fn32()
	local HttpService2 = game:GetService("HttpService")

	local tbl5 = {
		rocket = 120,
		ragdoll = 30,
		balloon = 30,
		inverse = 60,
		nightvision = 60,
		jail = 60,
		tiny = 60,
		jumpscare = 60,
		morph = 60,
	}

	local tbl6 = { "balloon", "inverse", "jail", "jumpscare", "morph", "nightvision", "ragdoll", "rocket", "tiny" }
	local tbl7 = {}

	for _, v7 in ipairs(tbl6) do
		tbl7[v7] = true
	end

	local tbl8 = { allowed = {}, order = {}, pinned = {}, random = false }

	pcall(function()
		if not (isfile and isfile("rymogspriv_admincmds.json") and readfile) then
			return
		end

		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return HttpService2:JSONDecode(readfile("rymogspriv_admincmds.json"))
		end)

		if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" then
			return
		end

		if type(result.allowed) == "table" then
			tbl8.allowed = result.allowed
		end

		if type(result.order) == "table" then
			tbl8.order = result.order
		end

		if type(result.pinned) == "table" then
			tbl8.pinned = result.pinned
		end

		tbl8.random = result.random == true
	end)

	local function fn33()
		pcall(function()
			if writefile then
				writefile("rymogspriv_admincmds.json", HttpService2:JSONEncode(tbl8))
			end
		end)
	end

	local function fn34()
		local tbl9 = {}
		local tbl10 = {}

		if type(tbl8.order) == "table" then
			for _, v7 in ipairs(tbl8.order) do
				if type(v7) == "string" and tbl7[v7] and not tbl10[v7] then
					tbl10[v7] = true
					tbl9[#tbl9 + 1] = v7
				end
			end
		end

		for _, v7 in ipairs(tbl6) do
			if not tbl10[v7] then
				tbl10[v7] = true
				tbl9[#tbl9 + 1] = v7
			end
		end

		return tbl9
	end

	local function fn35(arg)
		return tbl8.allowed[arg] ~= false
	end

	local function fn36()
		local tbl9 = {}

		for _, v7 in ipairs(fn34()) do
			if fn35(v7) then
				tbl9[#tbl9 + 1] = v7
			end
		end

		return tbl9
	end

	local function fn37(arg)
		local v7 = fn36()
		if not arg or #v7 < 2 then
			return v7
		end
		local tbl9 = {}
		local tbl10 = {}
		local tbl11 = {}

		for i, v8 in ipairs(v7) do
			if tbl8.pinned[v8] then
				tbl9[i] = v8
			else
				tbl9[i] = false
				tbl10[#tbl10 + 1] = i
				tbl11[#tbl11 + 1] = v8
			end
		end

		local v8 = Random.new()

		for i = #tbl11, 2, -1 do
			local v9 = v8:NextInteger(1, i)
			local v10 = tbl11[i]
			tbl11[i] = tbl11[v9]
			tbl11[v9] = v10
		end

		for i, v9 in ipairs(tbl10) do
			tbl9[v9] = tbl11[i]
		end

		return tbl9
	end

	_G.RymogsAdminCmds = {
		ALL = tbl6,
		COOLDOWNS = tbl5,
		cfg = tbl8,
		save = fn33,
		isKnown = function(arg)
			return tbl7[arg] == true
		end,
		isAllowed = fn35,
		setAllowed = function(arg, arg2)
			if arg2 == false then
			end

			local v7 = nil
			if tbl8.allowed[arg] == v7 then
				return
			end
			tbl8.allowed[arg] = v7
			fn33()
		end,
		isPinned = function(arg)
			return tbl8.pinned[arg] == true
		end,
		togglePin = function(arg)
			local v7
			tbl8.pinned[arg] = not tbl8.pinned[arg] or v7
			fn33()
			return tbl8.pinned[arg] == true
		end,
		mergedOrder = fn34,
		setOrder = function(order)
			local flag5 = #order == #tbl8.order

			if flag5 then
				for i = 1, #order do
					if order[i] ~= tbl8.order[i] then
						flag5 = false
						break
					end
				end
			end

			tbl8.order = order

			if not flag5 then
				fn33()
			end
		end,
		move = function(arg, arg2)
			local v7 = fn34()
			local n16 = arg + arg2
			if n16 < 1 or n16 > #v7 then
				return false
			end
			local v8 = v7[arg]
			v7[arg] = v7[n16]
			v7[n16] = v8
			tbl8.order = v7
			fn33()
			return true
		end,
		getRandom = function()
			return tbl8.random == true
		end,
		setRandom = function(arg)
			local random = arg == true
			if tbl8.random == random then
				return
			end
			tbl8.random = random
			fn33()
		end,
		ordered = fn36,
		sequence = fn37,
		nextReady = function()
			for _, v7 in ipairs(fn37(tbl8.random)) do
				local apOnCooldown

				if _G.isOnCooldown then
					apOnCooldown = _G.isOnCooldown(v7)
				else
					apOnCooldown = _G.apOnCooldown

					if apOnCooldown then
						apOnCooldown = _G.apOnCooldown(v7, tbl5[v7] or 30)
					end
				end

				if not apOnCooldown then
					return v7
				end
			end

			return nil
		end,
	}
end

fn32()

local function fn33()
	local Players4 = game:GetService("Players")
	local RunService3 = game:GetService("RunService")
	local localPlayer5 = Players4.LocalPlayer
	local connection = nil

	local function fn34(arg, arg2)
		local n16 = arg2 * arg2
		local v7 = nil

		for _, player in ipairs(Players4:GetPlayers()) do
			if player ~= localPlayer5 then
				local character = player.Character
				character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if character then
					local n17 = character.Position - arg
					local n18 = n17.X * n17.X + n17.Z * n17.Z

					if n18 < n16 then
						n16 = n18
						v7 = character
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return v7
	end

	local function fn35(autoRotate)
		local character = localPlayer5.Character
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

		if humanoid then
			pcall(function()
				humanoid.AutoRotate = autoRotate
			end)
		end
	end

	local function fn36()
		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil
		end

		_G.RyFaceAwayOn = false
		fn35(true)
	end

	local function fn37()
		if connection then
			return
		end
		_G.RyFaceAwayOn = true
		fn35(false)

		connection = RunService3.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if _G.RyFaceAwayOn ~= true then
				return
			end

			if type(_G.isTPExecuting) == "function" and _G.isTPExecuting() then
				return
			end
			local character = localPlayer5.Character
			character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not character then
				return
			end
			local v7 = fn34(character.Position, tonumber(_G.FaceAwayRange) or 80)
			if not v7 then
				return
			end
			local n16 = character.Position - v7.Position
			local vector = Vector3.new(n16.X, 0, n16.Z)
			if vector.Magnitude < 0.05 then
				return
			end
			character.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(character.Position, character.Position + vector.Unit)
		end)
	end

	_G.RyToggleFaceAway = function()
		if _G.RyFaceAwayOn == true then
			fn36()
		else
			fn37()
		end

		return _G.RyFaceAwayOn
	end

	_G.RySetFaceAway = function(arg)
		if arg then
			fn37()
		else
			fn36()
		end
	end

	localPlayer5.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		if _G.RyFaceAwayOn == true then
			task.delay(0.6, function()
				if _G.RyFaceAwayOn == true then
					fn35(false)
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
end

		frame8.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)

		Instance.new("UICorner", frame8).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
		local n21 = arg3
		local flag5 = false

		local function fn42(arg6)
			local n22 = arg4 or 1
			n21 = math.clamp(arg + math.floor((arg6 - arg) / n22 + 0.5) * n22, arg, arg2)
			frame8.Size = UDim2.new((n21 - arg) / math.max(arg2 - arg, 1e-06), 0, 1, 0)
			textLabel4.Text = string.format("%." .. n20 .. "f", n21)

			if flag5 and arg5 then
				pcall(arg5, n21)
			end
		end

		fn42(arg3)

		if arg5 then
			task.spawn(function()
				pcall(arg5, n21)
			end)
		end

		flag5 = true
		local flag6 = false

		local function fn43(arg6)
			fn42(arg + (arg2 - arg) * math.clamp((arg6 - textButton.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(textButton.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1))
		end

		textButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				flag6 = true
				fn43(input.Position.X)
			end
		end)

		game:GetService("UserInputService").InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
			if not flag6 then
				return
			end

			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				fn43(input.Position.X)
			end
		end)

		game:GetService("UserInputService").InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
				flag6 = false
			end
		end)

		return frame7, function()
			return n21
		end
	end

	tbl.Toggles = tbl.Toggles or {}
	tbl.Sliders = tbl.Sliders or {}

	fn37 = function(arg, arg2)
		tbl.Toggles["Extras_" .. arg] = arg2
		fn2()
	end

	fn38 = function(arg, arg2)
		local v16 = tbl.Toggles["Extras_" .. arg]
		if type(v16) == "boolean" then
			return v16
		end
		return arg2
	end

	fn39 = function(arg, arg2)
		tbl.Sliders["Extras_" .. arg] = arg2
		fn3()
	end

	fn40 = function(arg, arg2)
		local v16 = tbl.Sliders["Extras_" .. arg]
		if type(v16) == "number" then
			return v16
		end
		return arg2
	end

	local tbl7 = {}
	local tbl8 = {}
	local tbl9 = {}
	tbl6 = {}
	local tbl10 = {}
	local n20 = 4

	local function fn42()
		local n21 = #tbl10
		if n21 == 0 then
			return
		end
		local n22 = -(n16 * 2 + (n21 - 1) * n20) / n21

		for _, v16 in ipairs(tbl10) do
			v16.Size = UDim2.new(1 / n21, n22, 0, n17 - 8)
		end
	end

	local function createTextButton(text, arg, layoutOrder, arg2)
		local textButton = Instance.new("TextButton", frame5)
		textButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 80, 0, n17 - 8)
		textButton.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder
		tbl10[#tbl10 + 1] = textButton
		textButton.BackgroundColor3 = tbl5.ROW
		textButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textButton.Text = ""
		textButton.AutoButtonColor = false
		textButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
		Instance.new("UICorner", textButton).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)
		local frame7 = Instance.new("Frame", textButton)
		frame7.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
		frame7.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
		frame7.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2)
		frame7.BackgroundColor3 = tbl5.ACCENT2
		frame7.BorderSizePixel = 0
		Instance.new("UICorner", frame7).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
		local frame8 = Instance.new("Frame", textButton)
		frame8.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
		frame8.Visible = false
		local textLabel3 = Instance.new("TextLabel", textButton)
		textLabel3.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		textLabel3.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		textLabel3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textLabel3.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		textLabel3.TextSize = 11
		textLabel3.TextColor3 = tbl5.MUTED
		textLabel3.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
		textLabel3.Text = text

		textButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
			if str ~= arg then
				textButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
			end
		end)

		textButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
			if str ~= arg then
				textButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			end
		end)

		tbl7[arg] = { btn = textButton, lbl = textLabel3 }
		tbl8[arg] = frame7
		tbl9[arg] = frame8
		tbl6[arg] = arg2
		return textButton
	end

	fn41 = function(arg)
		if not tbl6[arg] then
			return
		end
		str = arg

		for k, v16 in pairs(tbl6) do
			v16.Visible = k == arg
		end

		for k, v16 in pairs(tbl7) do
			local flag5 = k == arg
			v16.lbl.TextColor3 = flag5 and tbl5.TEXT or tbl5.MUTED
			v16.btn.BackgroundTransparency = flag5 and 0 or 1
			local v17 = tbl9[k]

			if v17 then
				v17.BackgroundColor3 = flag5 and tbl5.ACCENT2 or tbl5.MUTED
			end

			local v18 = tbl8[k]

			if v18 then
				pcall(function()
					TweenService2:Create(v18, TweenInfo.new(0.14, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Size = UDim2.new(flag5 and 1 or 0, flag5 and -18 or 0, 0, 2) }):Play()
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	createTextButton("Visuals", "visuals", 1, v7)
	createTextButton("Movement", "movement", 2, v8)
	createTextButton("Teleport", "teleport", 3, v9)
	createTextButton("Admin", "admin", 5, v15)
	createTextButton("Keybinds", "keybinds", 6, v11)
	createTextButton("UI", "ui", 7, v12)
	createTextButton("Config", "config", 8, v13)

	for k, v16 in pairs(tbl7) do
		local v17 = k

		v16.btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			fn41(v17)
		end)
	end

	local priorityList = createTextButton("Priority List", "__prio", 9, nil)
	tbl6.__prio = nil
	tbl7.__prio = nil

	priorityList.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if _G.TogglePriorityPanel then

		end
	end)

	fn42()
	fn41("visuals")

	local function fn43()
		local rymogsAdminCmds = _G.RymogsAdminCmds
		if not rymogsAdminCmds then
			return
		end
		createFrame(v15, "Admin Panel")

		fn35("Random Command", rymogsAdminCmds.getRandom(), function(arg)
			rymogsAdminCmds.setRandom(arg)
		end).Parent = v15

		createFrame(v15, "Allowed Commands")
		local tbl11 = {}

		for _, v16 in ipairs(rymogsAdminCmds.ALL) do
			fn35(v16:sub(1, 1):upper() .. v16:sub(2), rymogsAdminCmds.isAllowed(v16), function(arg)
				rymogsAdminCmds.setAllowed(v16, arg)

				if tbl11.refreshOrder then
					tbl11.refreshOrder()
				end
			end).Parent = v15
		end

		createFrame(v15, "Command Order")
		local textLabel3 = Instance.new("TextLabel", v15)
		textLabel3.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
		textLabel3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textLabel3.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
		textLabel3.TextSize = 9
		textLabel3.TextColor3 = tbl5.MUTED
		textLabel3.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		textLabel3.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
		textLabel3.TextWrapped = true
		textLabel3.Text = "Arrows set the fire order. PIN locks a slot when Random Command is on."
		local frame7 = Instance.new("Frame", v15)
		frame7.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		frame7.BorderSizePixel = 0
		frame7.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
		local uiListLayout2 = Instance.new("UIListLayout", frame7)
		uiListLayout2.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
		uiListLayout2.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
		local n21 = 26
		local refreshOrder = nil

		refreshOrder = function()
			for _, child in ipairs(frame7:GetChildren()) do
				if not child:IsA("UIListLayout") then
					child:Destroy()
				end
			end

			local v16 = rymogsAdminCmds.mergedOrder()
			rymogsAdminCmds.setOrder(v16)

			for i, v17 in ipairs(v16) do
				local v18 = rymogsAdminCmds.isAllowed(v17)
				local v19 = rymogsAdminCmds.isPinned(v17)
				local frame8 = Instance.new("Frame", frame7)
				frame8.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
				frame8.BackgroundColor3 = tbl5.ROW
				frame8.BorderSizePixel = 0
				frame8.LayoutOrder = i
				Instance.new("UICorner", frame8).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
				local stroke = tbl5.STROKE
				Instance.new("UIStroke", frame8).Color = stroke
				local textLabel4 = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame8)
				textLabel4.Size = UDim2.new(1, -104, 1, 0)
				textLabel4.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
				textLabel4.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				textLabel4.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
				textLabel4.TextSize = 10
				textLabel4.TextColor3 = v18 and tbl5.TEXT or tbl5.MUTED
				textLabel4.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
				textLabel4.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
				textLabel4.Text = i .. ".  " .. v17:sub(1, 1):upper() .. v17:sub(2) .. (v18 and "" or "   (off)")

				local function createTextButton2(text, arg, arg2, arg3)
					local textButton = Instance.new("TextButton", frame8)
					textButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(arg2, n21 - 8)
					textButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, arg, 0.5, -(n21 - 8) / 2)
					textButton.AutoButtonColor = false
					textButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
					textButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
					textButton.TextSize = 9
					textButton.Text = text
					textButton.BackgroundColor3 = arg3 and tbl5.ACCENT2 or tbl5.STROKE
					textButton.TextColor3 = arg3 and tbl5.TEXT or tbl5.MUTED
					Instance.new("UICorner", textButton).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
					return textButton
				end

				local v20 = i

				createTextButton2("^", -96, 20, false).MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					if rymogsAdminCmds.move(v20, -1) then
						refreshOrder()
					end
				end)

				createTextButton2("v", -72, 20, false).MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					if rymogsAdminCmds.move(v20, 1) then
						refreshOrder()
					end
				end)

				createTextButton2("PIN", -48, 38, v19).MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					rymogsAdminCmds.togglePin(v17)
					refreshOrder()
				end)
			end

			frame7.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, #v16 * (n21 + 4))
		end

		tbl11.refreshOrder = refreshOrder
		refreshOrder()
	end

	fn43()
end

			},
			props = function(arg)
				local tbl10 = {}

				if arg:IsA("GuiObject") then
					tbl10[#tbl10 + 1] = "BackgroundTransparency"
				end

				if arg:IsA("TextLabel") or arg:IsA("TextButton") or arg:IsA("TextBox") then
					tbl10[#tbl10 + 1] = "TextTransparency"
				end

				if arg:IsA("ImageLabel") or arg:IsA("ImageButton") then
					tbl10[#tbl10 + 1] = "ImageTransparency"
				end

				if arg:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
					tbl10[#tbl10 + 1] = "ScrollBarImageTransparency"

			end,
			inst = function(arg, arg2)
				local tbl10 = tbl9.orig[arg]

				if not tbl10 then
					if arg2 <= 0 then
						return
					end
					tbl10 = {}
					tbl9.orig[arg] = tbl10
				end

				local tbl11 = tbl9.last[arg]

				if not tbl11 then
					tbl11 = {}
					tbl9.last[arg] = tbl11
				end

				for _, v26 in ipairs(tbl9.props(arg)) do
					local v27 = arg[v26]

					if tbl10[v26] == nil or tbl11[v26] ~= nil and math.abs(v27 - tbl11[v26]) > 0.001 then
						tbl10[v26] = v27
					end

					local n20 = tbl10[v26] + (1 - tbl10[v26]) * arg2

					if math.abs(v27 - n20) > 0.001 then
						arg[v26] = n20
					end

					tbl11[v26] = n20
				end
			end,
			apply = function(arg)
				local n20 = math.clamp((tonumber(tbl.UIAlpha[arg.key]) or 0) / 100, 0, 0.9)

							tbl10[#tbl10 + 1] = child
						end
					end
				end

				for _, v27 in ipairs(tbl10) do
					pcall(tbl9.inst, v27, n20)

					for _, descendant in ipairs(v27:GetDescendants()) do
						pcall(tbl9.inst, descendant, n20)
					end
				end
			end,
		}

		local function fn54()
			pcall(function()
				local flag5 = makefolder

				if flag5 then
					flag5 = not (isfolder and isfolder("ryhub_media"))
