		local function fn55(arg)
			local getcustomasset_ = getcustomasset or getsynasset or syn and syn.getcustomasset
			if type(getcustomasset_) ~= "function" then
				return nil
			end
			local ok, result = pcall(getcustomasset_, arg)
			if ok and type(result) == "string" then
				return result
			end
			return nil
		end

		local function fn56(arg)
			if type(arg) ~= "string" or arg == "" then
				return nil, "no image input"
			end
			local match = arg:match("^%s*(%d+)%s*$") or arg:match("rbxassetid://(%d+)") or arg:match("rbxasset://(%d+)")
			if match then
				return "rbxassetid://" .. match
			end

			if isfile and isfile(arg) then
				local v26 = fn55(arg)
				if v26 then
					return v26
				end
				return nil, "found the file but no getcustomasset"
			end

			if not writefile then
				return nil, "executor has no writefile"
			end
			local match2 = arg:match("^data:image/[%w%+%.%-]*;base64,(.+)$")

			if match2 then
				local str3 = match2:gsub("%s", "")
				local base64decode = crypt and crypt.base64decode or crypt and crypt.base64 and crypt.base64.decode or syn and syn.crypt and syn.crypt.base64decode
				if not base64decode then
					return nil, "executor has no base64 decoder"
				end
				local ok, result = pcall(base64decode, str3)
				if not ok or type(result) ~= "string" or #result < 64 then
					return nil, "base64 decode failed"
				end
				local str4 = (arg:match("^data:image/([%w]+)") or "png"):lower()

				if str4 == "jpeg" then
					str4 = "jpg"
				end

				if str4 ~= "png" and str4 ~= "jpg" and str4 ~= "tga" and str4 ~= "bmp" then
					str4 = "png"
				end

				local str5 = fn54() .. "/RyThemeBG." .. str4
				if not pcall(writefile, str5, result) then
					return nil, "writefile failed"
				end
				return fn55(str5) or nil, "no getcustomasset"
			end

			if not arg:match("^https?://") then
				return nil, "paste an asset id, http(s) link, file path or data:image"
			end
			local request_ = syn and syn.request or http and http.request or http_request or request
			if type(request_) ~= "function" then
				return nil, "executor has no http request"
			end

			local tbl11 = {
				["User-Agent"] = "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/124.0 Safari/537.36",
				Accept = "image/avif,image/webp,image/apng,image/*,*/*;q=0.8",
			}

			local function fn57(arg2, arg3)
				local ok, result = pcall(request_, { Url = arg2, Method = "GET", Headers = arg3 and tbl11 or nil })
				if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" then
					return nil, "request failed"
				end
				local body = result.Body or result.body
				if type(body) ~= "string" or #body < 64 then
					return nil, "empty response (HTTP " .. tostring(result.StatusCode or "?") .. ")"
				end
				return body
			end

			local function fn58(arg2)
				return (arg2:gsub("[^%w%-%._~/]", function(arg3)
					return string.format("%%%02X", string.byte(arg3))
				end))
			end

			if (arg:match("^https?://([^/]+)") or ""):find("tenor%.com") then
				local v26 = fn57(arg, true)

				if v26 then
					local match3 = v26:match("property=\"og:image\"[^>]-content=\"([^\"]+)\"") or v26:match("content=\"([^\"]+)\"[^>]-property=\"og:image\"")

					if match3 then
						arg = match3:gsub("\\u002[fF]", "/")
					end
				end
			end

			_G.HubBgGifUrl = arg:lower():find("%.gif") and arg or nil
			local str3 = arg:gsub("^https?://", "")
			local str4 = "couldn't fetch that image"
			local v26 = nil

			for _, v27 in ipairs({
				{ "https://images.weserv.nl/?url=" .. fn58(str3) .. "&w=1024&we&output=png", false },
				{ "https://i0.wp.com/" .. str3 .. "?w=1024", false },
				{ arg, true },
			}) do
				local v28
				v26, v28 = fn57(v27[1], v27[2])

				if v26 then
					local v29 = v26:byte(1)
					local v30 = v26:byte(2)

					if not (v29 == 137 and v30 == 80 or v29 == 255 and v30 == 216) then
						str4 = "host returned something that isn't a png/jpg"
						v26 = nil
						continue
					end
				else
					if v28 then
						str4 = v28
						v26 = nil
					else
						v26 = nil
					end

					continue
				end

				break
			end

			if not v26 then
				return nil, str4
			end
			local v27 = v26:byte(1)
			local v28 = v26:byte(2)
			local str5 = v27 == 137 and v28 == 80 and "png" or "jpg"
			local str6 = fn54() .. "/RyThemeBG." .. str5
			if not pcall(writefile, str6, v26) then
				return nil, "writefile failed"
			end
			return fn55(str6) or nil, "no getcustomasset"
		end

		local function fn57(parent, image)
			if not parent or not parent:IsA("GuiObject") then
				return
			end
			local ryThemeBG = parent:FindFirstChild("RyThemeBG")

			if image == nil then
				if ryThemeBG then
					ryThemeBG:Destroy()
				end

				return
			end

			if parent.AutomaticSize ~= Enum.AutomaticSize.None then
				if ryThemeBG then
					ryThemeBG:Destroy()
				end

				return
			end

			if not ryThemeBG then
				ryThemeBG = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
				ryThemeBG.Name = "RyThemeBG"
				ryThemeBG.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				ryThemeBG.BorderSizePixel = 0
				ryThemeBG.ZIndex = 0
				ryThemeBG.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
				local uiCorner = parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
				Instance.new("UICorner", ryThemeBG).CornerRadius = uiCorner and uiCorner.CornerRadius or UDim.new(0, 0)
				ryThemeBG.Parent = parent
			end

			ryThemeBG.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			ryThemeBG.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
			ryThemeBG.Image = image
			ryThemeBG.ImageTransparency = tonumber(_G.BgImageTransparency) or 0.05
			ryThemeBG.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
			ryThemeBG.Active = false
			local huge = math.huge

			for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
				if child ~= ryThemeBG and child:IsA("GuiObject") and child.ZIndex < huge then
					huge = child.ZIndex
				end
			end

			if huge == math.huge then
				huge = 1
			end

			ryThemeBG.ZIndex = math.min(huge - 1, -1)

			pcall(function()
				parent.ClipsDescendants = true
			end)
		end

		local function fn58(arg, arg2)

						descendant.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)

					end
				end
			end
		end

		local function fn60(arg, image)

			if not parent then
				return
			end
			local imageLabel = Instance.new("ImageLabel")

			Instance.new("UICorner", imageLabel).CornerRadius = uiCorner and uiCorner.CornerRadius or UDim.new(0, 0)
			imageLabel.ZIndex = math.min(arg.ZIndex - 1, 0)
			imageLabel.Parent = parent
			local tbl11 = {}

			local function fn61()
				if not arg.Parent then
					fn59(arg)
					return
				end
				imageLabel.AnchorPoint = arg.AnchorPoint
				imageLabel.Position = arg.Position
				local absoluteSize = arg.AbsoluteSize
				imageLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(absoluteSize.X, absoluteSize.Y)
				imageLabel.ZIndex = math.min(arg.ZIndex - 1, 0)
				local visible = arg.Visible
				local parent2 = arg.Parent

				while true do
					if visible and parent2 then
						if parent2:IsA("ScreenGui") then
							visible = parent2.Enabled
							break
						else
							if parent2:IsA("GuiObject") and not parent2.Visible then
								visible = false
							end

							parent2 = parent2.Parent
							continue
						end
					end

					break
				end

				imageLabel.Visible = visible
			end

			fn61()
			tbl11[#tbl11 + 1] = arg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Position"):Connect(fn61)
			tbl11[#tbl11 + 1] = arg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AnchorPoint"):Connect(fn61)
			tbl11[#tbl11 + 1] = arg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(fn61)
			tbl11[#tbl11 + 1] = arg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(fn61)

			tbl11[#tbl11 + 1] = arg.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
				fn61()
			end)

			local screenGui2 = arg:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScreenGui")

			if screenGui2 then
				tbl11[#tbl11 + 1] = screenGui2:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(fn61)
			end

			obj3[arg] = { img = imageLabel, conns = tbl11, sync = fn61 }
		end

		end)

		local function applyHubBackground(arg)
			local v26 = ipairs
			local v27 = arg and fn52() or fn53()

			for _, v28 in v26(v27) do
				local v29 = fn49(v28)

				if v29 then
					local n20 = tonumber(_G.BgPanelAlpha) or 0.55

					for _, child in ipairs(v29:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("Frame") and (child.BackgroundTransparency < 1 or obj3[child] ~= nil) or arg == nil and child:IsA("Frame") then
							if arg then
								if obj2[child] == nil then
									obj2[child] = child.BackgroundTransparency
								end

								child.BackgroundTransparency = math.max(child.BackgroundTransparency, n20)
								fn60(child, arg)
							else
								if obj2[child] ~= nil then
									child.BackgroundTransparency = obj2[child]
									obj2[child] = nil
								end

								fn59(child)
								pcall(fn57, child, nil)
							end

							pcall(fn58, child, arg ~= nil)
						end
					end

					RunService3.Heartbeat:Wait()
				end
			end
		end

		local tbl11

		tbl11 = {
			token = 0,
			conn = nil,
			loading = false,
			enabled = function()
				return tbl.Toggles == nil or tbl.Toggles.UI_BgAnimateGif ~= false
			end,
			stop = function()
				tbl11.token = tbl11.token + 1
				tbl11.loading = false

				if tbl11.conn then
					pcall(function()
						tbl11.conn:Disconnect()
					end)

					tbl11.conn = nil
				end
			end,
			status = function(hubGifStatus)
				_G.HubGifStatus = hubGifStatus

				pcall(function()
					textLabel4.Text = "  GIF: " .. hubGifStatus
				end)

				fn11("[rymogs gif] " .. hubGifStatus)
			end,
			setAll = function(image)
				for _, v26 in pairs(obj3) do
					if v26.img and v26.img.Parent then
						v26.img.Image = image
					end
				end
			end,
			parse = function(arg)
				if type(arg) ~= "string" or arg:sub(1, 3) ~= "GIF" then
					return 0, {}
				end
				local byte = string.byte
				local v26 = byte(arg, 11)
				local flag5 = v26 and v26 >= 128
				local n20 = 14

				if flag5 then
					n20 = 14 + 3 * 2 ^ (v26 % 8 + 1)
				end

				local tbl12 = {}
				local n21 = 0
				local n22 = 0

				while n20 <= #arg do
					local v27 = byte(arg, n20)

					if not (not v27 or v27 == 59) then
						if v27 == 33 then
							local v28 = byte(arg, n20 + 1)
							n20 += 2

							if v28 == 249 then
								n21 = (byte(arg, n20 + 2) or 0) + (byte(arg, n20 + 3) or 0) * 256
							end

							while true do
								local v29 = byte(arg, n20)

								if not v29 then
									break
								else
									n20 = n20 + 1 + v29
									if v29 ~= 0 then
										continue
									end
									break
								end
							end

							continue
						elseif v27 == 44 then
							n22 += 1
							n21 = n21 >= 2 and n21 or 10
							tbl12[n22] = n21 / 100
							local v28 = byte(arg, n20 + 9)
							local n23 = n20 + 10

							if v28 and v28 >= 128 then
								n23 += 3 * 2 ^ (v28 % 8 + 1)
							end

							n20 = n23 + 1

							while true do
								local v29 = byte(arg, n20)
								n21 = 0

								if not v29 then
									break
								else
									n20 = n20 + 1 + v29
									if v29 ~= 0 then
										continue
									end
									break
								end
							end

							continue
						end
					end

					break
				end

				return n22, tbl12
			end,
			start = function(arg)
				tbl11.stop()
				local token = tbl11.token
				tbl11.loading = true
				local request_ = syn and syn.request or http and http.request or http_request or request
				if type(request_) ~= "function" or not writefile then
					tbl11.loading = false
					return false, "no http/writefile"
				end

				local tbl12 = {
					["User-Agent"] = "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/124.0 Safari/537.36",
				}

				local function fn61(arg2, arg3, arg4)
					local body = nil
					local flag5 = false

					task.spawn(function()
						local ok, result = pcall(request_, { Url = arg2, Method = "GET", Headers = arg3 and tbl12 or nil })

						if ok and type(result) == "table" then
							body = result.Body or result.body
						end

						flag5 = true
					end)

					local now = tick()

					while true do
						local flag6 = not flag5

						if flag6 then
							flag6 = tick() - now < (arg4 or 15)
						end

						if flag6 then
							task.wait(0.05)
							continue
						end
						break
					end

					return body
				end

				local str3 = arg:gsub("^https?://", ""):gsub("[^%w%-%._~/]", function(arg2)
					return string.format("%%%02X", string.byte(arg2))
				end)

				tbl11.status("downloading gif...")
				local v26 = nil

				for _, v27 in ipairs({ { "https://images.weserv.nl/?url=" .. str3 .. "&output=gif&n=-1", false }, { arg, true } }) do
					v26 = fn61(v27[1], v27[2])
					if not (type(v26) == "string" and v26:sub(1, 3) == "GIF") then
						v26 = nil
						continue
					end
					break
				end

				if tbl11.token ~= token then
					return false, "cancelled"
				end

				if not v26 then
					tbl11.loading = false
					return false, "couldn't download the gif"
				end
				local ok, result, result2 = pcall(tbl11.parse, v26)
				if not ok then
					tbl11.loading = false
					return false, "gif parse error: " .. tostring(result)
				end

				if result < 2 then
					tbl11.loading = false
					return false, "not an animated gif (" .. tostring(result) .. " frames, " .. #v26 .. " bytes)"
				end
				local n20 = tonumber(_G.HubGifMaxFrames) or 90
				local tbl13 = {}

				if result <= n20 then
					for i = 0, result - 1 do
						tbl13[#tbl13 + 1] = i
					end
				else
					local n21 = result / n20

					for i = 0, n20 - 1 do
						tbl13[#tbl13 + 1] = math.floor(i * n21)
					end
				end

				local tbl14 = {}

				for i = 1, #tbl13 do
					local v27 = tbl13[i + 1] or result
					local n21 = 0

					for i2 = tbl13[i] + 1, v27 do
						n21 += result2[i2] or 0.1
					end

					tbl14[i] = math.max(n21, 0.03)
				end

				local n21 = tonumber(_G.HubGifWidth) or 320
				local tbl15 = {}
				local n22 = 0
				local n23 = 0
				local n24 = 0
				local n25 = 8
				local v27 = fn54()
				local fn62 = nil

				fn62 = function()
					while n24 < n25 and n23 < #tbl13 do
						n23 += 1
						n24 += 1
						local v28 = n23
						local v29 = tbl13[v28]

						task.spawn(function()
							if tbl11.token == token then
								local v30 = fn61("https://images.weserv.nl/?url=" .. str3 .. "&w=" .. n21 .. "&output=png&page=" .. v29, false)

								if type(v30) == "string" and v30:byte(1) == 137 then
									local str4 = v27 .. "/RyGif_" .. token .. "_" .. v28 .. ".png"

									if pcall(writefile, str4, v30) then
										tbl15[v28] = fn55(str4)
									end
								end
							end

							n24 -= 1
							n22 += 1
							tbl11.status("loading frames " .. n22 .. "/" .. #tbl13)
							fn62()
						end)
					end
				end

				fn62()
				local now = tick()
				local n26 = math.max(20, #tbl13 * 0.7)

				while n22 < #tbl13 and tick() - now < n26 do
					if tbl11.token ~= token then
						return false, "cancelled"
					end
					task.wait(0.1)
				end

				if tbl11.token ~= token then
					return false, "cancelled"
				end
				local tbl16 = {}
				local tbl17 = {}

				for i = 1, #tbl13 do
					if tbl15[i] then
						tbl16[#tbl16 + 1] = tbl15[i]
						tbl17[#tbl17 + 1] = tbl14[i]
					end
				end

				if #tbl16 < 2 then
					tbl11.loading = false
					return false, "couldn't fetch the gif frames (" .. #tbl16 .. "/" .. #tbl13 .. ")"
				end
				local flag5 = false

				task.spawn(function()
					pcall(function()
						game:GetService("ContentProvider"):PreloadAsync(tbl16)
					end)

					flag5 = true
				end)

				local now2 = tick()

				while not flag5 and tick() - now2 < 4 do
					task.wait(0.1)
				end

				if tbl11.token ~= token then
					return false, "cancelled"
				end
				local n27 = 1
				local n28 = 0
				tbl11.loading = false

				tbl11.conn = RunService3.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
					if tbl11.token ~= token then
						return
					end
					n28 += deltaTime
					local n29 = tbl17[n27] or 0.08
					if n28 < n29 then
						return
					end
					n28 -= n29

					if n28 > 0.4 then
						n28 = 0
					end

					n27 = n27 % #tbl16 + 1
					tbl11.setAll(tbl16[n27])
				end)

				return true, #tbl16
			end,
		}

		local function fn61()
			tbl11.stop()
			local themeImageUrl = textBox2.Text:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")

			if themeImageUrl == "" then
				tbl.ThemeImageUrl = ""
				pcall(fn2)
				_G.HubBgAsset = nil
				_G.HubBgGifUrl = nil

				task.spawn(function()
					pcall(applyHubBackground, nil)
				end)

				textLabel4.Text = "  Background cleared."
				return
			end

			textButton.Text = "..."

			task.spawn(function()
				local v26, v27 = fn56(themeImageUrl)

				if v26 then
					_G.HubBgAsset = v26
					tbl.ThemeImageUrl = themeImageUrl
					pcall(fn2)
					pcall(applyHubBackground, v26)
					textLabel4.Text = "  Applied."

					if _G.HubBgGifUrl and tbl11.enabled() then
						textLabel4.Text = "  Applied. Loading GIF frames..."
						local ok, result, result2 = pcall(tbl11.start, _G.HubBgGifUrl)

						if not ok then
							tbl11.loading = false
							tbl11.status("error: " .. tostring(result))
						elseif result then
							tbl11.status("animating " .. tostring(result2) .. " frames")
						elseif result2 ~= "cancelled" then
							tbl11.status("failed - " .. tostring(result2))
						end
					else
						textLabel4.Text = "  Applied (still - link isn't .gif or Animate GIF is off)."
					end
				else
					textLabel4.Text = "  Failed: " .. tostring(v27 or "unknown")
				end

				if textButton and textButton.Parent then
					textButton.Text = "APPLY"
				end
			end)
		end

		textButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(fn61)

		textBox2.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
			if enterPressed then
				fn61()
			end
		end)

		createTextButton("Clear Background", function()
			textBox2.Text = ""

		end)

		fn47("Text Outline on Background", tbl.Toggles and tbl.Toggles.UI_BgTextStroke ~= false, function(uiBgTextStroke)
			tbl.Toggles = tbl.Toggles or {}
			tbl.Toggles.UI_BgTextStroke = uiBgTextStroke

		end)

		fn47("Background on Main Hub", tbl.Toggles == nil or tbl.Toggles.UI_BgMainHub ~= false, function(uiBgMainHub)

			tbl.Toggles.UI_BgMainHub = uiBgMainHub

			end)
		end)

		fn47("Animate GIF Background", tbl11.enabled(), function(uiBgAnimateGif)
			tbl.Toggles = tbl.Toggles or {}
			tbl.Toggles.UI_BgAnimateGif = uiBgAnimateGif

			if not uiBgAnimateGif then
				tbl11.stop()

				if _G.HubBgAsset then
					tbl11.setAll(_G.HubBgAsset)
				end
			elseif _G.HubBgGifUrl and _G.HubBgAsset and not tbl11.conn and not tbl11.loading then
				task.spawn(function()
					textLabel4.Text = "  Loading GIF frames..."
					local ok, result, result2 = pcall(tbl11.start, _G.HubBgGifUrl)

					elseif result then
						tbl11.status("animating " .. tostring(result2) .. " frames")
					elseif result2 ~= "cancelled" then
						tbl11.status("failed - " .. tostring(result2))
					end
				end)
			end
		end)

		task.spawn(function()
			local themeImageUrl = tbl.ThemeImageUrl
			if type(themeImageUrl) ~= "string" or themeImageUrl == "" then
				return
			end
			task.wait(6)
			local v26 = fn56(themeImageUrl)
			if not v26 then
				return
			end
			_G.HubBgAsset = v26
			pcall(applyHubBackground, v26)
			task.wait(4)
			pcall(applyHubBackground, v26)

			if _G.HubBgGifUrl and tbl11.enabled() and not tbl11.conn and not tbl11.loading then
				local ok, result, result2 = pcall(tbl11.start, _G.HubBgGifUrl)

				if not ok then
					tbl11.status("error: " .. tostring(result))
				elseif result then
					tbl11.status("animating " .. tostring(result2) .. " frames")
				elseif result2 ~= "cancelled" then
					tbl11.status("failed - " .. tostring(result2))
				end
			end
		end)

		createFrame(v13, "Config")
		local textLabel5 = nil

			if textLabel5 then

		local str3 = "rymogspriv_export.json"
		local frame8 = Instance.new("Frame", v13)

		frame9.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)

		local function createTextButton2(text, layoutOrder, arg)
			local textButton2 = Instance.new("TextButton", frame9)
			textButton2.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -3, 1, 0)
			textButton2.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder

			textButton2.Text = text
			Instance.new("UICorner", textButton2).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
			local stroke3 = tbl5.STROKE
			Instance.new("UIStroke", textButton2).Color = stroke3

			textButton2.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				textButton2.BackgroundColor3 = tbl5.ACCENT
			end)

			textButton2.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				textButton2.BackgroundColor3 = tbl5.ROW
			end)

				pcall(arg, textButton2)
			end)

			return textButton2
		end

		createTextButton2("EXPORT", 1, function()
			local ok, text = pcall(function()
				return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(tbl)
			end)

			if not ok or type(text) ~= "string" then
				fn62("Export failed: could not encode config")
				return
			end
			textBox3.Text = text
			local flag5 = false

			if writefile then
				flag5 = pcall(writefile, "rymogspriv_export.json", text)
			end

			local writeClipboard = setclipboard or toclipboard or syn and syn.write_clipboard
			local flag6 = false

			if type(writeClipboard) == "function" then
				flag6 = pcall(writeClipboard, text)
			end

			local tbl12 = {}

			if flag5 then
				tbl12[#tbl12 + 1] = "saved to " .. str3
			end

			if flag6 then
				tbl12[#tbl12 + 1] = "copied to clipboard"
			end

			if #tbl12 == 0 then
				tbl12[#tbl12 + 1] = "in the box above (no writefile/clipboard)"
			end

			fn62("Exported " .. #text .. " bytes - " .. table.concat(tbl12, ", "))
		end)

		createTextButton2("IMPORT", 2, function()
			local text = textBox3.Text

			if type(text) ~= "string" then
				text = ""
			end

			local str4 = text:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
			if str4 == "" then
				fn62("Paste config JSON, or a filename, then press IMPORT")
				return
			end

			if str4:sub(1, 1) ~= "{" then
				if not (isfile and readfile and isfile(str4)) then
					fn62("Not JSON, and no such file: " .. str4)
					return
				end
				local ok, result = pcall(readfile, str4)
				if not ok or type(result) ~= "string" then
					fn62("Could not read " .. str4)
					return
				end
				str4 = result
			end

			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(str4)
			end)

			if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" then
				fn62("Import failed: that is not valid config JSON")
				return
			end

			pcall(function()
				if writefile then
					writefile("rymogspriv_backup.json", game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(tbl))
				end
			end)

			local v26, v27, v28 = pairs(result)
			local n20 = 0

			for k, v29 in v26, v27, v28 do
				tbl[k] = v29
				n20 += 1
			end

			tbl.Toggles = tbl.Toggles or {}
			tbl.Sliders = tbl.Sliders or {}
			tbl.Keybinds = tbl.Keybinds or {}
			tbl.Inputs = tbl.Inputs or {}
			tbl.Positions = tbl.Positions or {}
			tbl.Sizes = tbl.Sizes or {}
			tbl.UIHidden = tbl.UIHidden or {}
			pcall(fn2)
			fn62(n20 .. " keys imported (old config kept as rymogspriv_backup.json). Re-inject to apply everywhere.")
		end)

		textLabel5 = Instance.new("TextLabel", v13)
		textLabel5.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
		textLabel5.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textLabel5.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium

		textLabel5.Text = "  Export copies your whole config out. Import merges one back in."
	end

	fn45()

	UserInputService3.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)

_G.RyExtrasPanel = frame

_G.ToggleRyExtras = function()

task.spawn(function()
	_G.HubUIWait(4)

	pcall(function()
		local Players4 = game:GetService("Players")
		local RunService4 = game:GetService("RunService")
		local localPlayer6 = Players4.LocalPlayer

		if _G.InvisStealAngle == nil then
			_G.InvisStealAngle = 225
		end

		if _G.SinkSliderValue == nil then
			_G.SinkSliderValue = 8
		end

		if _G.RyWSValue == nil then
			_G.RyWSValue = 28
		end

		if _G.RyWSEnabled == nil then
			_G.RyWSEnabled = false
		end

		if _G.AutoInvisDuringSteal == nil then
			_G.AutoInvisDuringSteal = false
		end

		if _G.AutoRecoverLagback == nil then
			_G.AutoRecoverLagback = true
		end

		_G.invisibleStealEnabled = false
		local flag5 = false
		local tbl7 = {}
		local n20 = 0
		local n21 = 0
		local flag6 = false
		local v22 = nil
		local v23 = nil
		local v24 = nil
		local v25 = nil
		local fn42 = nil

		local function fn43()
			if v22 and v22.Parent then
				v22:Destroy()
			end

			v22 = nil
			flag6 = false

			if v23 then
				v23:Disconnect()
				v23 = nil
			end
		end

		local function fn44()
			for _, v26 in pairs(tbl7) do
				pcall(function()
					if v26 and v26.Parent then
						v26:Destroy()
					end
				end)
			end

			tbl7 = {}
			fn43()
			n20 = 0
			n21 = 0

			pcall(function()
				if workspace.CurrentCamera then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.CurrentCamera:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Name == "LagbackGhost" then
							child:Destroy()
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end

		local tbl8 = { enabled = false, conn = nil }

		_G.RySetWalkSpeed = function(arg)
			_G.RyWalkSpeedOn = arg and true or false
			tbl8.enabled = _G.RyWalkSpeedOn

			if tbl8.conn then
				tbl8.conn:Disconnect()
				tbl8.conn = nil
			end

			if not _G.RyWalkSpeedOn then
				return
			end

			tbl8.conn = RunService4.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
				if not _G.RyWalkSpeedOn then
					return
				end

				if localPlayer6:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
					return
				end

				if _G.StealHold == true then
					return
				end
				local character = localPlayer6.Character
				local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not humanoid or not character or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
					return
				end
				local moveDirection = humanoid.MoveDirection
				if moveDirection.Magnitude <= 0 then
					return
				end
				local n22 = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.RyWalkSpeed) or 26, 5, 32)
				if n22 <= humanoid.WalkSpeed then
					return
				end
				character.CFrame = character.CFrame + moveDirection * (n22 - humanoid.WalkSpeed) * (deltaTime or 0.016)
			end)
		end

		local function setWalkSpeedEnabled(arg)
			_G.RyWSEnabled = arg and true or false
			_G.RySetWalkSpeed(arg)

			if fn42 then
				fn42()
			end
		end

		_G.setWalkSpeedEnabled = setWalkSpeedEnabled

		local function setWalkSpeedValue(arg)
			local ryWalkSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(arg) or 26, 5, 32)
			_G.RyWalkSpeed = ryWalkSpeed
			_G.RyWSValue = ryWalkSpeed
			return ryWalkSpeed
		end

		_G.setWalkSpeedValue = setWalkSpeedValue
		setWalkSpeedValue(_G.RyWalkSpeed or _G.RyWSValue or 26)

		if not _G._xenFixRig then
			local flag7 = false

			local tbl9 = {
				{ "Root", "HumanoidRootPart", "LowerTorso" },
				{ "Waist", "LowerTorso", "UpperTorso" },
				{ "Neck", "UpperTorso", "Head" },
				{ "LeftShoulder", "UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm" },
				{ "LeftElbow", "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm" },
				{ "LeftWrist", "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand" },
				{ "RightShoulder", "UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm" },
				{ "RightElbow", "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm" },
				{ "RightWrist", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand" },
				{ "LeftHip", "LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg" },
				{ "LeftKnee", "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg" },
				{ "LeftAnkle", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot" },
				{ "RightHip", "LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg" },
				{ "RightKnee", "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg" },
				{ "RightAnkle", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot" },
			}

			local function fn45()
				local character = localPlayer6.Character
				if not character then
					return
				end
				local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if not humanoid or humanoid.RigType ~= Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
					return
				end

				for _, v26 in ipairs(tbl9) do
					local v27 = character:FindFirstChild(v26[2])
					local v28 = character:FindFirstChild(v26[3])

					if v27 and v28 then
						local flag8 = false

						for _, child in ipairs(v28:GetChildren()) do
							if child:IsA("Motor6D") and child.Name == v26[1] then
								flag8 = true
								break
							end
						end

						if not flag8 then
							local v29 = v27:FindFirstChild(v26[1] .. "RigAttachment")
							local v30 = v28:FindFirstChild(v26[1] .. "RigAttachment")

							if v29 and v30 then
								for _, child in ipairs(v28:GetChildren()) do
									if child:IsA("AnimationConstraint") and child.Name == v26[1] then
										pcall(function()
											child.Enabled = false
										end)
									end
								end

								pcall(function()
									local motor6D = Instance.new("Motor6D")
									local v31 = v27
									local v32 = v28
									local cFrame = v29.CFrame
									local cFrame2 = v30.CFrame
									motor6D.Name = v26[1]
									motor6D.Part0 = v31
									motor6D.Part1 = v32
									motor6D.C0 = cFrame
									motor6D.C1 = cFrame2
									motor6D.Parent = v28
								end)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

					return false
				end
				flag7 = true
				local ok = pcall(fn45)
				flag7 = false
				return ok
			end
		end

local function fn42()
	local Players4 = game:GetService("Players")
	local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
	local localPlayer6 = Players4.LocalPlayer
	local clock = os.clock

	local function fireClick(arg)
		if not arg then
			return false
		end

		return (pcall(function()
			if typeof(firesignal) == "function" then
				pcall(firesignal, arg.MouseButton1Click)
				pcall(firesignal, arg.MouseButton1Down)
				pcall(firesignal, arg.Activated)
			else
				local n20 = arg.AbsolutePosition.X + arg.AbsoluteSize.X / 2

			end)

		end

				return
			end
			flag7 = arg

			if arg then
				pcall(function()
					streamingMinRadius = workspace.StreamingMinRadius
					streamingTargetRadius = workspace.StreamingTargetRadius
				end)

				fn61()
			elseif streamingMinRadius then
				pcall(function()
					workspace.StreamingMinRadius = streamingMinRadius
					workspace.StreamingTargetRadius = streamingTargetRadius
				end)

				streamingMinRadius = nil
				streamingTargetRadius = nil
			end
		end

		fn46("Force Load Chunks", s.chunkOn == true, function(arg)
			fn62(arg)
			fn47("chunkOn", arg)
		end)

		fn44("Presets")
		local frame12 = Instance.new("Frame", frame10)
		frame12.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
		frame12.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		local uiListLayout3 = Instance.new("UIListLayout", frame12)
		uiListLayout3.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
		uiListLayout3.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
		uiListLayout3.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

			local textButton2 = Instance.new("TextButton", frame12)

						textButton2.Text = text
					end
				end)
			end)

			return textButton2
		end

		createTextButton("Low", 1, function()

			fn51(false)
			fn52(false)

		local function fn45()
			if #tbl8 == 0 then
				return
			end

			for _, v25 in ipairs(tbl8) do

			end

			attribute = nil
		end

		local function queueGrief(arg, arg2)
			if _G.GriefDetectorEnabled == false then
				return
			end

			if not arg or arg == "" or arg == localPlayer6.Name then
				return
			end

			if type(arg2) ~= "string" or arg2 == "" or arg2:sub(1, 1) == "#" then
				return
			end
			local flag5 = localPlayer6:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
			local flag6 = tick() - n20 < 0.5
			if not flag5 and not flag6 then
				return
			end

			if flag5 and not attribute then
				attribute = localPlayer6:GetAttribute("StealingPet")
			end

			for _, v25 in ipairs(tbl8) do
				if v25.griefer == arg and v25.item == arg2 then
					return
				end
			end

			tbl8[#tbl8 + 1] = { griefer = arg, item = arg2 }
		end

		_G.QueueGrief = queueGrief

		localPlayer6:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
			if localPlayer6:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
				attribute = localPlayer6:GetAttribute("StealingPet")
			else
				n20 = tick()
				task.delay(0.5, fn45)
			end
		end)

		task.spawn(function()
			while true do
				task.wait(tonumber(_G.GriefHolderScan) or 0.35)
				if _G.GriefDetectorEnabled == false then
					continue
				end

				pcall(function()
					for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
						if player ~= localPlayer6 and player.Character then
							for _, v25 in ipairs(tbl9) do
								if player.Character:FindFirstChild(v25) then
									tbl10[v25] = { name = player.Name, t = tick() }
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end)

		local function fn46(arg)
			for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if player ~= localPlayer6 and player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild(arg) then
					tbl10[arg] = { name = player.Name, t = tick() }
					return player.Name
				end
			end

			local v25 = tbl10[arg]
			local flag5

			if v25 then
				local t = v25.t
				flag5 = tick() - t < (tonumber(_G.GriefHolderTTL) or 5)
			else
				flag5 = v25
			end

			if flag5 then
				return v25.name
			end
			return nil
		end

		local function fn47()
			local character = localPlayer6.Character
			return character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		end

		local function fn48(arg, arg2, arg3)
			local flag5 = false

			local function fn49()
				if flag5 then
					return
				end
				flag5 = true
				arg3()
			end

			arg.Touched:Connect(function(hit)
				local character = localPlayer6.Character

				if character and hit:IsDescendantOf(character) then
					fn49()
				end
			end)

			local connection = nil

			connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if flag5 or not arg.Parent then
					if connection then
						connection:Disconnect()
						connection = nil
					end

					return
				end

				local v25 = fn47()

				if v25 and (arg.Position - v25.Position).Magnitude <= arg2 then
					fn49()
				end
			end)
		end

		local function fn49(arg)
			if _G.GriefDetectorEnabled == false then
				return
			end
			local name2 = arg.Name

			if name2:find("SentryBullet") then
				local isBasePart = arg:IsA("BasePart") and arg or arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

				if isBasePart then
					fn48(isBasePart, 5, function()
						queueGrief(fn46("All Seeing Sentry") or "Unknown", "All Seeing Sentry")
					end)
				end

				return
			end

			local match = name2:match("Missle_(.+)")

			if match then
				local isBasePart = arg:IsA("BasePart") and arg or arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

				if isBasePart then
					fn48(isBasePart, 15, function()
						queueGrief(match, "Heatseeker")
					end)
				end

				return
			end

			local match2 = name2:match("^PlayerName_(.+)_Doge$")

			if match2 then
				local isBasePart = arg:IsA("BasePart") and arg or arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

				if isBasePart then
					fn48(isBasePart, 8, function()
						queueGrief(match2, "Attack Doge")
					end)
				end

				return
			end
		end

		for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
			pcall(fn49, child)
		end

		workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			pcall(fn49, child)
		end)

		local n21 = 0

		localPlayer6:GetAttributeChangedSignal("RagdollEndTime"):Connect(function()
			if _G.GriefDetectorEnabled == false then
				return
			end

			if localPlayer6:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
				return
			end

			if tick() - n21 < 1.5 then
				return
			end

			if _ragRemaining() <= 0 then
				return
			end
			n21 = tick()

			for _, v25 in ipairs(tbl9) do
				local v26 = fn46(v25)
				if v26 then
					queueGrief(v26, v25)
					return
				end
			end
		end)

		ScreenGui.Enabled = _G.GriefDetectorEnabled ~= false
		_G.GriefDetectorPanel = Frame

task.spawn(function()
	_G.HubUIWait(6)

	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		task.spawn(function()
			while true do
				task.wait(0.5)

				if _G.LineToBest then
					pcall(function()
						local v22, v23, v24 = ipairs(scanAllPets() or {})
						local v25 = nil
						local v26 = nil

						for _, v27 in v22, v23, v24 do
							if v27.position and _petEligible(v27) then
								if v27._pri and (not v25 or v27._pri < v25._pri) then
									v25 = v27
								end

								local flag5 = not v26
								local flag6

								if flag5 then
									flag6 = flag5
								else
									flag6 = (v27.mps or 0) > (v26.mps or 0)
								end

								if flag6 then
									v26 = v27
								end
							end
						end

						_G.RyBestTarget = v25 or v26
					end)
				else
					_G.RyBestTarget = nil
				end
			end
		end)

		local tbl7 = {}

		local function fn43()
			for _, v22 in ipairs({ tbl7.beam, tbl7.a0, tbl7.a1, tbl7.anchor }) do
				if v22 then
					pcall(function()
						v22:Destroy()
					end)
				end
			end

			local v22 = tbl7
			local v23 = tbl7
			local v24 = tbl7
			tbl7.beam = nil
			v22.a0 = nil
			v23.a1 = nil
			v24.anchor = nil
		end

		RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			local ryBestTarget = _G.RyBestTarget
			local flag5 = not _G.LineToBest

			if not flag5 then
				flag5 = not (ryBestTarget and ryBestTarget.position)
			end

			if flag5 then
				if tbl7.beam then
					fn43()
				end

				return
			end

			local character = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
			character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not character then
				return
			end

			if not (tbl7.anchor and tbl7.anchor.Parent) then
				tbl7.anchor = Instance.new("Part")
				tbl7.anchor.Anchored = true
				tbl7.anchor.CanCollide = false
				tbl7.anchor.CanQuery = false
				tbl7.anchor.CanTouch = false
				tbl7.anchor.Transparency = 1
				tbl7.anchor.Size = Vector3.one
				tbl7.anchor.Parent = workspace
			end

			if (tbl7.anchor.Position - ryBestTarget.position).Magnitude > 0.1 then
				tbl7.anchor.Position = ryBestTarget.position
			end

			if not (tbl7.a0 and tbl7.a0.Parent == character) then
				if tbl7.a0 then
					pcall(function()
						tbl7.a0:Destroy()
					end)
				end

				tbl7.a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
				tbl7.a0.Parent = character
			end

			if not (tbl7.a1 and tbl7.a1.Parent == tbl7.anchor) then
				tbl7.a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
				tbl7.a1.Position = Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
				tbl7.a1.Parent = tbl7.anchor
			end

			if not (tbl7.beam and tbl7.beam.Parent) then
				tbl7.beam = Instance.new("Beam")
				tbl7.beam.FaceCamera = true
				tbl7.beam.LightEmission = 1
				tbl7.beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)
				tbl7.beam.Width0 = 0.45
				tbl7.beam.Width1 = 0.45
				tbl7.beam.Parent = character
			end

			tbl7.beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(tbl2.GoldBright)
			tbl7.beam.Attachment0 = tbl7.a0
			tbl7.beam.Attachment1 = tbl7.a1
		end)

		_G.setLineToBest = function(arg)
			_G.LineToBest = arg and true or false

			if not arg then
				fn43()
			end
		end
	end)

	if not ok then
		fn12("[line to best] failed: " .. tostring(result))
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
		local Players4 = game:GetService("Players")

		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return game:GetService("ExperienceService")
		end)

		if not ok then
			result = nil
		end

		local ryJoinQueue = { tries = 5, retry = 3, gap = 3 }
		_G.RyJoinQueue = ryJoinQueue
		local ryJoinQueueEnabled = false
		local flag5 = false
		local flag6 = false
		local n20 = 0
		local v22 = nil
		local n21 = 0
		local v23 = nil
		local v24 = nil

		local function fn43(arg, arg2)
			_G.RyJoinQueueLast = tostring(arg)

			if arg2 then
				fn12("[JoinQueue] " .. tostring(arg))
			else
				fn11("[JoinQueue] " .. tostring(arg))
			end

			if _G.RyRefreshJoinQueueBtn then
				pcall(_G.RyRefreshJoinQueueBtn)
			end
		end

		local function fn44(arg)
			return (tostring(arg or ""):gsub("[%s\"'{}%[%]]", ""))
		end

		local function fn45(arg, arg2)
			flag6 = true

			local ok2, result2 = pcall(function()
				if v24 then
					v24(TeleportService, arg, arg2, Players4.LocalPlayer)
				else
					TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(arg, arg2, Players4.LocalPlayer)
				end
			end)

			flag6 = false
			return ok2, result2
		end

		local function fn46(arg, arg2)
			if result then
				return pcall(function()
					return result:LaunchExperience({ placeId = arg, gameInstanceId = arg2 })
				end)
			end
			return fn45(arg, arg2)
		end

		local function fn47(arg, arg2)
			local num = tonumber(arg)
			local v25 = fn44(arg2)

			if not num or num == 0 then
				num = game.PlaceId
			end

			if v25 == "" then
				fn43("no job id given", true)
				return false
			end

			if v25 == v22 then
				return true
			end
			v22 = v25
			n20 += 1
			local v26 = n20

			task.spawn(function()
				for i = 1, ryJoinQueue.tries do
					if n20 ~= v26 then
						return
					end
					local n22 = ryJoinQueue.gap - os.clock() - n21

					if n22 > 0 then
						task.wait(n22)
					end

					if n20 ~= v26 then
						return
					end
					n21 = os.clock()
					fn43(string.format("joining... (%d/%d)", i, ryJoinQueue.tries), false)
					local v27, v28 = fn46(num, v25)
					if v27 then
						fn43("queued / joining", false)
						return
					end
					fn43(tostring(v28), true)
					task.wait(ryJoinQueue.retry)
				end

				if n20 ~= v26 then
					return
				end
				fn43("gave up, waiting for the next server", true)
				v22 = nil
			end)

			return true
		end

		TeleportService.TeleportInitFailed:Connect(function(arg, arg2, arg3)
			if not ryJoinQueueEnabled then
				return
			end
			fn43(tostring(arg2) .. ": " .. tostring(arg3), true)
		end)

		local function fn48()
			if flag5 then
				return
			end
			flag5 = true

			if hookfunction and newcclosure then
				pcall(function()
					v24 = hookfunction(TeleportService.TeleportToPlaceInstance, newcclosure(function(arg, arg2, arg3, ...)
						if ryJoinQueueEnabled and not flag6 then
							fn47(arg2, arg3)
							return
						end
						local v25 = v24
						local v26 = table.pack(...)
						v26.n = 4 + v26.n - 1
						table.move(v26, 1, v26.n, 4, v26)
						v26[1] = arg
						v26[2] = arg2
						v26[3] = arg3
						return v25(table.unpack(v26, 1, v26.n))
					end))
				end)
			end

			if hookmetamethod and getnamecallmethod and newcclosure then
				pcall(function()
					v23 = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", newcclosure(function(arg, ...)
						local v25 = table.pack(...)

						if ryJoinQueueEnabled and not flag6 and arg == TeleportService then
							local v26 = getnamecallmethod()

							if v26 == "TeleportToPlaceInstance" then
								local value = select(2, ...)
								fn47(..., value)
								return
							end

							if v26 == "TeleportAsync" then
								local v27 = ...
								local value = select(3, ...)
								value = value and value.ServerInstanceId
								if value and value ~= "" then
									fn47(v27, value)
									return
								end
								return v23(arg, table.unpack(v25, 1, v25.n))
							end
						end

						return v23(arg, ...)
					end))
				end)
			end
		end

		local function rySetJoinQueue(arg)
			ryJoinQueueEnabled = arg and true or false
			_G.RyJoinQueueEnabled = ryJoinQueueEnabled

			if ryJoinQueueEnabled then
				fn48()
				fn43("on - specific-server joins are queued", false)
			else
				n20 += 1
				v22 = nil
				_G.RyJoinQueueLast = nil

				if _G.RyRefreshJoinQueueBtn then
					pcall(_G.RyRefreshJoinQueueBtn)
				end
			end
		end

		_G.RySetJoinQueue = rySetJoinQueue

		_G.RyToggleJoinQueue = function()
			rySetJoinQueue(not ryJoinQueueEnabled)
			return ryJoinQueueEnabled
		end

		_G.RyJoinQueueEnabled = false
	end)

	if not ok then
		fn12("[JoinQueue] block failed: " .. tostring(result))
	end
end)
